INFS7903 · CH01

关系模型与规范化Relational modelling and normalisation

学习目标与课程全景Learning objectives and the course as a whole

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week01-Intro.pdf · PDF p. 15-37（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

本课程从数据库“内部如何工作”出发：SQL 说出需要哪些事实，关系代数组织查询，索引与文件结构定位记录，缓冲管理完成页传输，并发控制协调共享数据，恢复保证失败后仍可恢复。学完本章，你应能区分模式与实例、从函数依赖求候选键、判断范式，并解释分解的得失。

The course examines how a database works internally: SQL describes required facts, relational algebra organises queries, indexes and files locate records, buffer management transfers pages, concurrency control coordinates shared data, and recovery handles failures. After this chapter, you should distinguish schemas from instances, derive candidate keys from functional dependencies, identify normal forms and explain decomposition trade-offs.

前置知识补充：集合（set）不计顺序也不含重复；元组（tuple）是一组按属性定义的值。关系（relation）是同一模式下元组的集合。关系模式（relational schema）说明属性名、域和约束，关系实例（relation instance）是某时刻实际存储的元组。SQL 表默认可能含重复行，所以“关系”和实际 SQL 表不能在所有场合完全等同。

Prerequisite supplement: a set has neither ordering nor duplicate elements. A tuple is a collection of values defined over attributes. A relation is a set of tuples sharing a schema. A relational schema specifies attribute names, domains and constraints; a relation instance is the actual tuples at a particular time. SQL tables can contain duplicate rows by default, so relations and SQL tables are not interchangeable in every context.

元数据（metadata）描述数据，如列类型、主键、视图定义、权限和统计量；系统目录（system catalogue）保存这些描述。数据则是员工、工资等业务值。两者都最终以二进制存储，但用途不同：优化器查统计量选计划，执行器从数据页取实际记录。

Metadata describes data, including column types, keys, view definitions, privileges and statistics; the system catalogue stores these descriptions. Data consists of business values such as employees and salaries. Both ultimately use binary storage, but serve different purposes: the optimiser consults statistics to choose plans, while the executor fetches actual records from data pages.

函数依赖是对所有合法状态的约束Functional dependencies constrain every legal state

函数依赖（functional dependency，FD）X→Y 表示：对关系中任意两行 t₁、t₂，只要它们在 X 上相等，就必须在 Y 上相等。X、Y 都可以是一组属性。它说明“相同 X 不能对应不同 Y”，不是因果关系，也不是说 Y 在概念上不能离开 X 存在。一次样本中没有反例，仅说明该实例满足依赖，不能证明业务永远如此。

A functional dependency X→Y means that, for any two tuples t₁ and t₂, equality on X requires equality on Y. X and Y can each be sets of attributes. It means the same X cannot correspond to different Y values; it does not express causation or conceptual nonexistence of Y without X. A sample with no counterexample satisfies the dependency, but does not prove that the business rule always holds.

X → Y ⇔ ∀t₁,t₂ ∈ r(R),　t₁[X]=t₂[X] ⇒ t₁[Y]=t₂[Y]

例如 Emp(EID,Dept,DeptPhone) 中，EID→Dept，Dept→DeptPhone。员工号决定部门，部门决定统一联系电话，所以 EID→DeptPhone。若同一部门可以有多个独立电话且没有选定唯一电话，则 Dept→DeptPhone 不能成立。外键则表达包含关系：子表引用值必须出现在父表键中；不能把任意 FD 直接说成外键。

For Emp (EID, Dept, Dept Phone), suppose EID→Dept and Dept→Dept Phone. The employee identifies a department, which identifies its shared phone, so EID→Dept Phone follows. If a department has several independent phones without a designated unique one, Dept→Dept Phone does not hold. A foreign key instead expresses inclusion: referenced child values must occur in a parent key. An arbitrary FD is not automatically a foreign key.

前置知识补充：Armstrong 公理为自反性（Y⊆X 则 X→Y）、增广性（X→Y 则 XZ→YZ）、传递性（X→Y 且 Y→Z 则 X→Z）。由此可推出合并和分解规则。它们让我们从业务已知依赖推导隐含依赖，避免只盯着题目原样列出的箭头。

Prerequisite supplement: Armstrong’s axioms are reflexivity (Y⊆X implies X→Y), augmentation (X→Y implies XZ→YZ) and transitivity (X→Y and Y→Z imply X→Z). Union and decomposition rules follow. These rules derive implied dependencies from known business rules, so analysis must consider more than the arrows explicitly listed in a question.

属性闭包、超键与候选键Attribute closure, super keys and candidate keys

属性闭包（attribute closure）X⁺ 是在依赖集 F 下由 X 能确定的所有属性。算法从 X 开始，反复找左部已包含在当前集合中的依赖，把其右部加入，直到不能再扩张。属性只会增加且总数有限，所以必定停止；每次加入都有一条已满足的 FD 作依据，因此不会凭空推出属性。

The attribute closure X⁺ contains all attributes determined by X under F. Start with X, repeatedly find dependencies whose left side is already included, add their right side, and stop when nothing changes. Attributes only accumulate within a finite set, so the algorithm terminates. Every addition follows a satisfied FD, so no attribute is inferred without justification.

超键（superkey）能确定全部属性；候选键（candidate key）还要求最小，即去掉任何属性都不再是超键。主键（primary key）是选定的一个候选键。主属性（prime attribute）出现在至少一个候选键中。最小不是“长度最短”：两个不同大小的集合都可能各自无法再删，因此都是候选键。

A super key determines every attribute. A candidate key is additionally minimal: removing any attribute makes it cease to be a super key. A primary key is a selected candidate key. A prime attribute belongs to at least one candidate key. Minimal does not mean globally shortest: sets of different sizes can each be irreducible and therefore both be candidate keys.

Closure(X,F):
  result := X
  repeat
    old := result
    for each U -> V in F:
      if U is a subset of result: result := result union V
  until result = old
  return result

Tutorial 1 Ex1(i)：R(ABCD)，F={C→D, BC→A}。BC⁺：起点{B,C}，用C→D得{B,C,D}，再用BC→A得全部；B⁺只有B，C⁺只有CD，因此BC最小。B、C从未出现在任何非平凡依赖右部，无法由别的属性推出，所有键都必须包含BC，所以候选键恰好只有BC。

For Tutorial 1 Ex 1(i), R(ABCD) and F={C→D, BC→A}. BC⁺ begins as {B, C}, becomes {B, C, D} through C→D, then all attributes through BC→A. B⁺ is only B and C⁺ only CD, so BC is minimal. Neither B nor C appears on a nontrivial right side, so no other attributes can derive them; every key must include BC, making BC the only candidate key.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-3（原资料留在本地版） Ex1(i) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

范式如何消除更新异常How normal forms reduce update anomalies

若部门电话重复写在每个员工行中，修改电话要更新多行；漏改一行产生修改异常。没有员工时无法单独录入部门是插入异常；删除最后一名员工顺带丢掉部门电话是删除异常。规范化（normalisation）把不同事实拆成适当关系，减少这些不必要的重复。仅声明 PK/FK 不会自动完成规范化，必须依据 FD 检查属性组合。

Repeating a department phone in every employee row means changing it requires many updates; a missed row causes a modification anomaly. Being unable to add a department without an employee is an insertion anomaly. Losing its phone when deleting the last employee is a deletion anomaly. Normalisation separates facts into appropriate relations to reduce unnecessary repetition. Declaring PK s and FKs alone does not normalise a design; attribute combinations must be examined using FDs.

范式 / Form准确检查 / Precise check例子与理由 / Example and reason
1NF

每个属性值在课程模型中是原子值。

Attribute values are atomic in the course model.

一格存一个电话，而非未建模的电话列表。

One phone value per cell rather than an un modelled list.

2NF

1NF，且每个非主属性完全依赖每个候选键。

1 NF, with every non prime attribute fully dependent on every candidate key.

BC为键而C→D，D非主：部分依赖，违反2NF。

BC is a key and C→D with non prime D: a partial dependency violates 2 NF.

3NF

每个非平凡X→A，X为超键或A为主属性。

For every nontrivial X→A, X is a super key or A is prime.

允许右部主属性的例外，不能只背“没有传递依赖”。

Allows a prime right-side exception; “no transitive dependency” alone is insufficient.

BCNF

每个非平凡X→Y，X必须为超键。

Every nontrivial determinant X must be a super key.

更严格，但分解可能失去依赖保持。

Stricter, but decomposition may lose dependency preservation.

Ex1(i) 的 C→D 是键BC的一部分决定非主属性D，所以最高仅1NF；分为ABC和CD。交集为C，且C→CD，连接不会生成伪元组，因此无损。两条依赖分别在一张表内检查，因此依赖也保持。例(ii) BC→ABD 的决定项BC本身为键，关系已是BCNF。

In Ex 1(i), C→D makes part of key BC determine non prime D, so the highest form is only 1 NF. Decompose into ABC and CD. Their intersection is C and C→CD, so the join introduces no spurious tuples and is lossless. Each original FD can be checked within one component, so dependencies are preserved too. In part (ii), BC→ABD has key BC as its determinant, so the relation is already in BCNF.

Ex1(iii) 的依赖是 BC→A、CD→AB、A→D。属性C无法由其他属性生成，键必须含C；依次检查BC、CD、AC都可推出全部，且删任一属性均不行。因此键为BC、CD、AC，A、B、C、D全部是主属性。A→D 中A不是超键，违反BCNF，但D是主属性，仍满足3NF。这正是必须用正式3NF定义的原因。

In part (iii), F={BC→A, CD→AB, A→D}. C cannot be derived from other attributes, so every key includes C. BC, CD and AC each derive all attributes and are minimal, giving those three candidate keys. All attributes A, B, C, D are prime. A→D violates BCNF because A is not a super key, but D is prime, so 3 NF still holds. This is why the formal 3 NF definition matters.

课程给出BCNF分解ABC、AD：交集A且A→AD，因此无损。然而CD→AB不能只靠这两张表的局部约束完整检查；C、D的组合无法跨表推出A。这说明“无损连接”和“依赖保持”是不同性质。无损保证拆开再连接能恢复原事实；依赖保持保证不用连接就能检查原依赖。选择3NF还是BCNF要说明取舍。

The course BCNF decomposition is ABC and AD. Their intersection A determines AD, so it is lossless. However, CD→AB cannot be fully enforced through only the local constraints of those two tables: C and D together cannot derive A across them. Lossless join and dependency preservation are separate properties. Lossless ness reconstructs the original facts, while dependency preservation permits checking original FDs without joining. Explain the trade-off when choosing 3 NF versus BCNF.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 3（原资料留在本地版） Ex1(iii); dependency-preservation analysis is added explanation · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

总结、常见错误与自测Summary, mistakes and self-check

先确认业务 FD，再算闭包和全部候选键，最后判断范式与分解性质。常见错误是从现有样本猜出永恒依赖、把所有决定项称为键、只检查被选作主键的候选键、认为BCNF分解一定保持全部依赖。规范化会增加连接需求，第3章视图可隐藏复杂性，第8–9章解释如何减少连接成本。

Establish business FDs first, compute closures and all candidate keys, then assess normal forms and decomposition properties. Common mistakes are inferring permanent rules from a sample, calling every determinant a key, checking only the chosen primary key and assuming BCNF always preserves dependencies. Normalisation can increase joins; views in Chapter 3 hide complexity and Chapters 8–9 address their cost.

C1-S1

自编练习：R(A,B,C)，A→B，B→C。求键与最高范式。

For R(A, B, C) with A→B and B→C, identify the key and highest normal form.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

A⁺=ABC，B⁺=BC，C⁺=C，唯一候选键A。单属性键不存在对键真子集的非主属性部分依赖，所以2NF；B→C中B非超键、C非主属性，违反3NF。可分为AB与BC，交集B决定BC，无损且依赖保持。

A⁺=ABC, B⁺=BC and C⁺=C, so A is the only candidate key. A single-attribute key has no non prime partial dependency on a proper subset, giving 2 NF. B→C violates 3 NF because B is not a super key and C is non prime. AB and BC form a lossless, dependency-preserving decomposition because their intersection B determines BC.

C1-S2

自编练习：样本中姓名都不同，是否证明 Name→Salary 是业务依赖？

Does a sample in which all names differ establish Name→Salary?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

不能。样本确实满足该FD，但未来合法状态可能含同名不同薪员工。需要领域规则、唯一标识要求或明确约束才能把它作为所有合法状态的依赖。

No. The sample satisfies the FD, but a future legal instance might contain different employees with the same name and different salaries. A domain rule, unique-identification requirement or explicit constraint is needed to treat it as an FD over every legal state.

INFS7903 · CH02

SQL 查询思维与基础操作SQL reasoning and basic operations

学习目标与前置知识Learning objectives and prerequisites

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week01-Intro.pdf · PDF p. 38-46（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是把英语需求转换成可检查的 SQL：明确输出单位、关系路径、筛选层级、重复与 NULL 的含义。你应能独立完成 Prac1 的建表与数据操作，写相关子查询、聚合与“全部”条件。前置知识是键、外键与基本布尔逻辑。

The goal is to translate English requirements into verifiable SQL: identify the output unit, relationship paths, filtering level, duplicates and NULL semantics. You should complete Prac 1’s table and data operations independently, and write correlated sub queries, aggregation and universal conditions. Prerequisites are keys, foreign keys and basic Boolean logic.

先确定结果的一行代表什么Decide what one output row represents

解题第一步先写一句话：结果每行代表员工、部门，还是员工—项目组合？然后找标识它的键。连接子表常把一名员工扩成多行，如果最终只要员工名单，需用 EXISTS 或恰当 DISTINCT；不能看到重复就随手 DISTINCT，因为重复可能反映错误连接。

Begin with a sentence: does each output row represent an employee, a department or an employee–project pair? Identify its key. Joining a child table often expands one employee into several rows. For an employee list, use EXISTS or appropriate DISTINCT. Do not add DISTINCT reflexively: duplicates can reveal an incorrect join.

SQL 的概念求值顺序是 FROM/JOIN → WHERE → GROUP BY → HAVING → SELECT → DISTINCT → ORDER BY。它帮助理解语义，并不规定实际物理执行顺序。WHERE 在分组前筛行，HAVING 在分组后筛组；SELECT 列表决定输出属性，ORDER BY 才保证输出顺序。

SQL’s conceptual evaluation order is FROM/JOIN → WHERE → GROUP BY → HAVING → SELECT → DISTINCT → ORDER BY. This explains semantics without prescribing the physical execution order. WHERE filters rows before grouping; HAVING filters groups afterwards. The SELECT list defines output attributes, and ORDER BY establishes output order.

前置知识补充：NULL 表示缺失或未知，不是0也不是空字符串。x=NULL 得 UNKNOWN，必须用 IS NULL。WHERE 只保留TRUE，因此FALSE和UNKNOWN都被排除。COUNT(*)数行，COUNT(x)不数NULL，AVG(x)也忽略NULL；没有输入行时 SUM 通常为NULL，可以用COALESCE明确所需的零。

Prerequisite supplement: NULL represents missing or unknown information, rather than zero or an empty string. x=NULL evaluates to UNKNOWN; use IS NULL. WHERE retains only TRUE, excluding both FALSE and UNKNOWN. COUNT(*) counts rows, while COUNT(x) and AVG(x) ignore NULL. SUM over no rows normally returns NULL; use COALESCE when zero is required.

相关子查询与量词Correlated sub queries and quantifiers

非相关子查询不引用外层行，可以独立解释；相关子查询（correlated subquery）把外层当前行作为参数。例如查工资高于本部门平均工资的员工，内层按当前员工的Dno筛出同部门，再求AVG。课程称此类题“必须相关”，但等价的聚合连接或窗口方法也可表达；相关写法是清晰的一个选择。

An uncorrelated sub query does not refer to an outer row and can be understood independently. A correlated sub query uses the current outer row as a parameter. To find employees above their department average, filter the inner relation by that employee’s Dno before computing AVG. The lecture calls correlation necessary, but equivalent grouped joins or window methods can express the query too; correlation is one clear formulation.

SELECT e.SSN, e.FNAME, e.Dno
FROM EMPLOYEE e
WHERE e.Salary > (
  SELECT AVG(x.Salary) FROM EMPLOYEE x WHERE x.Dno=e.Dno
);

EXISTS 表示“至少一个匹配”，只关心是否有行；NOT EXISTS 表示“不存在反例”。NOT IN 遇到子查询中的NULL可能导致UNKNOWN，例如5 NOT IN (2,NULL)不能认定为真。以键匹配的 NOT EXISTS 通常更便于推理。先写存在什么，再决定是否否定，避免在英语的never、any、all之间混淆。

EXISTS means at least one matching row and only tests existence; NOT EXISTS means no counterexample exists. A NULL in a NOT IN sub query can produce UNKNOWN: 5 NOT IN (2, NULL) is not TRUE. A key-correlated NOT EXISTS is often easier to reason about. State what must exist before choosing negation, especially for English words such as never, any and all.

“做了所有项目”可转成“没有一个项目是他没做的”。对每个候选员工，外层寻找一个缺失项目；内层检查该员工与该项目的匹配。若找不到缺失项目就保留员工。项目集合为空时，所有员工都满足该条件，这是空集上的全称命题（vacuous truth）。

“Works on every project” becomes “there is no project that the employee does not work on.” For each candidate employee, search for a missing project; the inner query tests that employee–project pair. Keep employees with no missing project. If the project set is empty, every employee qualifies, illustrating vacuous truth for universal quantification.

SELECT e.*
FROM EMPLOYEE e
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM PROJECT p
  WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM WORKS_ON w
    WHERE w.ESSN=e.SSN AND w.PNO=p.PNUMBER
  )
);

用数量代替集合包含关系必须非常小心。“会的语言和Eve一样多”不代表“至少会Eve的所有语言”。正确计数法要先限制到目标集合、按不同键计数，并处理空目标集合。例如Eve会英语和法语，另一个人会日语和德语，两个COUNT都为2但完全不满足包含。

Replacing containment with a count requires care. “Speaks as many languages as Eve” does not imply “speaks all Eve’s languages.” A valid count method must restrict to the target set, count distinct keys and handle an empty target. If Eve speaks English and French while another person speaks Japanese and German, both counts are two but containment fails.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week01-Intro.pdf · PDF p. 44-46（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

完整例子：部门人数与高薪人数Worked example: department size and high earners

需求是“部门总人数超过5，列出其中工资大于40,000的人数”。一个6人部门只有1名高薪员工仍应出现；先WHERE salary>40000再HAVING COUNT(*)>5会把它删掉。先按全部员工分组，HAVING检验总人数，条件计数仅用于输出高薪人数即可，连0也能输出。

The requirement is to report the number earning above 40,000 in departments whose total head count exceeds five. A six-person department with one high earner must qualify. Filtering salary first and then applying HAVING COUNT(*)>5 incorrectly removes it. Group all employees, test total head count in HAVING and use conditional counting for the reported high-earner count, including zero.

SELECT Dno,
       COUNT(*) AS total_employees,
       COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE Salary > 40000) AS high_earners
FROM EMPLOYEE
GROUP BY Dno
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5;

课程 Week01 p43 的子查询方法能选对部门，但外层WHERE删掉所有非高薪员工，所以没有高薪员工的合格部门不会输出一行0；页中“可能显示0”的说法与SQL不符。上面的条件聚合是为补齐这个边界而增加的教学解法。跨系统可用 SUM(CASE WHEN Salary>40000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)。

The sub query method on Week 01 p43 selects eligible departments, but its outer WHERE removes all non-high-earners, so an eligible department with no high earner does not produce a zero row. The slide’s suggestion otherwise conflicts with its SQL. The conditional aggregate above is an added teaching solution for that boundary. Across systems, use SUM(CASE WHEN Salary>40000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END).

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week01-Intro.pdf · PDF p. 42-43（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Practical 1：环境、表结构与数据操作Practical 1: environment, schema and data operations

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac1-v2.pdf · PDF p. 3-11（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

课程环境是UQZone，入口可使用浏览器VSCode、WeTTY或pgAdmin。ZONEID是区域标识，SNUM是学生账号，必须用实际值替换。终端运行psql进入数据库客户端；提示符左侧通常显示当前数据库，不应当成SQL的一部分。psql启动后已经连接某数据库，随后用\c prac1切换；课程p6“还未连接数据库”的解释不准确。未登录课程环境，本次没有验证当前区域或远程入口是否可用。

The course uses UQZone through browser VSCode, WeTTY or pgAdmin. ZONE ID identifies the zone and SNUM the student account; substitute actual values. Run psql from the terminal to enter the database client. Its prompt usually shows the current database and is not part of SQL. psql already has a database connection when it starts; \c prac 1 changes it. The p6 description of being unconnected is inaccurate. No course login was made, so current zone status and remote entry availability were not verified.

准备步骤的目的是隔离实验：在练习数据库创建EMPLOYEE，按课件依次重命名工资列、把日期文本改成DATE、添加部门列、删除COMM、再把表改名EMP。USING表达式说明如何转换已有值；如果已有文本不是合法日期，转换会失败，应先检查数据。下列是根据课程整理的语法，未在PostgreSQL服务器执行。

Preparation isolates the experiment: create EMPLOYEE in a practice database, then rename the salary column, convert date text to DATE, add the department column, remove COMM and rename the table EMP. USING specifies conversion of existing values. Invalid date text causes conversion failure, so inspect existing data first. The following syntax is reconstructed from the course and has not been executed on a PostgreSQL server.

CREATE DATABASE prac1;
\c prac1
CREATE TABLE EMPLOYEE (
  EMPNO INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ENAME VARCHAR(20), JOB VARCHAR(20),
  MGR INTEGER, HIREDATE VARCHAR(20), SALARY NUMERIC(7,2), COMM NUMERIC(7,2)
);
ALTER TABLE EMPLOYEE RENAME COLUMN SALARY TO SAL;
ALTER TABLE EMPLOYEE ALTER COLUMN HIREDATE TYPE DATE USING HIREDATE::DATE;
ALTER TABLE EMPLOYEE ADD DEPTNO INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE EMPLOYEE DROP COLUMN COMM;
ALTER TABLE EMPLOYEE RENAME TO EMP;
\d EMP

插入先列出列名，减少结构变化引发的错位。课程原始五行如下；运行部门10加薪10%后，1001、1003、1004的工资应分别为82,500、60,500、66,000。再把1003的经理改为1002、部门改为30，删除1005，最后应剩4行。这些是课程记录与算术推导，未冒充本次执行结果。

Use explicit column lists for insertion to avoid positional errors after schema changes. The course’s original five rows appear below. Raising department 10 salaries by 10% should produce 82,500, 60,500 and 66,000 for employees 1001,1003,1004. Change 1003’s manager to 1002 and department to 30, then delete 1005; four rows should remain. These are source-recorded and arithmetically derived results, not claimed reruns.

INSERT INTO EMP(EMPNO,ENAME,JOB,MGR,HIREDATE,SAL,DEPTNO) VALUES
(1001,'JOHN CLAY','ACCOUNTANT',NULL,'2010-12-17',75000,10),
(1002,'PAUL HAYES','ENGINEER',NULL,'2006-07-20',85000,30),
(1003,'NIKKI JOLIE','ACCOUNTANT',1001,'2011-02-10',55000,10),
(1004,'JAMES STEPHENS','ACCOUNTANT',1001,'2011-05-10',60000,10),
(1005,'CLARA JONATHAN','ENGINEER',1002,'2011-02-13',60000,30);
SELECT EMPNO,ENAME FROM EMP WHERE DEPTNO=30;
UPDATE EMP SET SAL=SAL*1.1 WHERE DEPTNO=10;
UPDATE EMP SET MGR=1002,DEPTNO=30 WHERE EMPNO=1003;
DELETE FROM EMP WHERE EMPNO=1005;
SELECT * FROM EMP ORDER BY EMPNO;

检查方法是用\d核对模式，用带ORDER BY的SELECT核对行值，再看UPDATE/DELETE报告的行数。常见问题：忘分号会进入续行；漏WHERE会改全表；字符串要用单引号；psql元命令不放进通用SQL接口；文件路径以服务器客户端实际所在目录为准。课程下载sol-0.sql后写成\i sol.sql、prep-4.sql又写成prep.sql，文件名不一致，须执行实际下载的名称。mget是课程环境命令，本地未发现该工具和准备文件。

Verify the schema with \d, row values with an ordered SELECT and affected counts from UPDATE/DELETE. Common issues include missing semicolons causing continuation prompts, omitted WHERE clauses changing every row, incorrect string quotes, using psql meta commands in a generic SQL interface and wrong client-side paths. The materials download sol-0.sql but show \i sol.sql, and similarly mix prep-4.sql with prep.sql; execute the actual downloaded filename. mget belongs to the course environment and was not found locally, nor were those preparation files.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

SQL能力的核心是识别结果粒度和量词，再用键建立正确匹配。概念求值用于证明结果，执行计划用于理解性能。先保证含义正确，再研究第8–9章的优化；一个跑得快但漏掉0或空集合情况的查询仍然不正确。

SQL skill rests on output granularity, quantifiers and key-based matching. Conceptual evaluation proves results; execution plans explain performance. Establish correctness before optimising in Chapters 8–9: a fast query that mishandles zero counts or empty target sets is still incorrect.

C2-S1

自编练习：NOT IN 的子查询返回NULL时会怎样？

What happens to NOT IN when its sub query returns NULL?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

对没有匹配到明确值的候选项，比较可能为UNKNOWN，因此WHERE不保留它。用NOT EXISTS按实际相关键检查不存在匹配，可以避开这个全局NULL陷阱。仍需按业务决定候选键本身NULL是否应被保留。

A candidate not matching any definite value can yield UNKNOWN and be rejected by WHERE. Correlating NOT EXISTS on the intended key avoids this global NULL trap. Business semantics must still decide whether a NULL candidate key should qualify.

C2-S2

自编练习：为什么连接后的COUNT(*)可能多算员工？

Why can COUNT(*) after a join over count employees?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

一名员工可能匹配多条子记录。COUNT(*)数的是连接行而非独立员工；若需要员工数，用正确粒度预聚合、COUNT(DISTINCT eid)或EXISTS。不能在没有理解业务单位前机械去重。

One employee may match several child records. COUNT(*) counts joined rows rather than distinct employees. Pre-aggregate at the correct grain, use COUNT(DISTINCT eid), or use EXISTS when appropriate. Do not remove duplicates without understanding the business unit.

INFS7903 · TUT01

Tutorial 1：函数依赖与 SQLTutorial 1: functional dependencies and SQL

Read with Chapters 1–2. Each part presents its question before a folded answer. References use physical PDF pages. The sheet’s pp 3–4 provide course answers and Tut 1-PPT explains the same tasks; their completion-date conditions for Ex 3(f) differ.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
来源 / Source: tut/Tut1-PPT(1).pdf · PDF p. 4-24（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E1-i

对R(A B C D)，F={C→D, BC→A}：①求全部候选键和最高范式；②必要时分解到3NF或BCNF。

For R(A B C D), F={C→D, BC→A}: (1) state the candidate key (s) and highest normal form; (2) if necessary, normalise into 3 NF relations or BCNF.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

BC⁺=ABCD；B⁺=B，C⁺=CD，所以BC最小。B与C都无法由其他属性推出，故唯一键BC。C→D是对键真子集的非主属性部分依赖，最高1NF。分ABC、CD；交集C决定CD，故无损。两个原依赖各在一张表内，依赖保持；两表实际上均达BCNF。

BC⁺=ABCD, B⁺=B and C⁺=CD, so BC is minimal. B and C cannot be derived from other attributes, giving the only key BC. C→D is a partial dependency of non prime D on a proper key subset, so only 1 NF. Decompose into ABC, CD. Their intersection C determines CD, making the join lossless; both FDs are locally preserved and both components are actually in BCNF.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-3（原资料留在本地版） Ex1(i) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E1-ii

对R(A B C D)，F={BC→ABD}：①求全部候选键和最高范式；②必要时分解到3NF或BCNF。

For R(A B C D), F={BC→ABD}: (1) state the candidate key (s) and highest normal form; (2) if necessary, normalise into 3 NF relations or BCNF.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

BC⁺=ABCD，B与C在非平凡右部缺席，故唯一候选键BC。所有给定非平凡依赖的决定项都是超键，最高BCNF，不需分解。BC→B虽可由题目整体写法分解出来，但它是平凡依赖，无需拿来制造违反范式。

BC⁺=ABCD and neither B nor C can be derived from a nontrivial right side, giving only BC. Every nontrivial determinant is a super key, so the relation is BCNF and needs no decomposition. The B component in BC→ABD is trivial and creates no violation.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-3（原资料留在本地版） Ex1(ii) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E1-iii

对R(A B C D)，F={BC→A, CD→AB, A→D}：①求全部候选键和最高范式；②必要时分解到3NF或BCNF。

For R(A B C D), F={BC→A, CD→AB, A→D}: (1) state the candidate key (s) and highest normal form; (2) if necessary, normalise into 3 NF relations or BCNF.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

C必须在键中。BC⁺由BC→A、A→D得到ABCD；CD⁺由CD→AB得到全部；AC⁺先A→D再CD→AB得到全部，三者最小。所有属性都是主属性，所以A→D虽A非超键，仍满足3NF但不满足BCNF。课程分解ABC、AD无损，因为A→AD；CD→AB不局部保持。务必说明无损与依赖保持不同。

Every key must include C. BC⁺ derives A then D; CD⁺ derives AB; AC⁺ derives D then uses CD→AB. BC, CD, AC are all minimal. Every attribute is prime, so A→D satisfies 3 NF despite A not being a super key, but violates BCNF. The course decomposition ABC, AD is lossless because A→AD, but does not locally preserve CD→AB. Separate lossless ness from dependency preservation.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-3（原资料留在本地版） Ex1(iii) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E2

举一个满足2NF但不满足3NF的关系。

Give an example of a relation in 2 NF but not in 3 NF.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

课程例R(ABCD)，F={A→BCD,B→C}。A为唯一键，单属性键没有非主属性的部分依赖，因此2NF。B不是超键、C非主，B→C违反3NF。分ABD、BC，交集B决定BC，且两条FD可局部检查。常见错是例子连2NF都不满足。

The course example is R(ABCD), F={A→BCD, B→C}. A is the only key; its single attribute has no partial dependency of non prime attributes, so 2 NF holds. B is not a super key and C is non prime, so B→C violates 3 NF. Decompose into ABD and BC; intersection B determines BC and the dependencies are locally check able. Ensure the example really satisfies 2 NF.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-3（原资料留在本地版） Ex2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex3共享情境与模式Ex 3 shared context and schema

在线解谜系统允许学生与教师注册。打开题目就写入SOLVED，记录首次DateOpen；未完成时DateSolved为NULL，完成后填写日期。SOLVED只存已打开或已完成的题。PUZZLE的PName唯一，Difficulty为Simple、Advanced、Hard；Solution仅用于系统验证，不能向普通解题者公开。STUDENT与TEACHER是SOLVERS子类，Type取Student或Teacher，Year记录年级。DateOpen不能为NULL。

An online puzzle-solving system lets students and teachers register. Opening a puzzle creates a SOLVED row with its first DateOpen; DateSolved is NULL until successful completion. SOLVED contains only opened or solved puzzles. PUZZLE.PName is unique and Difficulty is Simple, Advanced or Hard. Solution is for system checking and must not be exposed to ordinary solvers. STUDENT and TEACHER subclass SOLVERS, Type is Student or Teacher, Year records study year, and DateOpen is mandatory.

PUZZLE(PName, Difficulty, Solution) -- PK PName
SOLVERS(RegNo, Password, Name, RegistrationDate, Type) -- PK RegNo
STUDENT(RegNo, Year) -- PK/FK RegNo -> SOLVERS
TEACHER(RegNo, Position) -- PK/FK RegNo -> SOLVERS
SOLVED(RegNo, PName, DateOpen, DateSolved) -- PK(RegNo,PName)
-- FKs RegNo -> SOLVERS; PName -> PUZZLE

中文情境对应原题pp1–2，下面逐题保留英文查询要求。教材使用最少公开列；原题中“details”与“Solution不能公开”有张力，因此展示题名和难度，不输出答案字段。执行完整性依赖原模式的键与外键；本地没有该谜题数据，以下SQL为推导并作局部小数据验证，非课程服务器输出。

The context corresponds to pp 1–2; each English query requirement follows below. This edition exposes minimal public columns. “Details” and the prohibition on revealing Solution create a tension, so answers show puzzle name and difficulty without the solution. Correctness assumes the declared keys and FKs. No puzzle dataset was supplied; the SQL is derived, with small local validation where recorded, rather than course-server output.

T01-E3-a

列出谜题，先按难度等级，再按题名排序。

Display a list of Puzzles, ordered by difficulty level and then by Puzzle Name.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

先明确难度是业务等级还是字母序。课程ORDER BY Difficulty会按文本排列Advanced、Hard、Simple；若题意是Simple→Advanced→Hard，应显式CASE映射1、2、3。后用PName确定同等级顺序。

Decide whether difficulty means semantic rank or alphabetic order. The course ORDER BY Difficulty sorts text as Advanced, Hard, Simple. For Simple→Advanced→Hard, map them to 1,2,3 with CASE, then order by PName within each rank.

SELECT PName,Difficulty FROM PUZZLE
ORDER BY CASE Difficulty WHEN 'Simple' THEN 1 WHEN 'Advanced' THEN 2 WHEN 'Hard' THEN 3 END, PName;
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(a) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E3-b

列出从未被完成过的谜题信息。

List the Puzzle (s) details that have never been solved.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

“从未完成”不等于“从未打开”：存在DateSolved=NULL的记录仍可能合格。寻找不存在非NULL完成日期的匹配。课程用NOT IN在PName非空主键前提下可行，但只输出PName而非所有公开详情；这里用NOT EXISTS保留语义并避免NULL陷阱。

Never solved differs from never opened: rows with NULL DateSolved do not constitute completion. Exclude a puzzle only if a matching non-null completion exists. The course NOT IN works with non-null primary-key PName but returns only names. NOT EXISTS expresses the condition directly and avoids the NULL trap.

SELECT p.PName,p.Difficulty FROM PUZZLE p
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SOLVED s
 WHERE s.PName=p.PName AND s.DateSolved IS NOT NULL);
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(b) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E3-c

找出最早注册的全部解题者姓名及类别。

List the name and corresponding type (Student or Teacher) of the solver (s) who was the first ever to register in this system.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

用MIN取最早日期，再保留相等的所有行，能处理并列。LIMIT 1会漏掉同日/同时间注册的人。课程原SQL缺少MIN右括号，此处修复。

Find the minimum registration date and retain every equal row to handle ties. LIMIT 1 could omit equally early registrants. The course SQL is missing a closing parenthesis for MIN; this version repairs it.

SELECT Name,Type FROM SOLVERS
WHERE RegistrationDate=(SELECT MIN(RegistrationDate) FROM SOLVERS);
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(c) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E3-d

找出学生完成次数多于教师的谜题。

Find the puzzles that have been solved more times by students than by teachers.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

仅计DateSolved非NULL，按PName分组，将学生和教师计数相比较。零教师完成也能处理；无任何学生完成的题不可能满足“学生更多”。主键(RegNo,PName)意味着同人同题只存一次完成，因此是完成者数量而非尝试次数。

Count only non-null completions, group by PName and compare student versus teacher counts. Zero teacher completions are handled; a puzzle with no student completion cannot qualify. The (RegNo, PName) key means one completion row per person and puzzle, so this counts successful solvers rather than attempts.

SELECT s.PName FROM SOLVED s JOIN SOLVERS v USING(RegNo)
WHERE s.DateSolved IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY s.PName
HAVING COUNT(*) FILTER(WHERE v.Type='Student')
     > COUNT(*) FILTER(WHERE v.Type='Teacher');
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(d) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E3-e

找出至少完成一道Hard谜题的一年级学生。

Find the “First Year” student (s) who has solved at least one “Hard” Puzzle.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

先把SOLVERS与STUDENT按RegNo连接，Year按题目文字匹配，再EXISTS验证一条已完成Hard记录。EXISTS避免一个学生完成多题重复输出。保留RegNo作为可靠身份。

Join SOLVERS to STUDENT on RegNo, filter the stated Year text and use EXISTS for a completed Hard puzzle. EXISTS avoids duplicate students from multiple matching puzzles. Keep RegNo as reliable identity.

SELECT v.RegNo,v.Name FROM SOLVERS v JOIN STUDENT t USING(RegNo)
WHERE t.Year='First Year' AND v.Type='Student'
AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SOLVED s JOIN PUZZLE p USING(PName)
 WHERE s.RegNo=v.RegNo AND s.DateSolved IS NOT NULL AND p.Difficulty='Hard');
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(e) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T01-E3-f

找出至少完成Whiz Kid已完成全部谜题的解题者。

Find the solvers who have solved at least all the puzzles solved by the solver with the name “Whiz Kid”.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

外层必须从所有SOLVERS出发。寻找Whiz Kid的任一已完成题，若候选没有已完成该题就是反例；不存在反例才保留。候选的DateSolved也必须非NULL，“打开过”不等于“做完”。若目标集合为空，所有候选符合。Name未声明唯一；此SQL将同名Whiz Kid的已完成题取并集，如题目要求某个唯一人，需补充RegNo。原答案RegoNo/Solver拼写与候选完成条件有问题，配套PPT还遗漏目标完成日期。

Start with every SOLVERS row. Look for a completed Whiz Kid puzzle that the candidate has not completed; retain candidates with no counterexample. Candidate DateSolved must also be non-null: opened is not solved. An empty target set qualifies everyone. Name is not declared unique; this SQL unions completed puzzles of all matching names. Supply a RegNo if one specific person is intended. The reference answer has RegoNo/Solver typos and candidate-completion omissions; the companion PPT also omits the target completion filter.

SELECT v.RegNo,v.Name FROM SOLVERS v
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
 SELECT 1 FROM SOLVED w JOIN SOLVERS target ON target.RegNo=w.RegNo
 WHERE target.Name='Whiz Kid' AND w.DateSolved IS NOT NULL
 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SOLVED mine
  WHERE mine.RegNo=v.RegNo AND mine.PName=w.PName
    AND mine.DateSolved IS NOT NULL)
);
来源 / Source: tut/Tut01-FD-SQL-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3(f) · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

INFS7903 · CH03

视图与物化视图Views and materialised views

学习目标与三层模式Learning objectives and the three-schema architecture

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week02-Views.pdf · PDF p. 3-12（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

本章目标是解释视图提供的逻辑独立性、权限边界和查询简化，手工重写视图查询，判断更新与刷新的区别。前置是第2章SELECT、GROUP BY、NULL与约束。三层模式中，外部模式是用户看到的数据接口，概念模式是整体逻辑结构，内部模式是物理表示；映射让其中一层变化尽量少影响其他层。

This chapter explains logical independence, privilege boundaries and query simplification through views, manual query rewriting, and the distinction between updates and refreshes. Prerequisites are SELECT, GROUP BY, NULL and constraints from Chapter 2. In the three-schema architecture, external schemas are user interfaces, the conceptual schema is the overall logical structure, and the internal schema is physical representation. Mappings reduce the impact of changes across layers.

普通视图（view）是命名查询（named query），保存定义，查询时根据当前可见的基表数据计算。它可以隐藏不需要的列、限定行或封装连接。例如教务处看学生身份与地址，专业管理者只看学号和专业。视图不是自动的安全保证：必须配合基表权限、视图所有者与角色设置，避免用户同时能绕过接口直接读原表。

An ordinary view is a named query whose definition is stored and evaluated against currently visible base data when queried. It can hide columns, restrict rows or encapsulate joins. An administrative role might see identity and addresses, while a programme role sees ID s and programmes. A view is not an automatic security guarantee: base-table privileges, ownership and roles must prevent direct access that bypasses the intended interface.

CREATE VIEW IT_Students AS
SELECT sid,name,age FROM Student WHERE major='IT';
GRANT SELECT ON IT_Students TO programme_reader;

查询重写：保留谓词与分组语义Query rewriting preserves predicates and grouping

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week02-Views.pdf · PDF p. 14-24（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

查询修改（query modification）把视图名替换为定义中的关系与表达式，再把视图条件与用户条件合并。例如从IT_Students选age>19，实际语义是major=IT且age>19。若视图条件是major=IT OR major=MM，必须加括号后再AND age>19；否则运算优先级会让所有IT学生绕过年龄条件。

Query modification replaces a view reference with its defining relations and expressions, combining the view and user predicates. Selecting age>19 from IT_Students means major=IT and age>19. If the view condition is major=IT OR major=MM, parenthesise it before adding AND age>19; otherwise operator precedence lets all IT students bypass the age condition.

SELECT name FROM Student
WHERE (major='IT' OR major='MM') AND age>19;

对聚合视图不能机械地把所有外层WHERE拼进基表WHERE。Majors(major,mtotal)以COUNT(*)按major分组，外层mtotal>100是筛选分组结果，所以重写为HAVING COUNT(*)>100。对基表先筛人再数人与先数人再筛组，会产生不同含义。

For aggregate views, do not mechanically paste every outer WHERE into the base-table WHERE. Majors (major, mtotal) groups by major and uses COUNT(*); filtering mtotal>100 selects group results, so it becomes HAVING COUNT(*)>100. Filtering people before counting and filtering groups after counting have different meanings.

CREATE VIEW Majors(major,mtotal) AS
SELECT major,COUNT(*) FROM Student GROUP BY major;
-- Equivalent form of SELECT * FROM Majors WHERE mtotal>100
SELECT major,COUNT(*) FROM Student GROUP BY major HAVING COUNT(*)>100;

课程学生例子初始IT有Peter与Bill共2人、MM有Bob共1人。把Peter专业改为MM后，重新查询普通视图得IT=1、MM=2；只把Peter年龄改掉不影响按专业计数。原因是输出只依赖major和行存在性。此“依赖分析”也是后面增量维护的基础。

In the course student example, IT initially contains Peter and Bill, while MM contains Bob. Changing Peter’s major to MM makes the ordinary view show IT=1 and MM=2. Changing only Peter’s age does not affect counts by major, because the output depends on major and row existence. This dependency analysis also underlies incremental maintenance.

物化：用存储和维护换取重复计算Materialisation trades storage and maintenance for re computation

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week02-Views.pdf · PDF p. 25-30（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

物化视图（materialised view）实际保存查询结果。普通视图像一张每次重新计算的工作表，物化视图像保存好的汇总快照。后者查询可能更快，但基表更新后可能陈旧。PostgreSQL 的普通物化视图需显式REFRESH，不能把课程对“应当维护”的概念描述误解为该软件默认自动增量刷新。

A materialised view physically stores a query result. An ordinary view resembles a worksheet recalculated on demand; a materialised view resembles a saved summary snapshot. Reading the latter can be faster, but base changes can make it stale. PostgreSQL’s ordinary materialised views need an explicit REFRESH; the lecture’s conceptual requirement to maintain them is not a promise of automatic incremental refresh by that software.

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_dept_sal AS
SELECT deptno,AVG(sal) AS avg_sal FROM emp GROUP BY deptno;
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_dept_sal;

是否物化应比较一个时间窗口内的总成本：查询次数×每次节约，能否抵过刷新、写入维护和空间成本，并满足新鲜度要求。教学补充：若维护平均值，可以存SUM和COUNT，每增加工资s，就令sum增加s、count增加1，avg=sum/count；不能把旧平均值与新工资简单再平均，因为两者权重不同。

Choose materialisation by comparing total cost over a time window: can query frequency multiplied by per-query savings cover refresh, write-maintenance and storage costs while meeting freshness requirements? Teaching supplement: maintain an average using SUM and COUNT. Adding salary s increments sum by s and count by one, then avg=sum/count. Averaging the old average and new salary directly gives incorrect weights.

例如原有工资40与60，平均50；新增100后应为(40+60+100)/3=66.67，而不是(50+100)/2=75。增量维护复杂连接时还须考虑重复匹配、删除和分组消失，完整重算虽然昂贵，却容易定义。

For salaries 40 and 60, the average is 50. Adding 100 gives (40+60+100)/3=66.67, not (50+100)/2=75. Incremental maintenance of complex joins also requires accounting for repeated matches, deletions and disappearing groups. Full re computation can be expensive but has straightforward semantics.

更新视图与刷新视图是不同方向Updating through a view and refreshing a view have different directions

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week02-Views.pdf · PDF p. 31-45（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

视图可更新性（view updateability）讨论用户对视图做INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE时，能否明确映射到基表行。刷新讨论基表改后如何重新得到物化结果。前者是视图到基表，后者是基表到物化视图。聚合值AVG=5通常有无限多组基表值对应，所以默认没有唯一逆操作。连接视图删除一行时，也不清楚应删员工、部门还是关联记录。

View update ability asks whether INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE through a view can map unambiguously to base rows. Refreshing asks how to re compute a materialised result after base changes. The first direction is view to base table; the second is base table to materialised view. An aggregate such as AVG=5 usually has infinitely many underlying value combinations, so no unique inverse update exists. Deleting a joined row can likewise leave ambiguity about deleting an employee, department or association.

课程采用保守规则：单表并保留主键的简单视图可更新；聚合、DISTINCT、GROUP BY、集合运算和多表连接通常不可直接更新。实际PostgreSQL 17 对简单单表视图并不要求在输出中暴露主键。缺少主键列的INSERT仍可能因为基表主键无默认值而失败；这不等于该视图所有UPDATE/DELETE都不允许。考试解释题应说明课程规则，软件操作题再写明具体DBMS规则。

The course uses a conservative rule: simple single-table views retaining the primary key are updateable; aggregation, DISTINCT, GROUP BY, set operations and joins generally are not directly updateable. PostgreSQL 17 does not require a simple single-table view to expose its primary key. An INSERT omitting a mandatory base key may fail without a default, but that does not prohibit every UPDATE or DELETE through the view. State the course convention in conceptual answers and the specific DBMS rule in software tasks.

PostgreSQL 17 · Automatically updatable views

WITH CHECK OPTION 约束的是通过视图新增或修改后的行仍须满足视图谓词。例如只显示gpa≥6的视图，把某行改为5：无该选项时基表可能更新成功、行从视图消失；有该选项时拒绝。它不是用来撤销所有基表更新的全局CHECK，也不让聚合视图自动拥有逆映射。

WITH CHECK OPTION requires rows inserted or updated through a view to satisfy its predicate afterwards. In a view showing gpa≥6, changing a row to five can succeed in the base table and make it disappear from the view without the option; with it, the change is rejected. This is not a global CHECK on all base updates and does not create an inverse mapping for aggregate views.

INSTEAD OF 触发器（第4章）能为连接视图定义具体写入行为。例如界面提交一个姓名，由触发器先确认姓名恰好匹配一人，再把人的键写入关联表。这是开发者显式指定语义，不能声称DBMS从连接定义自动推断出唯一操作。

An INSTEAD OF trigger in Chapter 4 can define explicit write behaviour for a join view. For example, a form submits a name, the trigger confirms exactly one matching person, then inserts that person’s key into an association table. The developer supplies the semantics; the DBMS does not automatically infer a unique action from the join definition.

Practical 4：数据、结果与性能实验Practical 4: data, results and performance experiments

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac4-v2.pdf · PDF p. 3-12（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

准备文件应建立EMP、DEPT及其副本EMP1、DEPT1；课程示例各EMP表50,005行、各DEPT表9行。本地未找到prep-4.sql，不能复现实例中的随机工资或耗时。先建V_EMP_ACC筛deptno=30，插入3035 NICK COLES，再通过视图改名JAMES ADAM。检查基表WHERE empno=3035得到这行，才证明写入传播。部门30在不同Practical数据中名称不同，应按实际DEPT核对。

The preparation file creates EMP, DEPT and copies EMP1, DEPT1; the course example has 50,005 rows per EMP table and nine per DEPT table. prep-4.sql is absent locally, preventing reproduction of random salary values and timings. Create V_EMP_ACC for deptno=30, insert employee 3035 NICK COLES, then rename that row JAMES ADAM through the view. Verify it in the base table with WHERE empno=3035 to establish propagation. Department 30 has different names across practical datasets, so inspect the actual DEPT data.

CREATE VIEW v_emp_acc AS
SELECT empno,ename,job,hiredate,sal,deptno FROM emp WHERE deptno=30;
INSERT INTO v_emp_acc VALUES(3035,'NICK COLES','ENGINEER',DATE '2015-01-01',70000,30);
UPDATE v_emp_acc SET ename='JAMES ADAM' WHERE empno=3035;
SELECT * FROM emp WHERE empno=3035;

随后把视图改为COUNT与AVG，观察它不再默认可更新；再创建按部门工资汇总的普通视图与物化视图，使用\timing和EXPLAIN ANALYZE比较。普通视图计划扫描基表、连接、聚合；物化视图只扫描已存储的9行。课程前面修改EMP而没改EMP1，导致某些平均值不同：性能对比前应确认输入是否一致，不能把输入不同误判成“物化产生错误”。

Next redefine the view using COUNT and AVG and observe that automatic update ability no longer applies. Create ordinary and materialised salary summaries, comparing them with \timing and EXPLAIN ANALYZE. The ordinary plan scans, joins and aggregates base data; the materialised plan scans nine stored rows. Earlier steps change EMP without changing EMP1, explaining differing averages. Confirm equivalent inputs before comparison rather than treating different inputs as incorrect materialisation.

总结、易错点与自测Summary, pitfalls and self-check

区分四件事：视图接口、查询重写、物化维护、通过视图更新。普通视图不会天然预先计算结果；物化视图不会天然实时同步；主键不是每个DBMS的可更新必要条件；一次查询更快不代表包含刷新成本后总成本更低。

Keep four issues separate: the view interface, query rewriting, materialised maintenance and updates through a view. Ordinary views do not inherently pre compute results; materialised views do not inherently synchronise in real time; exposing a primary key is not universally necessary for update ability; and a faster read does not prove lower total cost once refresh is included.

C3-S1

自编练习：视图筛选salary>50000，通过视图把某行改为45000会怎样？

A view shows salary>50000. What can happen if a row is updated to 45000 through the view?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

若该简单视图可更新且没有CHECK OPTION，基表更新后行不再出现在视图；有CHECK OPTION则拒绝。还需满足基表约束与权限。判断不能只说“该行原来在视图中所以一定能改”。

For an updateable simple view without CHECK OPTION, the base update can succeed and the row disappears from the view. With CHECK OPTION, it is rejected. Base constraints and privileges still apply. Original membership in the view alone does not establish that the update is permitted.

C3-S2

自编练习：为什么刷新物化视图不能解决聚合视图的可更新性？

Why does refreshing a materialised view not make an aggregate view updateable?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

刷新从已确定的基表状态算出结果；可更新性要求从一个期望结果反推出应改哪些基表值。前向计算可以唯一，逆向分配却可能不唯一，两个问题的方向不同。

Refreshing computes a result from a known base state. Update ability asks which base values should change to realise a desired view result. The forward calculation can be unique while the inverse allocation is ambiguous; the problems have opposite directions.

INFS7903 · TUT02

Tutorial 2：视图与可更新性Tutorial 2: views and updateability

来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
来源 / Source: tut/Tut2-PPT.pdf · PDF p. 3-42（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T02-E1

简述关系数据库为什么需要视图。

Briefly discuss: why do we need views in relational databases?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

视图用稳定逻辑接口隐藏表结构与连接细节，让同一事实按不同用户需求呈现，并可结合权限隐藏列或限定行。普通视图只保存定义，不必复制数据；物化视图另讨论存储、维护与新鲜度。课程答案仅指向教材，本段为课程概念整合。

Views hide schema and join details behind stable logical interfaces, present the same facts for different users and can restrict rows or columns with privileges. An ordinary view stores its definition without duplicating data; materialised views introduce separate storage, maintenance and freshness decisions. The course answer gives reading references; this is an integrated explanation.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T02-E2

视图创建需要权限且有所有者。简述如何用视图做授权控制。

Whoever creates a view in DBMS must be authorised and the view has ownership. Briefly describe how views can be used for security control such as authority control.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

DBA按角色建立只显示所需数据的视图，GRANT授予相应访问，REVOKE收回权限；同时检查基表访问和所有者权限，防止绕过视图。身份认证回答“是谁”，授权回答“能做什么”；仅创建视图而仍把基表全部授权给用户，不能达成预期隔离。

The DBA creates role-specific views exposing required data, grants suitable access and revokes it when needed. Review base access and ownership to prevent bypass. Authentication establishes identity; authorisation establishes permitted actions. Creating a view while retaining unrestricted base privileges does not achieve the intended restriction.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex3 的 COMPANY 数据与汇总Ex 3 COMPANY data and summary

CREATE VIEW DEPT_SUMMARY(D,C,Total_S,Average_S) AS
SELECT Dno,COUNT(*),SUM(Salary),AVG(Salary)
FROM EMPLOYEE GROUP BY Dno;

T02-E3-A

SELECT * FROM DEPT_SUMMARY的结果是什么？

What is the result of SELECT * FROM DEPT_SUMMARY?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

部门1只有James工资55,000。部门5四人工资30,000+40,000+38,000+25,000=133,000，均值33,250。部门4三人25,000+43,000+25,000=93,000，均值31,000。输出(D,C,Total_S,Average_S)：(1,1,55000,55000)、(5,4,133000,33250)、(4,3,93000,31000)。未写ORDER BY，不保证三组输出顺序。

Department 1 has James at 55,000. Department 5 sums 30,000+40,000+38,000+25,000=133,000 across four employees, averaging 33,250. Department 4 sums 25,000+43,000+25,000=93,000 across three, averaging 31,000. Rows (D, C, Total_S, Average_S) are (1,1,55000,55000),(5,4,133000,33250),(4,3,93000,31000), without guaranteed ordering.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex3A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T02-E3-B

求总工资大于100,000的部门号与人数。

SELECT D, C FROM DEPT_SUMMARY WHERE Total_S>100000;

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

只有部门5的133,000超过阈值，输出(5,4)。比较Total_S而非Average_S或人数。

Only department 5’s 133,000 exceeds the threshold, yielding (5,4). Compare Total_S rather than Average_S or employee count.

为所有员工加薪10%。

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

查IT部门经理姓名和工资。

T02-E4-E

查超过20名员工的部门号、员工数和平均工资。

Find the average salary for each department with more than 20 employees, showing department id, employee count and average salary.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

假设Works主键(eid,did)，每员工部门组合唯一，连接后按did分组，HAVING COUNT(*)>20。pcttime不作为平均权重，因为题目没要求按工时加权。

Assume Works has unique (eid, did). Join, group by did and retain COUNT(*)>20. Do not weight by pcttime unless requested.

SELECT w.did,COUNT(*) AS employee_count,AVG(e.salary) AS average_salary
FROM Works w JOIN Emp e ON e.eid=w.eid
GROUP BY w.did HAVING COUNT(*)>20;
来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex4E · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T02-E4-F

解释Ex4(E)的概念求值过程。

Explain the conceptual evaluation strategy for your statement in Exercise 4(E).

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

配对Emp与Works，按eid保留匹配，保留did与工资及计数所需多重性，按did分组，计算COUNT和AVG，筛人数>20的组。概念过程不要求物理上真的做乘积或排序；中途不能按集合去重工资，否则同工资员工被少算。

Pair Emp and Works, retain eid matches, preserve did, salary and multiplicity, group by did, compute COUNT and AVG, then retain groups over twenty. Physical execution need not materialise a product or sort. Do not de duplicate salaries in the middle, which would under count employees.

T02-E4-H

SeniorEmp可更新吗？若不可，如何修改？

Is Senior Emp updateable? If not, how can its definition be modified to make it updateable?

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

课程答案：因缺主键eid而不可，加入eid为sid。软件核对：PostgreSQL简单单表视图可不暴露主键而支持UPDATE/DELETE；INSERT若缺基表必填eid且无默认值仍失败。应标明按课程保守规则还是实际DBMS回答，不把两个层次混为一谈。

State whether applying the course’s conservative rule or the DBMS-specific rule.

CREATE VIEW SeniorEmp_with_id(sid,sname,sage,salary) AS
SELECT eid,ename,age,salary FROM Emp WHERE age>50;

T02-E4-I

按年龄分组求平均工资的AvgSalaryByAge可更新吗？说明理由。

Is Avg Salary By Age (age, avg Salary), defined by SELECT age, AVG(salary) FROM Emp GROUP BY age, updateable? Explain.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；下文含教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with teaching derivation and necessary corrections

默认不可更新。改变某年龄组的平均值可能对应许多底层员工工资修改，无法唯一翻译；即使当前某组只有一人，系统规则也不能假定未来永远一人。可定义特定触发器语义，但那是额外设计。

Not automatically updateable: a changed average admits many underlying salary modifications, with no unique translation. Even a current one-person group is not guaranteed always to stay so. A custom trigger can define semantics, but that is extra design.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut02-Views-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex4I · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex5–6视图定义与符号Ex 5–6 view definitions and notation

CREATE VIEW HeavyWeights("p#",wt,col) AS
SELECT "p#",weight,colour FROM P WHERE weight>14;

原题用p#作为示意标识符；PostgreSQL执行时此处加双引号。Ex6仅把SELECT中的weight改为weight*15，WHERE仍是weight>14，配套PPT p37确认这一读法。题干VALUE应为VALUES，智能引号要换成SQL单引号。

The original uses p# as a schematic identifier; executable PostgreSQL examples quote it. Ex 6 changes only the selected weight to weight*15, leaving WHERE weight>14; companion PPT p37 confirms this reading. Replace VALUE with VALUES and typographic quotes with SQL single quotes.

INFS7903 · CH04

完整性约束、序列与触发器Constraints, sequences and triggers

学习目标与问题背景Learning objectives and motivation

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-14（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是把业务陈述改写成可检验的不变量，选对声明式约束或触发器，并能设计成功、失败及边界测试。前置是键、NULL、SQL数据修改与事务的基本概念。约束不只是报错机制：它把正确性的要求放在所有应用共享的数据库入口，避免某个程序忘记检查。

The goal is to turn business statements into testable in variants, choose declarative constraints or triggers, and design successful, failing and boundary tests. Prerequisites are keys, NULL, SQL modifications and the basic idea of a transaction. Constraints do more than raise errors: they place correctness requirements at a database boundary shared by applications, preventing one program from silently omitting a check.

静态约束（static constraint）描述一个状态是否合法，如结束日期晚于开始日期；动态约束（dynamic constraint）描述从旧状态到新状态是否允许，如工资不得降低。跨行的预算总和仍是状态性质，不因“涉及很多行”就自动变成动态约束。涉及旧值与新值比较时，触发器通常更自然。

A static constraint describes a valid state, such as an end date after a start date. A dynamic constraint describes a permitted transition, such as never reducing salary. A cross-row salary budget is still a state property; involving many rows does not automatically make it dynamic. A trigger is often natural when old and new values must be compared.

先选声明式约束Prefer declarative constraints when they express the rule

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 9-19（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
机制 / Mechanism保证 / Guarantee边界 / Boundary
NOT NULL

必须有值。

A value must be present.

空字符串不是NULL。

Empty text is not NULL.

UNIQUE

约束列组合不重复。

Constrained combinations are unique.

PostgreSQL默认允许多个NULL；不是自动NOT NULL。

PostgreSQL normally allows multiple NULL s; it is not automatically NOT NULL.

PRIMARY KEY

唯一且非NULL，标识行。

Unique and non-NULL row identity.

复合主键每个分量都不可NULL。

Every component of a composite key is non-NULL.

FOREIGN KEY

非空引用必须匹配被引用键。

Non-null references match a referenced key.

删除父行需指定动作；可空引用本身可能合法。

Parent deletion needs an action; nullable references can be legal.

CHECK

每行条件不能为FALSE。

A row condition must not be FALSE.

UNKNOWN通常通过；要求必填时还需NOT NULL。

UNKNOWN normally passes; add NOT NULL for mandatory values.

例如 CHECK(salary>=10000) 并不禁止NULL工资，因为比较为UNKNOWN。若业务要求人人工资至少10,000，需salary NUMERIC NOT NULL CHECK(salary>=10000)。同一行的多个列可一起比较，例如CHECK(end_date>start_date)，并非CHECK只能看一列。PostgreSQL CHECK不支持用子查询跨表计数；用函数偷偷跨表读取也不能提供可靠的持续一致性保证。

CHECK(salary>=10000) does not reject NULL salary because the comparison is UNKNOWN. If every employee must earn at least 10,000, use salary NUMERIC NOT NULL CHECK(salary>=10000). A CHECK can compare several columns in the same row, such as CHECK(end_date>start_date); it is not restricted to one column. PostgreSQL CHECK does not support sub queries for cross-table counting, and hiding such lookups in a function does not provide reliable ongoing consistency.

PostgreSQL 17 · Constraints

CREATE TABLE dept(
 deptno INTEGER CONSTRAINT pk_deptno PRIMARY KEY,
 dname VARCHAR(14) CONSTRAINT un_dname UNIQUE,
 location VARCHAR(13) NOT NULL,
 CONSTRAINT chk_deptno CHECK(deptno>9 AND deptno<100)
);
ALTER TABLE emp ADD CONSTRAINT fk_deptno
FOREIGN KEY(deptno) REFERENCES dept(deptno) ON DELETE CASCADE;

外键动作与依赖方向Foreign-key actions and dependency direction

外键从子行指向父键。插入子行时检查父是否存在；删除父时检查谁依赖它。RESTRICT/NO ACTION通常拒绝破坏引用的删除；CASCADE删除依赖行；SET NULL保留行但清空引用；SET DEFAULT填默认引用且默认值仍须合法。若外键列是复合主键的一部分，SET NULL会与非空要求冲突。选择动作要解释业务含义，而不是为了让DELETE“能跑”而全部CASCADE。

A foreign key points from a child row to a parent key. Inserting a child checks parent existence; deleting a parent checks dependants. RESTRICT/NO ACTION normally reject broken references; CASCADE removes dependent rows; SET NULL preserves rows without their reference; SET DEFAULT assigns a default reference that must still be valid. SET NULL conflicts with a foreign-key column that forms part of a primary key. Choose actions for business meaning rather than using CASCADE everywhere just to make DELETE succeed.

Prac2 删除ENGINEERING部门时，默认外键会阻止留下悬空员工。课程DIY要求改ON DELETE CASCADE，操作是先DROP CONSTRAINT fk_deptno，再以新动作ADD同名外键。效果是删除该部门同时删除其中员工。真实系统可能更适合拒绝或转移部门；本练习的选择来自题目，不是所有组织的通用策略。

In Prac 2, deleting ENGINEERING under the default foreign key must not leave orphaned employees. The DIY asks for ON DELETE CASCADE: drop fk_deptno, then add it again with the new action. Deleting the department then deletes its employees. A real organisation might prefer rejection or reassignment; cascading is this exercise’s requirement, not a universal policy.

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac2-v2(1).pdf · PDF p. 8-9（原资料留在本地版） DIY 3.1–3.2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Practical 2：用反例检验约束Practical 2: test constraints with counterexamples

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac2-v2(1).pdf · PDF p. 3-9（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

先完成Prac1后再建DEPT，依次添加主键、范围CHECK、名称UNIQUE与LOCATION非空；尝试删除并重加UNIQUE以学会按名称管理。前三条合法部门是10 Accounting Brisbane、20 Marketing Sydney、30 Engineering Gold Coast。后面20 Management重复主键、9 Finance超出两位数范围、70 Marketing重复名称，应分别失败。

Complete Prac 1 first, then create DEPT and add its primary key, range CHECK, name UNIQUE and mandatory LOCATION. Drop and re-add UNIQUE to practise named management. Valid departments are 10 Accounting Brisbane, 20 Marketing Sydney and 30 Engineering Gold Coast. The later rows 20 Management, 9 Finance and 70 Marketing respectively duplicate a key, violate the two-digit range and duplicate a name; test each failure separately.

原资料同时给出“六行多值INSERT”示意和“前三行成功”的预期。PostgreSQL一个INSERT语句中只要有一行失败，整个语句都失败，不会自动保留前三行。要得到课程预期，可先用一条语句插入三条合法行，再分开尝试三条非法行。若在显式事务中出现错误，应ROLLBACK或回滚到SAVEPOINT后继续，不能直接忽略错误。

The source shows a six-row multi-value INSERT sketch alongside the expectation that only the first three rows succeed. In PostgreSQL, one invalid row fails the entire INSERT statement; the first three are not automatically retained. Insert the three valid rows together, then test invalid rows in separate statements. If an explicit transaction enters an error state, ROLLBACK or roll back to a SAVE POINT before continuing.

EMP后续要求ENAME唯一、JOB非空、HIREDATE晚于2000-01-01，并建立部门外键。Prac1已经把HIREDATE转成DATE，因此应按DATE比较，Prac2表中的VARCHAR标注不能推翻实际DDL。插入部门40的David应因无对应父行而失败。检查约束可用\d emp；目录查询按约束名连接时还应考虑模式与表，避免不同表同名约束造成错配。

EMP then requires unique ENAME, non-null JOB, HIREDATE after 2000-01-01 and a department foreign key. Prac 1 already converted HIREDATE to DATE, so use date comparison; the VARCHAR label in Prac 2’s table does not override actual DDL. Inserting David with department 40 should fail without that parent. Inspect constraints with \d emp. Catalogue queries joining only by constraint name should also account for schema and table to avoid collisions.

触发器的事件、条件与动作Trigger events, conditions and actions

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 21-26（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

触发器（trigger）按事件—条件—动作（event-condition-action，ECA）模型工作。事件如INSERT、UPDATE、DELETE；条件决定何时执行；动作检查或修改数据。BEFORE可验证或改NEW，AFTER适合在行修改后维护其他数据；INSTEAD OF在视图上替代原操作。FOR EACH ROW每影响一行触发一次，FOR EACH STATEMENT每条语句一次，即使影响0行也可能触发。

A trigger follows the event-condition-action (ECA) model. Events include INSERT, UPDATE and DELETE; conditions determine when the action applies; actions check or modify data. BEFORE can validate or change NEW, AFTER can maintain related data following a row change, and INSTEAD OF replaces an operation on a view. FOR EACH ROW fires once per affected row; FOR EACH STATEMENT fires once per statement, potentially even when zero rows are affected.

PostgreSQL触发器通常调用RETURNS trigger的函数。NEW是插入或更新后的行，OLD是更新前或删除的行；INSERT没有可用OLD，DELETE没有可用NEW。BEFORE行触发器RETURN NEW允许继续，RETURN NULL跳过该行；抛异常拒绝操作。AFTER行触发器返回值被忽略。不能在语句级触发器中把NEW当成唯一当前行，因为可能一次改了很多行。

A PostgreSQL trigger normally calls a function returning trigger. NEW is the proposed inserted or updated row; OLD is the pre-update or deleted row. INSERT has no usable OLD and DELETE no usable NEW. A BEFORE row trigger returns NEW to proceed, NULL to skip the row, or raises an exception to reject the operation. An AFTER row trigger’s return value is ignored. A statement trigger cannot treat NEW as one current row when a statement can change many rows.

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_salary_reduction()
RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
 IF NEW.salary < OLD.salary THEN
   RAISE EXCEPTION 'Salary cannot be reduced';
 END IF;
 RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
CREATE TRIGGER no_salary_reduction
BEFORE UPDATE OF salary ON person
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_salary_reduction();

上述为教学改写，假设person.salary非空。10,000→9,000应拒绝，10,000→10,000允许，10,000→11,000允许。若允许NULL，必须先定义NULL转换的业务含义，再写IS DISTINCT FROM等条件。课程Oracle例子使用:NEW与BEGIN…END内联体，不能原样复制到PostgreSQL；Week04 p26还有INITCASP拼写，应核对INITCAP。通知经理的伪过程不等于真的拒绝更改。

The teaching rewrite assumes non-null person. salary. Reject 10,000→9,000, allow equality and allow 10,000→11,000. If NULL is allowed, first define its transition semantics and then use suitable tests such as IS DISTINCT FROM. The course Oracle examples use: NEW and an inline BEGIN…END body, which cannot be pasted unchanged into PostgreSQL. Week 04 p26 also has INITCASP where IN IT CAP should be checked. A pseudo procedure that notifies a manager does not itself reject an invalid change.

Practical 3：序列与触发器配合Practical 3: sequences and triggers

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac3-V2.pdf · PDF p. 3-8（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

序列（sequence）提供并发安全的数值分配对象，nextval每次取下一个值。它不是行数，也不保证没有间断：失败或回滚可能已经消耗号码。课程EMPNO_SEQ从1000开始，而已有EMP含1001–1004；如果直接沿用旧数据，第一个1000可能成功，后面的1001会冲突。要按实验前提清空练习数据或把起点调整到现有最大值以上，不能把“序列本身唯一”误认为“与已有表值永远不冲突”。

A sequence is an object for concurrency-safe numerical allocation, with nextval obtaining the next value. It is not a row count and does not guarantee gap less numbering: failed operations or rollbacks can consume values. The course EMPNO_SEQ starts at 1000 while existing EMP rows include 1001–1004. Reusing that data can let 1000 succeed and 1001 conflict. Follow the experiment’s reset assumptions or start above existing values; uniqueness within a sequence does not prevent collisions with pre-existing table values.

CREATE SEQUENCE deptno_seq MINVALUE 50 MAXVALUE 99
INCREMENT BY 10 START WITH 50;
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_deptno() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
 NEW.deptno := nextval('deptno_seq');
 RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
CREATE TRIGGER bi_dept BEFORE INSERT ON dept
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_deptno();
INSERT INTO dept(dname,location) VALUES('Training','Brisbane');
SELECT * FROM information_schema.sequences;
SELECT tgname FROM pg_trigger WHERE NOT tgisinternal;

该部门序列依次给50、60、70、80、90，下一次100超过MAXVALUE=99会失败；这与两位数CHECK也一致。Prac3 p6把某次预期错误归因于CHECK，但若发生在EMP插入，重复主键、名称UNIQUE或外键也可能先报错。应以真实约束名和执行顺序诊断；本次未运行该实验，不能指定唯一真实错误。清理时先删使用它的触发器，再按依赖删除序列或函数。

The department sequence yields 50,60,70,80,90, then fails because 100 exceeds MAXVALUE=99; that also aligns with the two-digit CHECK. Prac 3 p6 attributes an expected error to a CHECK, but EMP insertions may first encounter duplicate keys, name uniqueness or foreign keys. Diagnose using the actual constraint name and execution order. This experiment was not run here, so no unique observed error is asserted. Clean up dependent triggers before removing their sequence or function as appropriate.

跨行规则、并发与完整测试Cross-row rules, concurrency and complete testing

“员工工资不超过经理工资”不能只检查插入员工：员工调薪、换经理，经理降薪都可能破坏不变量。需要覆盖所有能影响规则的事件。单次SELECT经理工资后检查也不自动防止另一个事务同时改经理工资；必须选择一致的锁定策略或更强隔离并处理重试。第11章将解释为什么“先查再写”可能竞争。

“Employee salary must not exceed the manager’s” requires more than checking employee insertion. Salary changes, manager reassignment and manager pay reductions can all break it. Cover every event affecting the invariant. Reading the manager’s salary and checking it does not automatically prevent another transaction changing that salary concurrently. Choose a consistent locking strategy or stronger isolation with retry handling; Chapter 11 explains this check-then-write race.

类似“部门工资总和小于预算”应说明严格小于还是小于等于、空部门总和按0处理、转部门影响旧部门和新部门、预算减少也要检查。触发器的形式正确不等于业务规则完整。测试矩阵至少包含正常值、等号边界、越界、NULL、批量语句、更新关键引用、删除和两个并发会话。

For “department total salary below budget”, specify strict versus inclusive comparison, treat an empty sum as zero when intended, account for both departments during transfers and check budget decreases too. A syntactically valid trigger need not implement the complete rule. Test normal values, equality boundaries, violations, NULL, multi row statements, changed references, deletions and two concurrent sessions.

延迟检查与历史验证Deferred checking and historical validation

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 27-32（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

延迟（deferred）是把允许延迟的约束推迟到提交时检查，适合一个事务中暂时违反引用、最后修好的情形；禁用（disabled）是根本不检查；NOT VALID是暂不扫描验证旧数据，但约束仍检查新的相关修改。三者不可混同。PostgreSQL不是所有约束都能延迟，例如CHECK不按SET CONSTRAINTS延迟。

Deferred checking postpones a deferrable constraint until commit, useful when references are temporarily inconsistent within a transaction but repaired before it ends. Disabled means checking is turned off. NOT VALID skips initial validation of historical data while enforcing relevant new changes. These are distinct. PostgreSQL cannot defer every constraint; CHECK is not deferred by SET CONSTRAINTS.

ALTER TABLE emp ADD CONSTRAINT fk_dept_review
FOREIGN KEY(deptno) REFERENCES dept(deptno)
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY IMMEDIATE NOT VALID;
ALTER TABLE emp VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_dept_review;
BEGIN;
SET CONSTRAINTS fk_dept_review DEFERRED;
-- related modifications that restore the constraint by commit
COMMIT;

课程页中的ENABLE NOVALIDATE、DISABLE CONSTRAINT及ALTER TRIGGER … DISABLE主要是Oracle语法，不能因旁边链接写PostgreSQL就视为可执行。PostgreSQL触发器启停的典型语法为ALTER TABLE t DISABLE TRIGGER trigger_name，与约束延迟不是同一操作。操作类答案须写清DBMS与版本，课程服务器版本本次未知。

ENABLE NO VALIDATE, DISABLE CONSTRAINT and ALTER TRIGGER … DISABLE in the lecture are mainly Oracle syntax; a nearby PostgreSQL link does not make them executable there. PostgreSQL typically uses ALTER TABLE t DISABLE TRIGGER trigger_name for trigger control, which differs from deferring a constraint. State the DBMS and version for operational answers; the course-server version is unknown here.

PostgreSQL 17 · ALTER TABLE · SET CONSTRAINTS

总结与自测Summary and self-check

把约束设计成“规则—作用范围—受影响事件—并发策略—测试”的闭环。声明式机制优先处理键、域和引用，触发器处理转移或复杂跨行规则。每次例外都要说明NULL、历史数据和提交时状态。A1提供了年龄、容量、状态迁移和派生积分的综合应用，第13章建立对应索引。

Complete the loop from rule to scope, affected events, concurrency strategy and tests. Use declarative mechanisms for keys, domains and references, with triggers for transitions and complex cross-row rules. Explain NULL, historical data and commit-time state for exceptions. A1 combines age, capacity, state transitions and derived credits; Chapter 13 maps these applications.

C4-S1

自编练习：CHECK(x>0)是否拒绝NULL？怎样要求必填正值？

Does CHECK(x>0) reject NULL? How would you require a positive value?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

不会因该CHECK拒绝，NULL比较为UNKNOWN。使用x NUMERIC NOT NULL CHECK(x>0)，分别负责存在性与范围。测试NULL、0、负数与正数，前三类拒绝、正数通过。

The CHECK alone does not reject it because NULL comparison is UNKNOWN. Use x NUMERIC NOT NULL CHECK(x>0), separately enforcing presence and range. Test NULL, zero, negative and positive values: reject the first three categories and allow the last.

C4-S2

自编练习：为何两个看似正确的容量检查触发器仍可能超额报名？

Why can two correct-looking capacity triggers still overbook a shift?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

两个事务都可能读到同一个未满计数，各自允许一行后共同超过容量。触发器在各自事务内执行，不自带全局串行化。可围绕同一父资源行采用一致锁定并保证所有写路径遵守，或使用能检测冲突的隔离与重试设计。

Both transactions can read the same below-capacity count and independently allow an insertion whose combined effect exceeds capacity. A trigger executes within its transaction and does not provide global serialisation by itself. Consistently lock the shared parent resource across all write paths, or use conflict-detecting isolation with retries.

T04-E2-A

给出涉及Dept的外键，并说明删除部门时可采取哪些动作。

Give an example of a foreign key involving Dept. What are the options when deleting a Dept tuple?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

Works.did→Dept.did。可拒绝、CASCADE删除关联、SET DEFAULT到真实存在默认部门、SET NULL。最后一种在did属于主键(eid,did)时会违反非空，课程明确说此处暂忽略主键；实际设计不能忽略。NO ACTION可在可延迟条件下与RESTRICT检查时机不同。

Use Works.did→Dept.did. Options are rejection, cascading dependent Works rows, setting a valid default department or setting NULL. SET NULL conflicts with did being part of (eid, did); the course temporarily ignores that key, but a real design cannot. NO ACTION can differ from RESTRICT in deferrable checking timing.

T04-E2-B

创建三个关系并声明适当主键、外键。

Write SQL to create the relations with appropriate primary and foreign keys.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

先Emp，再引用它的Dept，再Works。此解选择删除员工时清空部门经理、删除Works关联；删除部门时级联删除Works。课程SQL把ON DELETE前多写逗号，导致它脱离外键；这里放在对应REFERENCES之后。题目接受其他合理策略，但Ex2D必须与所选DDL一致。

Create Emp, then Dept, then Works. This solution clears a deleted manager reference and cascades deleted employee/department associations. The course syntax has commas separating ON DELETE from its FK; place the clause directly after the matching REFERENCES. Other justified policies are possible, but Ex 2 D must match the chosen DDL.

CREATE TABLE Emp(eid INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,ename TEXT,age INTEGER,salary NUMERIC);
CREATE TABLE Dept(did INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,dname TEXT,budget NUMERIC,
 managerid INTEGER REFERENCES Emp(eid) ON DELETE SET NULL);
CREATE TABLE Works(eid INTEGER REFERENCES Emp(eid) ON DELETE CASCADE,
 did INTEGER REFERENCES Dept(did) ON DELETE CASCADE,pcttime INTEGER,
 PRIMARY KEY(eid,did));
来源 / Source: tut/Tut04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 2-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex2B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T04-E2-C

保证每个部门都有经理。

Define Dept so every department is guaranteed to have a manager.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

managerid必须NOT NULL且REFERENCES Emp(eid)。只有外键仍可NULL，只有NOT NULL可能引用不存在的人。若选择ON DELETE SET NULL则与此要求冲突，应改为拒绝删除或先重派经理。

Require managerid NOT NULL REFERENCES Emp (eid). A foreign key alone allows NULL, while NOT NULL alone can name a nonexistent employee. ON DELETE SET NULL conflicts with this requirement, so reject such deletions or reassign first.

ALTER TABLE Dept ALTER COLUMN managerid SET NOT NULL;

T04-E3-1

自定示例模式，用触发器保证工资不得降低。

Write an SQL database trigger enforcing: a person’s salary cannot be reduced. You may assume a sample schema.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

假设person(id主键,salary非空)。BEFORE UPDATE比较OLD与NEW，小于时RAISE EXCEPTION；相等与增加允许。完整PostgreSQL函数见第4章prevent_salary_reduction。课程AFTER+INFORM_MANAGER是概念伪代码，通知本身不阻止更新，不能当作完整强制约束。

Assume person (id primary key, salary non-null). Compare OLD and NEW in a BEFORE UPDATE trigger and RAISE EXCEPTION on reduction; equality and increases pass. Chapter 4 gives the complete PostgreSQL function prevent_salary_reduction. The course AFTER/INFORM_MANAGER example is conceptual pseudo code; notification alone does not prevent the update.

T04-E3-2

用触发器保证Weekly_Income与Repay_Amount联动更新。

Write a trigger enforcing: Weekly_Income must be updated together with Repay_Amount.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

题目未给还款公式，课程自行假设缺值时按15%。以下给明确的教学假设：还款始终等于周收入15%，salary等字段不相关；BEFORE中直接设置NEW避免AFTER再次UPDATE造成递归。若题意仅要求用户在同一语句显式提供两列，必须另定义检测规则，不能把15%伪装成题干事实。

The question gives no repayment formula; the course assumes 15% when missing. This teaching version explicitly assumes repayment always equals 15% of weekly income. Set NEW directly in BEFORE to avoid recursive AFTER updates. If the intended rule merely requires explicitly supplying both columns in a statement, specify a different detection rule; 15% is not a fact given by the question.

CREATE FUNCTION sync_repayment() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
 NEW.repay_amount := NEW.weekly_income*0.15;
 RETURN NEW;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER sync_income BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF weekly_income,repay_amount
ON person FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION sync_repayment();
来源 / Source: tut/Tut04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 2-6（原资料留在本地版） Ex3.2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T04-E3-3

自定模式，用触发器保证部门工资总和严格小于预算。

Write a trigger enforcing SUM(DEPT. Salary)<BUDGET. Salary, assuming sample schemas.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

取staff(id,dname,salary非空)与dept(name,budget非空)。每次人员插入、工资变动、调部门及部门预算变动，都需验证相关部门COALESCE(SUM,0)<budget；不满足就抛异常。课程语句级触发器却直接引用NEW.dname，且“通知”没有拒绝，不能直接执行。以下为单会话教学函数，按AFTER行触发；并发仍需所有写路径统一锁父部门并处理死锁/重试。严格小于在sum=budget时也必须拒绝。

Assume staff (id, dname, salary non-null) and dept (name, budget non-null). Validate COALESCE(SUM,0)<budget after insertions, salary changes, department transfers and budget changes, rejecting violations. The course statement trigger incorrectly uses row NEW.dname, and notification does not reject changes. This single-session teaching function uses AFTER row triggers; concurrent enforcement additionally needs consistent parent locking across all paths with deadlock/retry handling. Strict less-than also rejects equality.

CREATE FUNCTION check_budgets() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
 IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dept d WHERE
   COALESCE((SELECT SUM(s.salary) FROM staff s WHERE s.dname=d.name),0)>=d.budget)
 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Department budget exceeded'; END IF;
 RETURN NULL;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER check_staff_budget AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON staff
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION check_budgets();
CREATE TRIGGER check_budget_change AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON dept
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION check_budgets();
来源 / Source: tut/Tut04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 2-6（原资料留在本地版） Ex3.3 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
