T04-E4-2

保证所有经理年龄大于30。

Define a table constraint on Dept ensuring all managers have age>30.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

课程给CHECK含子查询，不是PostgreSQL支持的CHECK。可用触发器：Dept新增/改managerid时查Emp.age，Emp改age时若它是经理也检查。以下假设age非NULL、允许无经理部门，只约束非空managerid；若要求人人有经理另加NOT NULL。年龄正好30拒绝，31允许。

The course CHECK with a sub query is not supported by PostgreSQL. Use triggers on Dept insert/manager changes and Emp age changes. This example assumes non-null employee age and allows departments without managers, constraining only existing manager references; add NOT NULL if managers are mandatory. Reject age 30 and allow 31.

CREATE FUNCTION managers_over_30() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
 IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM Dept d JOIN Emp e ON e.eid=d.managerid
            WHERE e.age IS NULL OR e.age<=30)
 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Manager must be older than 30'; END IF;
 RETURN NULL;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER check_manager_assignment AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON Dept
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION managers_over_30();
CREATE TRIGGER check_manager_age AFTER UPDATE OF age ON Emp
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION managers_over_30();
来源 / Source: tut/Tut04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-6（原资料留在本地版） Ex4.2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T04-E4-3

删除工资高于其任一任职部门经理的员工信息，并保持引用完整性。

Delete all information about employees whose salaries exceed that of the manager of one or more departments they work in. Preserve relevant integrity constraints.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

先按W.eid=e.eid相关到当前员工，再对他工作的部门找到经理，存在一名较低薪经理则删除。选择Works.eid ON DELETE CASCADE、Dept.managerid ON DELETE SET NULL时，关联自动删除、经理引用清空。若采用Ex2C的经理非空要求，就需先重派经理或拒绝删除，不能同时假设SET NULL合法。课程首个查询缺W.eid=e.eid相关，且Work/Works混用；后面的ANY版修复相关意图。

Correlate W.eid=e.eid to the current employee, locate managers only for that employee’s departments and delete if any manager earns less. With Works.eid ON DELETE CASCADE and nullable Dept.managerid ON DELETE SET NULL, associations disappear and management references clear. If managers are mandatory as in Ex 2 C, reassign first or reject deletion. The course’s first query omits employee correlation and mixes Work/Works; its ANY version repairs the intended correlation.

DELETE FROM Emp e
WHERE EXISTS (
 SELECT 1 FROM Works w JOIN Dept d ON d.did=w.did
 JOIN Emp manager ON manager.eid=d.managerid
 WHERE w.eid=e.eid AND e.salary>manager.salary
);
来源 / Source: tut/Tut04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-7（原资料留在本地版） Ex4.3 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

INFS7903 · CH05

存储组织与磁盘计算Storage organisation and disk calculations

学习目标与前置知识Learning objectives and prerequisites

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week05-Storage.pdf · PDF p. 2-15（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是解释数据为何按块传输，区分随机与顺序I/O，计算容量、记录长度和块数，并依据工作负载选择heap、ordered或hash文件。前置知识补充：1 byte=8 bits；本教材容量计算默认十进制kB=1000 bytes、MB=10⁶ bytes，KiB=1024 bytes。不要在同一计算中混用毫秒和秒，也不要把位每秒当字节每秒。

The goal is to explain block transfers, distinguish random from sequential I/O, calculate capacities, record lengths and block counts, and select heap, ordered or hash files for a workload. Prerequisite supplement: one byte is eight bits; capacity calculations here use decimal k B=1000 bytes and MB=10⁶ bytes unless stated, while Ki B=1024 bytes. Do not mix milliseconds and seconds or bits/s with bytes/s.

存储层次（storage hierarchy）把正在使用的数据放RAM，大量持久数据放磁盘，归档可用更慢介质。RAM快但容量和成本受限且通常易失；HDD包含机械运动，SSD没有寻道臂与旋转等待。课程的寻道公式是HDD教学模型，不能直接用于估算SSD的机械延迟。缓存复用热点内容，缓冲协调速度不匹配；同一内存区在实现中可以兼具两种用途。

The storage hierarchy places active data in RAM, persistent bulk data on disk and archives on slower media. RAM is fast but limited, costly and normally volatile. HDD s involve mechanical motion; SSD s have no seek arm or rotational wait. The course’s seek formulas model HDD s and should not be applied as mechanical SSD delays. Caches reuse hot data and buffers smooth transfer-rate mismatches; an implementation can use a memory region for both purposes.

直接内存访问（direct memory access，DMA）由CPU设置源/目的地址、长度与方向，再让控制器完成大块传输，减少CPU逐字节搬运。I/O仍可能等待设备。排队延迟来自多个请求争设备，热点来自请求集中到同一区域，访问延迟来自设备完成单次请求所需时间。三个问题可能同时发生。

With direct memory access (DMA), the CPU sets addresses, length and transfer direction, then a controller moves a block without CPU copying each byte. The I/O can still wait for the device. Queueing delay comes from requests competing for service; hotspots concentrate requests on one area; access latency is the time to serve an individual request. All three can coexist.

磁盘几何与容量Disk geometry and capacity

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week05-Storage.pdf · PDF p. 16-24（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

盘片（platter）有一个或两个记录表面；每面有同心磁道（track），每道划分扇区（sector）。所有表面相同半径的磁道合称柱面（cylinder）；磁头（head）随磁臂（arm）移动选择柱面，盘片旋转让目标扇区经过磁头。柱面数等于每表面磁道数，不是所有表面的磁道总数。

A platter has one or two recording surfaces, each with concentric tracks divided into sectors. Tracks of the same radius across surfaces form a cylinder. Heads move with an arm to select a cylinder; rotation brings the required sector under a head. The number of cylinders equals tracks per surface, not total tracks across all surfaces.

C_track = sectors_per_track × bytes_per_sector
C_cylinder = surfaces × C_track
C_disk = surfaces × tracks_per_surface × C_track

Tutorial 5 Ex7：B=512 bytes，每磁道20块，每面400道，15张双面盘片。表面数30；每道20×512=10,240 bytes；柱面400个；每柱面30×10,240=307,200 bytes；总盘30×400×10,240=122,880,000 bytes=122.88MB。这里每块512 bytes，题目把块与扇区容量取成一致；真实文件系统不必这样。

For Tutorial 5 Ex 7, B=512 bytes, there are 20 blocks per track, 400 tracks per surface and 15 double-sided platters. Thirty surfaces give 20×512=10,240 bytes per track, 400 cylinders, 30×10,240=307,200 bytes per cylinder and 30×400×10,240=122,880,000 bytes=122.88 MB overall. This exercise uses 512-byte blocks with the assumed sector-sized unit; real filesystems need not do so.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex7 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

完整I/O计算：寻道、等待与传输Full I/O calculation: seek, wait and transfer

设转速r为rpm，一圈时间Trev=60,000/r毫秒；随机到达时平均旋转等待rd=Trev/2。传输率tr=每道容量/Trev，块传输时间btt=B/tr。单次随机块访问平均为s+rd+btt，其中s是平均寻道。连续k块若不再重新定位，成本近似s+rd+k×btt；k个随机块近似k×(s+rd+btt)。忽略排队、缓存、控制器等额外开销。

For rotation speed r rpm, a revolution takes Trev=60,000/r milliseconds, with average rotational delay rd=Trev/2 for random arrivals. Transfer rate tr=track capacity/Trev and block transfer time btt=B/tr. One random block averages s+rd+btt, where s is average seek time. If k contiguous blocks require no further positioning, approximate s+rd+k×btt; k random blocks cost k×(s+rd+btt). These models omit queueing, caching, controller and other overheads.

Trev=60000/r ms　　rd=Trev/2　　tr=C_track/Trev
T_random(k)=k(s+rd+btt)　　T_contiguous(k)=s+rd+k·btt

继续Ex7，r=3000rpm，所以Trev=20ms、rd=10ms。tr=10240/20=512 bytes/ms，btt=512/512=1ms。若s=30ms，一块41ms；20个随机块820ms，而20个连续块60ms。差异主要来自重复付出的寻道与等待，不是每块“字节突然传得更快”。

Continuing Ex 7, r=3000 rpm gives Trev=20 ms and rd=10 ms. tr=10240/20=512 bytes/ms and btt=512/512=1 ms. With s=30 ms, one block takes 41 ms, twenty random blocks 820 ms and twenty contiguous blocks 60 ms. The saving mainly avoids repeated seek and rotation costs; it does not make each byte transfer intrinsically faster.

Week05 pp22–24 的5400rpm例子约等于11.11ms/圈而非精确11ms。若保留精度，50×512=25,600bytes每道，tr=2,304,000 bytes/s，rd≈5.556ms；平均寻道10ms时读整道约26.667ms。课件26.5ms源于中间先取11ms。报告时注明舍入策略，避免把单位换算差异当概念错误。

The 5400 rpm example on Week 05 pp 22–24 is approximately 11.11 ms per revolution, not exactly 11 ms. Retaining precision with 50×512=25,600 bytes per track gives tr=2,304,000 bytes/s and rd≈5.556 ms. A10 ms seek plus a full-track read takes about 26.667 ms. The slide’s 26.5 ms rounds the revolution to 11 ms first. State rounding policy rather than confusing it with a conceptual difference.

记录布局与块因子Record layout and blocking factor

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week05-Storage.pdf · PDF p. 27-38（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

文件（file）是记录的集合，物理上由块组成。块（block/page）是课程中的I/O单位，记录（record）是应用处理的业务单位。定长记录（fixed-length record）可用offset=记录号×R+字段偏移定位字段；例如SID4字节、Name24字节、Age4字节，Age偏移28，第二条记录Age偏移32+28=60。这里从0计数，不含页头。

A file contains records and is physically divided into blocks. A block/page is the course’s I/O unit; a record is the application’s business unit. For fixed-length records, field offset=record index×R+field offset. With SID=4 bytes, Name=24 and Age=4, Age begins at offset 28, and the second record’s Age at 32+28=60. This uses zero-based indexing and omits page headers.

定长布局简单，字段定位快，但最大长度字段会浪费空间，块尾也可能留下放不下整条记录的空隙。变长记录（variable-length record）可用长度前缀、分隔符或偏移目录记录边界；更节省实际值空间，却需要额外元数据，记录增长可能导致移动。课程顺序解析例子要先走过Name才能找Age；带偏移目录的实际布局可避免每次逐字段扫描。

Fixed layouts simplify operations and field access but can waste space inside maximum-length fields and at block tails. Variable-length records use length prefixes, delimiters or offset directories to mark boundaries. They save space for actual values but require metadata and can move when growing. The course’s sequential encoding requires traversing Name before Age; practical offset-directory layouts can avoid repeated field-by-field parsing.

不跨块（unspanned）要求一条记录整体装进一块：bfr=⌊B/R⌋，块数b=⌈N/bfr⌉。跨块（spanned）允许一条记录分布在多块；如果每块留P字节指针，按总字节估计b=⌈NR/(B−P)⌉。跨块与定长/变长是两个独立维度；课件“定长不能跨块”是其示例组织的约定，不是普遍物理定律。

Unspanned organisation keeps a whole record in one block: bfr=⌊B/R⌋ and b=⌈N/bfr⌉. Spanned organisation permits a record across blocks; if P bytes per block hold a pointer, estimate b=⌈NR/(B−P)⌉. Spanning and fixed versus variable length are separate dimensions. The slide’s statement that fixed-length records cannot span blocks is a convention of its example, not a universal physical law.

unspanned:　bfr=⌊B/R⌋,　b=⌈N/bfr⌉
spanned estimate:　b=⌈N·Ravg/(B−P)⌉

Ex8字段字节合计112，删除标记再1，R=113。512字节块只能放4条，20,000条需5,000块，每个装满4条的块余60字节。Ex9变长格式在每个出现字段上还加1字节类型：必现字段及两个标记100字节；选填部分=10×0.8+5×0.85+5×0.15+4×0.9=16.6，总平均116.6。虽然可选字段减少，类型开销可让平均记录反而大于113，不能先验认为变长一定更短。

In Ex 8, field bytes total 112 plus a deletion marker, giving R=113. A512-byte block holds four records, so 20,000 records need 5,000 blocks, leaving 60 bytes in each four-record block. In Ex 9, each present field also has a one-byte type tag: mandatory fields and two markers use 100 bytes; optional fields average 10×0.8+5×0.85+5×0.15+4×0.9=16.6, giving 116.6 bytes. Optional omission can be outweighed by tag overhead, so variable length is not automatically shorter.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 2-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex8–9 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

三种文件组织与查询选择Three file organisations and workload choices

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week05-Storage.pdf · PDF p. 39-56（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

堆文件（heap file）不按业务键排序，适合追加和全扫描。插入可读尾块、加记录、写回；删除常标记空位供复用。链表实现区分满块与有空闲块；目录实现记录每块剩余空间，便于找插入位置。heap不是内存管理中的堆，也不意味着每次查询能按值直接随机定位。

A heap file has no business-key order and suits appending and full scans. Insertion can read the last block, add a record and write it back; deletion can mark reusable space. Linked-list implementations separate full and non full blocks; directory implementations record free space to locate insertion pages. This heap differs from a memory-allocation heap and does not support direct value-based random lookup merely by being unordered.

有序文件（ordered/sequential file）按排序键物理存放，适合按该键二分定位或连续范围读取。插入要保序，可能移动很多记录，也可先写溢出区再定期合并。二分查找维护候选区间，每次取中点比较后删掉不可能的一半，保证目标若存在仍在剩余区间；文件没按查询字段排序时不能对该字段直接二分。

An ordered/sequential file physically follows a sort key, supporting binary positioning and contiguous range reads on that key. Insertions must preserve order and can move many records, or use an overflow area with periodic merging. Binary search maintains a candidate interval and discards the impossible half after each midpoint comparison, preserving any existing target in the remaining interval. It cannot be applied directly to an un sorted query field.

交互：二分查找的候选区间（自编例子）Interactive: the shrinking binary-search interval (author-created example)

步骤 / Step区间、比较与动作 / Interval, comparison and action
1

值1…14，找13；mid=7，13>7。

Values 1…14, target 13; mid=7, move right.

2

区间8…14，mid=11，13>11。

Interval 8…14, mid=11, move right.

3

区间12…14，mid=13，找到。

Interval 12…14, mid=13, found.

哈希文件（hash file）把记录放到h(k)指定的桶。课程例子把电话分机所有数字相加，再取末位：659072与652907之和都是29，落桶9。不同键落同桶叫碰撞；桶满后需溢出块。均匀散列降低链长，过高装载率或倾斜会让查找退化。范围内连续键不一定落相邻桶，因此范围查找通常需扫描文件。

A hash file places a record in bucket h (k). The course sums extension digits and takes the final digit: both 659072 and 652907 sum to 29 and map to bucket 9. Different keys mapping to one bucket collide; a full bucket requires overflow space. Uniform hashing reduces chains, while high load or skew can degrade search. Consecutive keys need not occupy adjacent buckets, so range queries normally scan the file.

操作 / OperationHeapOrderedHash

表格是课程简单外存模型，M为数据块数，不包括缓存和索引。Hash的范围成本不能只数结果块，因为必须先发现在哪些桶。生命周期选择：先写入增长可用heap，稳定只读范围分析可用ordered，大量等值读写可考虑hash；如果读写条件不可预测，hash不自动胜出，还应结合第6章索引与更新成本。

The table uses the course’s simple external-memory model, with M data blocks and no cache or indexes. Hash range cost cannot count only output blocks because the system must first discover their buckets. During a lifecycle, append-heavy growth can suit heap, stable read-only range analysis ordered files, and equality-heavy reads/writes hashing. Unpredictable predicates do not automatically favour hashing; consider Chapter 6 indexes and update costs too.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

存储题先画层次：盘→面→道→块→记录，逐层核对单位。访问题先判断顺序还是随机，记录题先判断跨块与开销，文件题先问查询按哪个字段、等值还是范围。统计上成功唯一线性搜索约M/2块；失败搜索与需取全体匹配的非唯一搜索通常扫描全部。最坏二分探测次数更精确可写⌊log₂M⌋+1，课程常用⌈log₂M⌉近似，边界如M=1应单独处理。

For storage calculations, draw disk→surface→track→block→record and check units at every level. For access, distinguish sequential from random. For records, identify spanning and overheads. For files, ask which field and whether equality or range. A successful unique linear search averages about M/2 blocks; failed searches and complete non unique retrieval normally scan all. A more precise worst-case binary probe count is ⌊log₂M⌋+1; the course often approximates with ⌈log₂M⌉, so handle boundaries such as M=1 separately.

C5-S1

自编练习：每块1000字节、每条120字节且不跨块。81条需要多少块？

A block holds 1000 bytes and an unspanned record uses 120 bytes. How many blocks hold 81 records?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

bfr=⌊1000/120⌋=8。b=⌈81/8⌉=11。前10块各8条，末块1条；满块每块浪费40字节，末块空余880字节。不能把满块剩余量当成最后一块剩余量。

bfr=⌊1000/120⌋=8 and b=⌈81/8⌉=11. Ten blocks hold eight records each and the last holds one. A full block leaves 40 bytes, while the last leaves 880. Do not apply the full-block waste to the final partial block.

C5-S2

自编练习：为何减少记录字节数可能并未显著改善单条随机查询？

When would reducing bytes per record fail to improve a random single-record query much?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

如果仍需相同次数的随机定位，成本仍由寻道和旋转主导，传输节省只占小部分。但更短记录提高bfr、减少扫描页数、改善缓存，批量查询可能明显受益。必须说明所讨论的工作负载。

If the same number of random positions must be accessed, seek and rotation still dominate and transfer savings are small. Shorter records can nevertheless increase bfr, reduce scan pages and improve caching, substantially benefiting bulk queries. State the workload under discussion.

Tutorial 5：存储讨论与全部计算Tutorial 5: storage discussions and calculations

T05-E1

关系数据库怎样保存元数据和业务数据？

In a relational database system, how are metadata and data stored?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

元数据存系统目录，如模式、约束、索引定义与统计；业务值存在数据文件页中，索引节点也有物理文件页。两者都二进制存储。访问SQL先解析名称与权限，再按文件/索引定位具体页；不是目录替代业务数据。

Metadata resides in the system catalogue, including schemas, constraints, index definitions and statistics. Business values occupy data-file pages and index nodes have physical pages too. Both use binary representation. SQL resolves names and privileges before using files/indexes to locate pages; catalogue metadata does not replace business data.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） E1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T05-E2-1

Draw disk access showing Sector, Tracks, Heads, Arm, Cylinder and Platters.

Use the original Week 05 p17 diagram in Chapter 5: multiple platters, concentric tracks on each surface, sectors along a track, a cylinder of equal-radius tracks across surfaces, and heads linked to the arm. A cylinder is not the set of every track.

T05-E2-2

数据访问中的块是什么？

What is a block in data access?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

本课程中块是磁盘与内存之间的一次传输单位，可包含多个相邻扇区；记录按块组织。物理扇区、DBMS页、操作系统页不必在所有系统具有同一大小，题目给什么B就用什么。

In the course, a block is a transfer unit between disk and memory and can contain adjacent sectors. Records are organised into blocks. Physical sectors, DBMS pages and OS pages need not share one size; use the specified B.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） E2-2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T05-E2-3

记录如何组织在heap、sequential、hash文件的块中？

How are records organised into blocks in (1)heap,(2)sequential,(3)hash files?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

Heap无键序，链表或目录跟踪块与空位；Sequential按排序字段有序，查范围可连续读；Hash按h(k)分桶，满桶接溢出块。定长/变长布局与这三种文件类型是不同维度。

Heap has no key order and uses lists/directories for pages and free space. Sequential files follow a sort field for contiguous range reads. Hash files use h (k) buckets and overflow blocks. Fixed/variable record layout is separate from these organisations.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） E2-3 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T05-E2-4

为什么寻道和旋转等待主导HDD I/O？

Why do seek time and rotation delay dominate disk I/O?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

机械定位与转动远慢于电子传输与内存操作；随机请求反复支付这两项。连续块只支付较少定位，所以省时。该解释针对HDD，不把机械延迟套到SSD。

Mechanical positioning and rotation are slow relative to electronic transfer and memory operations. Random requests repeatedly pay them; contiguous reads reduce positioning. This explanation concerns HDD s rather than mechanical delays in SSD s.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） E2-4 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T05-E3

讨论排队延迟、热点及不同文件的处理策略。

Discuss queueing delay and hotspots in disk I/O and strategies for different file types.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

排队是请求量超过服务速度形成等待，热点是访问集中。缓存、预取和批量顺序读减少设备工作；heap目录找空位减少链表遍历，ordered范围利用局部性，hash需均匀分桶并控制溢出。多盘或读副本可分摊读取，写复制还要保持一致。增加空间可换时间但不是无成本。

Queueing occurs when demand exceeds service rate; hotspots concentrate access. Caching, pre fetching and sequential batches reduce device work. Heap directories reduce free-space travers al, ordered ranges exploit locality, and hash layouts need even buckets with controlled overflow. Multiple disks/read replicas distribute reads, while replicated writes require consistency. Trading space for time still has costs.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut05-Storage-2026(1).pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） E3 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T05-E4

比较定长与变长记录的存储和访问。

Compare fixed-length and variable-length records in storage and access.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导、更正或明确假设 / Course answer with derivation, corrections or explicit assumptions

定长字段偏移直接计算、修改简单，代价是最大长度填充和块尾碎片。变长减少字段填充但需长度/分隔/偏移元数据，增长可能移动；顺序编码定位后字段需跳过前字段，偏移目录可改善。两者都要按具体跨块规则算容量，变长并不保证总开销更小。

Fixed length permits direct offsets and simple modification but wastes padding and block tails. Variable length reduces field padding but needs length/delimiter/offset metadata and may move on growth. Sequential encoding traverses preceding fields, while offset directories help. Compute capacity under the actual spanning rule; variable length need not minimise total bytes.

T05-E5

T05-E9-B

INFS7903 · CH06

索引与 B+ 树Indexes and B+ trees

学习目标与前置知识Learning objectives and prerequisites

学完本章，你应能从数据布局和查询条件选择索引，计算索引页数与访问次数，逐步画出 B+ 树插入和范围查询，并用执行计划检验索引是否真正被使用。前置知识是第5章的块、记录、取整与随机 I/O，以及第2章的查询条件。

After this chapter, you should be able to choose indexes from data layout and query predicates, calculate index pages and access counts, trace B+ tree insertion and range searches, and check actual index use in an execution plan. Prerequisites are blocks, records, rounding and random I/O from Chapter 5, and query predicates from Chapter 2.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week06-Indexing.pdf · PDF p. 3-15（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

索引解决的核心问题The problem an index solves

数据库索引（database index）是一条从搜索键（search key）到数据位置的访问路径（access path）。应用说“找 SSN=某值”，磁盘需要块地址；索引把两者连接起来。搜索键不一定唯一，也不一定是主键。例如姓氏可以作为搜索键，多个人可以同姓。

A database index is an access path from a search key to data locations. An application asks for a particular SSN, whereas the disk needs a block address; the index connects the two. A search key need not be unique or be a primary key. A surname can be a search key even when several people share it.

把索引想成按词排序的书后索引：先找词，再按页码取正文。数据库中的索引项通常包含键值和指针。索引记录比完整数据行短，同一页能放更多项，因此在索引中搜索往往比直接搜索数据文件更便宜。但找到索引项通常还要读数据页，计算时不能漏掉最后这一步。

Think of a book index: locate a sorted term, then use its page number to fetch the text. A database index entry normally contains a key value and a pointer. Entries are shorter than full records, so more fit on a page and searching the index is often cheaper than searching the data file. Finding an index entry usually still requires a data-page read; that final step belongs in the calculation.

索引也有代价。插入、删除或改变被索引列时，DBMS 必须维护索引；页可能分裂，日志也会增加。索引占用磁盘和缓存。它是否值得建立，取决于查询频率、过滤强度、写入频率与维护成本，而不是“列越多索引越好”。

Indexes also have costs. Inserting, deleting or changing indexed values requires maintenance, can split pages and generates additional logging. Indexes occupy disk and cache space. Their value depends on query frequency, filtering strength, write frequency and maintenance cost; adding more indexes is not an unconditional improvement.

物理顺序决定稀疏还是稠密Physical order determines sparse versus dense indexing

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week06-Indexing.pdf · PDF p. 13-22（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
索引 / Index数据文件与索引项 / Layout and entries适用与限制 / Uses and limits

主索引

Primary index

按唯一排序键有序；通常每数据块一个锚点。

Ordered on a unique key; normally one anchor per data block.

稀疏；定位候选块，再在块内搜索。

Sparse; locate a candidate block, then search inside it.

聚簇索引

Clustering index

按非唯一字段分组有序；每不同值指向首块。

Ordered by a non unique field; one entry per distinct value points to its first block.

适合同值多行与连续读取；需要维护物理分组。

Useful for groups and contiguous reads; grouping requires maintenance.

辅助索引

Secondary index

文件未按该搜索字段排序；必须覆盖每行或每键的记录指针集合。

File not ordered on this field; cover every row or each key’s record-pointer list.

稠密；可在不同字段建立多个访问路径。

Dense; multiple fields can provide alternative access paths.

稀疏索引（sparse index）能省空间，是因为中间没有列出的键仍在可推断的连续区间中。例如锚点 10、40、70 分别指向三块，找键55时，选择40所在块。若数据任意分散，未列出的55可能在任何块，稀疏索引就无法保证找到它。稠密索引（dense index）必须保留足够的信息覆盖所有搜索值；非唯一键可以指向一串记录地址，而不必机械地重复存放同一个键。

A sparse index saves space because omitted keys remain in infer able contiguous intervals. If anchors 10, 40 and 70 point to three blocks, key 55 belongs in the block anchored by 40. With arbitrarily scattered records, 55 could be anywhere, so sparse entries cannot guarantee retrieval. A dense index retains enough information to cover all search values; a non unique key may point to a list of record addresses instead of repeatedly storing the same key.

注意课程术语与产品术语的差别：这里的“主索引”指有序文件上的稀疏结构，不能直接等同 PostgreSQL 的主键索引。PostgreSQL 的 PRIMARY KEY 自动建立唯一 B-tree 索引，却不会让 heap 文件从此永久按主键排序。索引定义等元数据存于系统目录，索引节点本身占用独立存储页。

Distinguish course terminology from product terminology. Here, a primary index means a sparse structure over an ordered file; it is not synonymous with a PostgreSQL primary-key index. PostgreSQL automatically creates a unique B-tree index for a PRIMARY KEY, but does not thereby keep the heap permanently sorted by that key. Index definitions and other metadata reside in the catalogue, while index nodes occupy their own storage pages.

从记录大小到多层访问成本From record size to multi level access cost

来源 / Source: tut/Tut06-Index-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） Ex1–2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

先统一符号。B 是每块字节数，R 是每数据记录字节数，N 是数据记录数，V 是索引键字节数，P 是指针字节数。数据不跨块且忽略额外页头时，bfr=⌊B/R⌋，b=⌈N/bfr⌉。索引项大小 Rᵢ=V+P，索引块因子 f=⌊B/Rᵢ⌋。先向下取整得到容量，再向上取整得到所需块数。

First fix the notation. B is bytes per block, R bytes per data record, N data records, V bytes per index key and P bytes per pointer. For unspanned records and ignored page headers, bfr=⌊B/R⌋ and b=⌈N/bfr⌉. An index entry has size Rᵢ=V+P, giving index blocking factor f=⌊B/Rᵢ⌋. Round capacity down first, then round the required block count up.

bfr = ⌊B/R⌋　　b = ⌈N/bfr⌉　　Rᵢ = V + P　　f = ⌊B/Rᵢ⌋

Tutorial 6 Ex1 的 R=115，B=512，N=30,000，因此 bfr=4、b=7,500。SSN 与指针分别9和6字节，f=⌊512/15⌋=34。有序唯一键的主索引是7,500项，需⌈7500/34⌉=221块；无序文件上的辅助索引是30,000项，需⌈30000/34⌉=883块。同一个搜索键，物理布局不同，索引大小就不同。

In Tutorial 6 Ex 1, R=115, B=512 and N=30,000, giving bfr=4 and b=7,500. SSN and pointer sizes are 9 and 6 bytes, so f=⌊512/15⌋=34. A primary index on the ordered unique key has 7,500 entries and needs ⌈7500/34⌉=221 blocks. A secondary index over the unordered file has 30,000 entries and needs ⌈30000/34⌉=883 blocks. The same search key produces different index sizes under different physical layouts.

聚簇字段有1,000个不同值，每值30行。第一层索引1,000项占30块，第二层为30个下层块建30项，占1块，合计31个索引块、2层。每组30行按每块4行装，需要⌈30/4⌉=8块；查找并取全组的成本为2+8=10块。这里“每组在新块开始”使数据文件至少需要1,000×8=8,000块，不能再把最初的7,500块当成分组后的精确大小。

The clustering field has 1,000 distinct values and 30 rows per value. The first level has 1,000 entries in 30 blocks; the second indexes those 30 blocks and occupies one block, for 31 index blocks and two levels. Each group needs ⌈30/4⌉=8 data blocks, giving 2+8=10 reads to locate and retrieve a whole group. Because every group starts a new block, the grouped data file needs at least 1,000×8=8,000 blocks; the original 7,500 is not its exact post-clustering size.

对多层索引，最稳妥的方法是逐层取整：b₁=⌈Nᵢ/f⌉，b₂=⌈b₁/f⌉，一直到1。总空间是各层之和；路径访问只读每层一个块。不能把“总索引块数”误当成“一次搜索读取块数”。树高的对数估计还依赖扇出、占用率、根的特例及是否缓存根页。

For multi level indexes, round at each level: b₁=⌈Nᵢ/f⌉, b₂=⌈b₁/f⌉, continuing to one. Total space is the sum across levels, whereas a search reads one block per level. Do not confuse all index blocks with the blocks read by one search. Logarithmic height estimates also depend on fan out, occupancy, root exceptions and root-page caching.

Ex2 的课程答案是：辅助索引100块，二分查找⌈log₂100⌉=7次，再读数据块，共8次；B+ 树按半满有效容量50估计，⌈log₅₀10000⌉=3层，再读数据块，共4次。后者是题解的简化估计，不是给定字节数即可推出的严格最大高度定理：真实内部节点有 p 个树指针、p−1个键，叶还需记录指针与后继指针。作答时先说明沿用课程半满模型。

The course answer to Ex 2 gives 100 secondary-index blocks, ⌈log₂100⌉=7 binary-search reads and one data read, totalling eight. It estimates the B+ tree with effective half-full capacity 50, giving ⌈log₅₀10000⌉=3 levels plus one data read, totalling four. The latter is a simplified course estimate, not a strict maximum-height theorem determined by those byte sizes alone: internal nodes contain p child pointers and p−1 keys, while leaves need record and sibling pointers. State explicitly when using the course half-full model.

B+ 树：把定位与数据指针分开B+ trees separate navigation from data pointers

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week06-Indexing.pdf · PDF p. 30-45（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

B+ 树（B+ tree）的内部节点保存分隔键和子节点指针，真正指向记录的指针集中在叶。所有叶深度相同，避免某个键落在特别长的路径中。叶之间有后继指针，范围查询只需定位起点，再顺着叶链扫描。B-tree 在内部节点也可能保存数据指针；B+ 树中命中内部键后仍须向下走到叶。

A B+ tree stores separator keys and child pointers in internal nodes, with record pointers concentrated in leaves. All leaves have equal depth, avoiding unusually long paths for some keys. Sibling pointers connect the leaves, so a range query locates its starting leaf and then follows the chain. A B-tree can also store data pointers internally; in a B+ tree, matching an internal key still requires descending to a leaf.

阶（order）p 在本课程指内部节点最多 p 个孩子，因此最多 p−1 个分隔键。不同教材对“阶”和叶容量的定义可能不同，必须先读题。本章 p=3 动画沿用课程插入图：叶最多2项；溢出3项时，左叶留2项、右叶1项；分隔键是左子树最大值，等于分隔键时走左边。Tutorial 6 Ex4–5 的另一组图采用右子树最小值作分隔键，等值走右边。两种约定都可正确，不能在同一棵树中混用。

In this course, order p means at most p children per internal node and therefore at most p−1 separators. Textbooks can define order and leaf capacity differently, so read the question’s convention first. The p=3 animation follows the course insertion figures: leaves hold at most two entries; three entries split into two on the left and one on the right; separators are left-subtree maxima, with equality going left. The other figures in Tutorial 6 Ex 4–5 use right-subtree minima, with equality going right. Either convention works, but a single tree must use one consistently.

插入先按搜索规则找到叶，保持键有序地加入。叶满时分裂，并把边界键复制到父节点，键仍留在叶。父节点若溢出，也分裂；内部分隔键的提升通常是移走，不是额外保留同一个内部项。根分裂创建新根，树高只在这时增加。插入成本通常与树高成正比，最坏会沿路径发生多次分裂。

Insertion descends to the correct leaf and inserts in sorted order. An overflowing leaf splits and copies its boundary key into the parent while retaining the key in a leaf. An overflowing parent also splits; promoting an internal separator normally removes it from the split internal node rather than retaining an extra internal copy. Splitting the root creates a new root, which is when height increases. Insertion cost is normally proportional to height, with splits potentially propagating along the path.

上表是动画的静态等价说明，可用于打印学习。检查不变量：所有键在叶上恰好出现一次；叶从左到右有序；每条根到叶路径等长；每个内部节点的孩子数比分隔键数多1。原答案图个别位置的“10*”与插入序列不符，应按20核对，不能凭图片笔误加入未插入的10。

The table is the animation’s static equivalent for printed study. Check the in variants: each inserted key occurs exactly once in the leaves, leaves are ordered left to right, every root-to-leaf path has equal length, and each internal node has one more child than separator. A “10*” in part of the original answer diagram conflicts with the insertion sequence and should be checked as 20; a typographical error must not introduce an un inserted key.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut06-Index-2026.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Ex3 course answer diagram · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

删除找到叶后移除键。若节点低于题定最低占用，先尝试向相邻兄弟借项并更新父分隔键；不能借时合并，并从父删除一个分隔键。合并可能向上传播；根只剩一个孩子时可降低树高。课程 Week06 pp43–45 的删除8、15、12演示中，删除12触发重新分配或合并，因此答案可以因合法策略不同而有不同形状。验证不变量比记住单一图形更可靠。

Deletion removes a key from its leaf. If occupancy drops below the stated minimum, first try redistribution with a sibling and update the parent separator; otherwise merge and remove a parent separator. Merging can propagate upwards, and a root with one remaining child can shrink the tree. In Week 06 pp 43–45, deleting 8, 15 and 12 eventually requires redistribution or merging, so different permitted policies can yield different shapes. Validate the in variants instead of memo rising a single drawing.

哈希、位图与组合条件Hashing, bitmaps and combined predicates

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week06-Indexing.pdf · PDF p. 23-29（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

哈希索引（hash index）用 h(k) 找桶，适合等值查找，碰撞与溢出链会增加读取。它不保留键顺序，因此 k>某值不能沿相邻桶得到有序范围。位图索引（bitmap index）为每个不同值保留一条位向量，第 i 位表示第 i 行是否符合该值；多个条件可做位运算。低不同值数量、读多写少的分析场景较适合。

A hash index maps h (k) to a bucket and suits equality lookups, although collisions and overflow chains increase reads. It does not preserve key order, so adjacent buckets do not form an ordered range for k>some value. A bitmap index stores a bit vector for each distinct value, with bit i indicating whether row i has that value. Conditions combine through bit operations. Low-distinct-value analytical columns with few updates are suitable candidates.

dept=A: 111000　　mgr=QS: 101000　　chdr=N: 011101
AND → 001000 → 第3行 / row 3

上式对应课程位图例子：三个条件同时为1的只有第3行。不要把 PostgreSQL 执行计划里的 Bitmap Index Scan / Bitmap Heap Scan 等同于持久化的专用位图索引：它也可从普通索引动态构建记录位置位图。EMPNO 这种近乎每行不同的列若每值一条长位图，空间代价很大；B-tree 更自然。

In the course bitmap example, only row 3 has a one in all three vectors. A PostgreSQL Bitmap Index Scan or Bitmap Heap Scan is not the same as a dedicated persistent bitmap index: it can construct a bitmap of record locations dynamically from ordinary indexes. For EMPNO, where almost every row has a distinct value, a long bitmap per value is costly; a B-tree is a more natural choice.

Practical 5：从建立索引到解释证据Practical 5: from index creation to evidence

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac05-v2.pdf · PDF p. 3-11（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

前提是课程 prep-5.sql 已建立 EMP 100,000行与 PROJECT 200,000行；本地资料未包含该准备脚本，因此下列课程输出是原资料记录，本次没有在课程 PostgreSQL 实例重跑。先核对 \d emp、\d project 和行数，开启 \timing，运行 EXISTS 计数，建立 empno_idx，再运行同一查询。比较应同时查看结果、计划与多次耗时。课程计数180,000保持不变，48.228ms与47.200ms只说明该次测量差异很小。

The prerequisite is the course prep-5.sql data: 100,000 EMP rows and 200,000 PROJECT rows. That preparation script is absent locally, so the following outputs are records from the source, not reruns on the course PostgreSQL instance. Inspect \d emp, \d project and row counts, enable \timing, run the EXISTS count, create empno_idx, then repeat the same query. Compare results, plans and repeated timings. The course count remains 180,000; 48.228 ms versus 47.200 ms only shows a small difference in those measurements.

\timing on
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PROJECT p
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM EMP e WHERE e.EMPNO = p.PROJECTLEADER);
CREATE INDEX empno_idx ON EMP(EMPNO);
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PROJECT p
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM EMP e WHERE e.EMPNO = p.PROJECTLEADER);
SELECT indexname, indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE tablename='emp';
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgstattuple;
SELECT tree_level, leaf_pages, index_size FROM pgstatindex('empno_idx');
\timing off

pgstatindex 的 tree_level=1 通常表示根在叶之上一层，根到叶需2个索引页，不能不看定义就当成“树只有1页”。扩展可能需要权限。删除普通索引用 DROP INDEX；约束依赖的索引需通过恰当的约束操作管理。Practical 随后删除 empno_idx、改变 EMPNO 类型并添加主键，以观察自动产生的唯一索引。操作会改变实验对象，应在练习副本中依次执行。

A pgstatindex tree_level of 1 normally means the root is one level above the leaves, giving two index pages on a root-to-leaf path; it does not mean the tree has only one page. The extension may require permission. Drop an ordinary index with DROP INDEX; manage a constraint-dependent index through the appropriate constraint operation. The practical then drops empno_idx, changes EMPNO’s type and adds a primary key to observe its automatic unique index. These operations change the experiment’s objects and belong in a practice copy, executed in sequence.

函数索引部分要求查询与索引使用相同表达式。对列建立普通索引并不自动等价于索引 f(列)。课程用 IMMUTABLE 包装 TO_CHAR：这只是向优化器作出“相同输入永远同一结果”的声明，不会使原本依赖环境的函数自动变成纯函数。应验证区域设置、格式与函数行为；教学补充中可优先用确实不可变的 lower(text) 例子。

The function-index section requires the query and index to use the same expression. Indexing a column does not automatically index f (column). The course wraps TO_CHAR in an IMMUTABLE function: this asserts that identical inputs always give identical outputs, but does not magically make an environment-dependent function pure. Check locale, formatting and function behaviour. For a teaching supplement, lower (text) provides a clearer example with an appropriately immutable text operation.

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac05-v2.pdf · PDF p. 8-10（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

必要课外核对：PostgreSQL 17 的表达式索引文档说明了 lower(col) 查询与对应索引的关系，并指出写入时需维护表达式值。这里用固定版本说明软件规则，未确认课程服务器实际版本。

Necessary external check: the PostgreSQL 17 expression-index documentation explains the relationship between lower (col) queries and their matching indexes, and the maintenance required during writes. This fixed version documents the software rule; the actual course-server version has not been verified.

PostgreSQL 17 · Indexes on Expressions

常见错误、总结与自测Common errors, summary and self-check

本章的关键联系是：第5章给出每页能放多少内容，第6章用更短的索引项降低定位成本，第9章决定某次查询是否值得走这条路径。常见错误包括忘记最终数据页、把总索引空间当成路径成本、把搜索键当主键、把索引默认当有利、混用分隔键边界。

The connection is that Chapter 5 determines page capacity, Chapter 6 uses short index entries to reduce navigation cost, and Chapter 9 decides whether a query should use that path. Common mistakes are omitting the final data page, confusing total index space with path cost, equating a search key with a primary key, assuming every index helps, and mixing separator conventions.

C6-S1

自编练习：12,000行、每数据块6行、每索引块40项。比较稀疏主索引和稠密辅助索引的大小。

A file has 12,000 rows, 6 rows per data block and 40 index entries per block. Compare sparse-primary and dense-secondary index sizes.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

数据块=12000/6=2000。主索引2000项，50块；辅助索引12000项，300块。若继续加多层，主索引50→2→1，共53块、3层；辅助索引300→8→1，共309块、3层。空间不同不必然导致高度不同。

Data blocks=12000/6=2000. The primary index has 2000 entries in 50 blocks; the secondary has 12000 entries in 300 blocks. Extending upwards gives 50→2→1, totalling 53 blocks and three levels for the primary; 300→8→1 gives 309 blocks and three levels for the secondary. Different storage sizes need not imply different heights.

C6-S2

自编练习：为什么返回全表90%记录的查询走索引可能更慢？

Why can a query returning 90% of a table be slower with an index?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

需要遍历大量索引项，再访问几乎全部数据页；非聚簇情况下读取可能分散。顺序扫描一次读取全表可能更便宜。判断依据是匹配比例、物理局部性、缓存及计划测量，不能只看是否有索引。

It traverses many index entries and then touches almost all data pages, potentially with scattered reads for an unclustered index. One sequential scan can be cheaper. Assess the match fraction, physical locality, cache and measured plan rather than index existence alone.

T06-E5-B

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

I1→I3→L8。L8原[98,99,100,105]加入109后5项，按题规拆L8[98,99]与新L9[100,105,109]，把右叶最小100复制入I3。I3由[90,98]变[90,98,100]未满，I1不变，树高不变。新叶链L8→L9，不能把100从叶删除。

Follow I 1→I3→L8. Insert 109 into[98,99,100,105], then split into old L8[98,99] and new L9[100,105,109]. Copy the new right minimum 100 into I 3, making[90,98,100]. I 3 still fits, so I 1 and height stay unchanged. Link L8→L9; retain 100 in the leaf.

INFS7903 · CH07

关系代数与查询表达Relational algebra and query formulation

学习目标与运算闭包Learning objectives and relational closure

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 3-11（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是把自然语言查询改写成关系代数（relational algebra，RA），逐步解释每个中间关系的模式与含义，并检查等价变换是否丢失必要信息。前置是集合、布尔逻辑、键与SQL连接。关系运算的输入与输出都是关系，所以一个运算的结果可以继续成为下一个输入，这就是运算闭包性质。

The goal is to translate natural-language queries into relational algebra (RA), explain each intermediate relation’s schema and meaning, and check whether transformations lose needed information. Prerequisites are sets, Boolean logic, keys and SQL joins. Relational operators take relations and return relations, so one result becomes the next input: the closure property.

SQL通常声明“要什么”，RA表达式给出高层的操作组合，但仍未指定磁盘如何扫描、连接用哪种物理算法。第8章把逻辑运算变成具体实现。经典RA采用集合语义，投影去重；SQL默认袋语义（bag semantics）可保留重复，比较SQL与RA时须显式说明这一点。

SQL usually declares what is wanted, while RA specifies a high-level composition of operations. It still does not specify disk scans or a physical join algorithm; Chapter 8 supplies those implementations. Classical RA has set semantics and projection removes duplicates. SQL normally has bag semantics and can retain duplicates, so state this difference when comparing them.

选择、投影与重命名Selection, projection and renaming

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 12-22（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

选择（selection）σ条件(R)保留满足条件的行，列不变；投影（projection）π属性(R)保留指定列并消除重复，行数不会增加。RA的σ对应SQL的WHERE，而不是SQL SELECT关键字；SQL SELECT列列表更接近π。重命名（rename）ρ给关系或属性新名，尤其用于自连接与集合兼容。

Selection σcondition (R) retains qualifying rows without changing columns. Projection πattributes (R) retains columns and removes duplicates, so it cannot increase row count. RA selection corresponds to SQL WHERE, not the SQL SELECT keyword; the SQL SELECT list is closer to projection. Rename ρ gives a relation or attributes new names, especially for self-joins and set compatibility.

T ← σDno=5 ∧ Salary>30000(EMPLOYEE)
RESULT ← πFname,Lname(T)

例如员工John与Joyce在部门5，Alice在部门4，选择部门5后只剩前两人；若仅投影Dno，两行都变成(5)，集合投影只保留一个。把πDno提前到σSalary>30000之前会丢掉Salary，后面条件无法计算。每次操作后写出剩余属性，是发现这类错误最有效的方法。

Suppose John and Joyce work in department 5 and Alice in department 4. Selecting department 5 retains the first two. Projecting only Dno maps both to (5), leaving one tuple under set projection. Moving πDno before σSalary>30000 removes Salary and makes the predicate impossible to evaluate. Writing each intermediate schema is an effective way to detect this mistake.

集合操作、兼容与布尔逻辑Set operations, compatibility and Boolean logic

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 24-39（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

并（union）R∪S是任一集合中的行，交（intersection）R∩S是两者都有，差（difference）R−S只在R中。三者要求并相容（union compatibility）：属性数量相同，对应域相容，必要时先重命名。员工项目表和家属表不能直接并，但各自投影ESSN后可以并或交。

Union R∪S contains tuples in either relation, intersection R∩S those in both, and difference R−S those only in R. They require union compatibility: equal arity and compatible corresponding domains, with renaming when needed. WORKS_ON and DEPENDENT cannot be directly union ed, but their ESSN projections can be union ed or intersected.

工作或有家属 / works or has dependants:　πESSN(WORKS_ON) ∪ πESSN(DEPENDENT)
两者都有 / both:　πESSN(WORKS_ON) ∩ πESSN(DEPENDENT)
有家属但无工作 / dependants but no project:　πESSN(DEPENDENT) − πESSN(WORKS_ON)

并、交可交换且可结合，差通常不可交换也不可结合。布尔AND是同时满足，OR是至少一个满足；不能把OR筛选拆成串联σ，因为串联表示AND。De Morgan规则¬(P∧Q)=¬P∨¬Q，¬(P∨Q)=¬P∧¬Q。对“既不在Brisbane，也不归部门4”的项目，正确条件是Plocation≠Brisbane AND Dnum≠4。

Union and intersection are commutative and associative; difference generally is neither. Boolean AND requires both conditions and OR at least one. A disjunction cannot become successive selections because successive selections mean AND. De Morgan’s laws are ¬(P∧Q)=¬P∨¬Q and ¬(P∨Q)=¬P∧¬Q. Projects neither located in Brisbane nor controlled by department 4 require Plocation≠Brisbane AND Dnum≠4.

乘积、连接与自连接Products, joins and self-joins

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 40-55（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

笛卡尔积（Cartesian product）R×S生成所有配对，若各r、s行则有rs行，列数相加。θ连接（theta join）R⋈θS相当于σθ(R×S)，θ可以是=、<等比较；等值连接（equi-join）只使用相等条件。自然连接（natural join）自动在全部同名列相等处匹配，并只留一份公共列。若没有同名列，自然连接变成乘积，不会猜到Dno应该等于Dnumber。

The Cartesian product R×S forms every pair: r and s input rows yield rs rows and the combined arity. A theta join R⋈θS equals σθ(R×S), with comparisons such as = or <; an equi-join uses equality only. A natural join matches on all identically named columns and keeps one copy of each common column. With no shared names it becomes a product; it does not infer that Dno should match Dnumber.

自连接（self-join）要区分“员工角色”和“经理角色”。先把EMPLOYEE重命名为E与M，然后按E.SuperSSN=M.SSN连接。若直接自然连接同一个模式，所有同名列都会被限制相等，结果不再是经理关系。查询部门经理要按D.MgrSSN=E.SSN，而不是E.Dno=D.Dnumber，后者得到部门所有员工。

A self-join distinguishes employee and manager roles. Rename EMPLOYEE as E and M, then join on E.SuperSSN=M.SSN. Naturally joining identical schemas constrains every shared column to equality and does not express supervision. To find department managers, join D.MgrSSN=E.SSN; E. Dno=D.Dnumber retrieves all departmental employees instead.

πE.Fname,E.Lname,M.Fname,M.Lname(ρE(EMPLOYEE) ⋈E.SuperSSN=M.SSN ρM(EMPLOYEE))

内连接（inner join）丢弃没有匹配的行。左外连接（left outer join）保留左侧全部行，右侧缺失属性补NULL；右外连接反向保留。想保留没有经理的员工，可把员工放左侧做左外连接；不能在后面WHERE M.Salary>某值而又期待无经理行保留，因为NULL比较会把它们滤掉。需要区分ON中的匹配限制与WHERE的最终筛选。

An inner join discards unmatched rows. A left outer join retains every left row, filling missing right attributes with NULL; a right outer join reverses the retained side. To retain employees without managers, place employees on the left of a left join. A later WHERE M. Salary>some value removes the NULL-extended rows, so distinguish ON match conditions from final WHERE filtering.

除法与“全部”的反例法Division and the counterexample interpretation of every

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 56-63（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

除法（division）R(Y,X)÷S(X)返回这样的Y：对S中的每一个X，都有(Y,X)出现在R。它检验集合包含，不是简单相等，也允许候选人多做项目。一个完整例子：R={(Ana,P1),(Ana,P2),(Bo,P1)}，S={P1,P2}，结果只有Ana，因为Bo缺(P2)。

Division R(Y, X)÷S(X) returns Y values paired in R with every X in S. It tests containment rather than exact equality and permits extra projects. For R={(Ana, P1),(Ana, P2),(Bo, P1)} and S={P1, P2}, only Ana qualifies because Bo lacks P2.

T₁ ← πY(R)
Missing ← (T₁ × S) − R
Bad ← πY(Missing)
Result ← T₁ − Bad

推导顺序是先列候选人，再列每个候选人应该具备的全部组合，减去已做到的组合，剩下的就是反例。投影反例中的Y得到失败者，最后从候选人集合删掉失败者。这个表达式说明{σ,π,∪,−,×}足以构成核心RA运算；连接可由乘积加选择表达，交可由R−(R−S)表达，通常还需用ρ管理名字。

The construction first lists candidates, then every candidate–requirement pair, subtracts pairs actually present and identifies counterexamples. Project their Y values to obtain failures, then remove them from the candidates. This explains the expressive completeness of the core operators {σ,π,∪,−,×}: joins use product plus selection, and intersection is R−(R−S), with ρ normally available to manage names.

交互：找出缺失组合Interactive: identify missing pairs

边界是候选人来源。标准上式从πY(R)取候选人，若要求集合S为空，返回πY(R)。如果业务候选人是所有EMPLOYEE，包括从没出现在WORKS_ON中的人，应把T₁定义成完整员工集合，然后按缺失配对法判断。这样才能与第2章从EMPLOYEE出发的双重NOT EXISTS在空目标时对应。

The candidate source is a boundary condition. The standard construction takes candidates from πY(R), so an empty S returns πY(R). If business candidates include every EMPLOYEE, even those absent from WORKS_ON, define T₁ as the complete employee set before testing missing pairs. That matches Chapter 2’s double NOT EXISTS rooted in EMPLOYEE when the required set is empty.

完整查询的构造与检查Constructing and checking a complete query

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week07-RA(1).pdf · PDF p. 65-71（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

查“Research部门员工姓名和地址”：先σDname=Research(DEPARTMENT)，按Dnumber=Dno连EMPLOYEE，再πFname,Lname,Address。查“做部门5全部项目”：先投影部门5的PNUMBER并重命名PNO，作为除数；从WORKS_ON取ESSN,PNO为被除数；得到员工键后再连接姓名。先得到键再附加显示属性，能避免姓名重复和集合不相容问题。

For names and addresses of Research employees, select Dname=Research from DEPARTMENT, join EMPLOYEE on Dnumber=Dno and project names and address. For employees on every department-5 project, project the selected PNUMBER values and rename them PNO as the divisor; use WORKS_ON’s ESSN, PNO as the dividend. Join the resulting employee keys back to names. Deriving keys before display attributes avoids duplicate-name and compatibility problems.

解题检查四项：每个条件引用的列是否仍存在；每条连接是否对应真实关系；集合操作两侧是否相容；结果一行的含义是否符合题意。课程pp13–71共有27个编号例题，已在课堂题库全部登记并提供表达式；Tutorial 7另按原题登记，包括标为skip的三道聚合题，补充解法会明确标注扩展运算。

Check four things: whether each predicate’s columns remain available, whether each join follows an intended relationship, whether set operands are compatible and whether output rows represent the required unit. The 27 numbered lecture examples on pp 13–71 are registered with expressions in the classroom bank. Tutorial 7 is indexed separately, including the three skipped aggregate tasks, whose supplementary answers explicitly use extended algebra.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

RA是贯穿SQL语义和优化的共同语言。选择减行、投影减列与重复，连接组合相关事实，差与除处理否定和全称要求。写长式子前先写中间关系的自然语言含义，再核对模式，通常比直接拼符号更容易正确。

RA connects SQL semantics with optimisation. Selection reduces rows, projection reduces columns and duplicates, joins combine related facts, and difference and division express negation and universal requirements. Explain intermediate relations in words and check their schemas before composing a long expression.

C7-S1

自编练习：πA(σB>0(R))能否变成σB>0(πA(R))？

Can πA(σB>0(R)) become σB>0(πA(R))?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

一般不能，右式先丢掉B，条件无从求值。安全的下推保留B：πA(σB>0(πA,B(R)))。优化不能以丢失后续需要的信息为代价。

Generally not: the right side removes B before evaluating the predicate. A safe push down retains it: πA(σB>0(πA, B(R))). Optimisation must preserve information needed by later operators.

C7-S2

自编练习：如何表达没有家属的员工？

How do you express employees who have no dependants?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

先把EMPLOYEE.SSN与DEPENDENT.ESSN重命名为同名键，求所有员工键减有家属员工键，再连回EMPLOYEE投影姓名。不能用“连接后筛家属名不等于某值”代替不存在判断。

Rename EMPLOYEE.SSN and DEPENDENT.ESSN to a common key, subtract employees with dependants from all employee keys, then join back to names. A joined-row predicate saying a dependant name differs from some value does not express nonexistence.

Use COMPANY on p1. Let E=EMPLOYEE, W=WORKS_ON, P=PROJECT, D=DEPARTMENT, L=DEPT_LOCATIONS, N=DEPENDENT. Rename intermediate attributes withρwhen needed. Results use set semantics without a guaranteed order.

T07-E1-a

找部门5、在ProductX每周超过10小时的员工姓名。

Retrieve names of department 5 employees working more than 10 hours/week on ProductX.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

先选ProductX，按PNUMBER=PNO连W，筛Hours>10，再按ESSN=SSN连E并筛Dno=5。输出John Smith与Joyce English；Franklin没有做项目1，不能因他是经理就加入。

Select ProductX, join W on PNUMBER=PNO, filter Hours>10, join E on ESSN=SSN and filter Dno=5. Output John Smith and Joyce English. Franklin is not assigned to project 1; management alone does not qualify.

πFname,Lname(σDno=5 ∧ Hours>10(E ⋈SSN=ESSN W ⋈PNO=PNUMBER σPname='ProductX'(P)))

T07-E1-c

找由Franklin Wong直接管理的员工。

Find employees directly supervised by Franklin Wong.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

先把Franklin的SSN抽为BossSSN，和E.SuperSSN匹配，结果John Smith、Ramesh Narayan、Joyce English。只要直接下属，不递归找下属的下属。

B(BossSSN) ← πSSN(σFname='Franklin' ∧ Lname='Wong'(E))
RESULT ← πFname,Lname(E ⋈SuperSSN=BossSSN B)

T07-E1-e

找参与所有项目的员工。

Retrieve employees who work on every project.

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

W投影(ESSN,PNO)除以P的项目号集合（重命名PNO）。所得SSN再连E。6个项目无人全覆盖，因此空。可以做额外项目，但必须不缺任何要求项目。

T07-E1-f

找未参与任何项目的员工。

Retrieve employees who do not work on any project.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

所有SSN减去W出现的ESSN（同名），再取姓名。即使James工时NULL，他仍有项目20的关联行，算参与，因此空。

RESULT ← πFname,Lname(E ⋈ NonWorking)

T07-E1-i

找参与Houston项目、但所属部门没有Houston地点的员工姓名和地址。

Retrieve names and addresses of employees on a Houston project whose department has no Houston location.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

先求做Houston项目的员工键；再求全部部门号减有Houston地点的部门号，并连E取该部门员工；两个员工键集合取交。结果Jennifer Wallace，291 Berry,Bellaire,TX。写“某部门地点不等于Houston”不等于该部门完全没有Houston地点，因为部门5有多个地点。

Find employees on Houston projects, then departments with no Houston location using set difference, and intersect employee keys. Output Jennifer Wallace,291 Berry, Bell aire, TX.A department having a non-Houston location does not imply no Houston location: department 5 has several.

H(SSN) ← πESSN(W ⋈PNO=PNUMBER σPlocation='Houston'(P))
NoH ← πDnumber(D) − πDnumber(σDlocation='Houston'(L))
A ← πSSN(E ⋈Dno=Dnumber NoH)
RESULT ← πFname,Lname,Address(E ⋈ (H ∩ A))

Ex2访问路径参数Ex 2 access-path parameters

R(A,B,C)有5,000,000行，每页10行，共500,000页；按唯一A有序，A为0…4,999,999。比较直接有序文件和A上的B+树，未给具体树高与缓存。

R(A, B, C)has 5,000,000 rows, ten per page, so 500,000 pages. It is sorted on unique A, with values 0…4,999,999. Compare direct access with an A B+tree; exact height and caching are unspecified.

T07-E2-A

A≤50,000时直接读有序文件还是B+树更高效？

Which is likely more efficient for σA≤50,000(R): direct sorted-file access or B+tree?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

从文件开头读至50000所在页即可，50,001行占5001页。树索引还要额外定位，再读同一前缀，通常稍贵。这里不是任意小范围，而是从已知最小值开始的前缀，不能只见范围就必选索引。

Read the sorted prefix from the beginning:50,001 rows occupy 5001 pages. The index adds positioning before reading essentially that same prefix, so direct access is slightly preferable. This is a prefix from the known minimum, not an arbitrary small interval.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut07-RA-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） E2-A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T07-E2-B

50,000≤A<50,010时选哪种？

Which is likely better for σA≥50,000 and A<50,010(R)?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

10个连续值，按对齐页可落1页；直接文件二分约19次定位，再读范围，B+树通常路径更短，再读目标页。没有树高只能定性说通常更优，不能编造精确I/O差值。

Ten consecutive values can occupy one aligned page. Direct binary search across 500,000 pages needs about 19 probes, whereas a B+tree usually has a shorter path before fetching the range. Without its height, no exact I/O saving is justified.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut07-RA-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） E2-B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T07-E2-C

A≠50,000时选哪种？

Which is likely better for σA≠50,000(R)?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附推导和边界核对 / Course answer with derivation and boundary checks

返回4,999,999行，几乎全表；直接扫描500,000页并跳过该行。索引定位再扫不减少数据页，额外路径通常无利。若是覆盖索引且只要A可能不同，但题目要R全部属性，不能擅改。

It returns 4,999,999 rows, nearly the whole relation. Scan 500,000 pages and omit the row. Index positioning does not reduce data reads. A covering index for A-only output would be a different question; the prompt requests R.

INFS7903 · CH08

查询处理与成本模型Query processing and cost models

学习目标：逻辑操作怎样执行Learning objectives: implementing logical operators

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week08-QPO-01(1).pdf · PDF p. 3-12（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是解释SQL从解析到执行的路径，比较选择、投影与连接的实现，估计中间结果大小并计算给定成本模型。前置是第5–7章的块、索引和RA。查询执行计划（query execution plan，QEP）把逻辑需求组织成可执行操作树；叶是访问基表，父节点消费孩子输出，通常从下向上理解。

The goal is to explain parsing through execution, compare implementations of selection, projection and join, estimate intermediate sizes and calculate a stated cost model. Prerequisites are blocks, indexes and RA from Chapters 5–7. A query execution plan (QEP) organises logical requirements into executable operations. Leaves access base relations and parents consume child output, so read the tree from the bottom upwards.

解析器检查SQL语法与名称，语义分析连接目录中的列、类型和权限；重写展开视图；规划器选择顺序、访问路径与连接算法；执行器产生结果。目录中的行数、键、外键、索引和分布估计影响规划。逻辑等价不等于代价相同：同样答案可先过滤或先连接、走索引或全扫。

The parser checks syntax and names, semantic analysis resolves columns, types and privileges through the catalogue, rewriting expands views, the planner chooses ordering, access paths and join methods, and the executor produces results. Row counts, keys, foreign keys, indexes and distribution estimates affect planning. Logically equivalent plans need not have equal cost: they can filter before joining, join first, use an index or scan.

选择与投影的实现条件Implementing selection and projection

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week08-QPO-01(1).pdf · PDF p. 11-13（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

没有合适访问路径时，选择需检查全表；按有序键可二分定位，索引可直接找候选位置，哈希适合等值。多条件AND可以先用一个强过滤索引再检查其他条件，也可组合索引或交集记录位置。OR常需把各分支结果并起来并处理重复，不能用AND式串联筛选替代。

Without a suitable access path, selection examines the table. Ordered keys permit binary positioning, indexes identify candidate locations and hashing suits equality. Conjunctive predicates can use one restrictive index followed by residual checks, a composite index or intersection of record locations. Disjunction often combines branch results with duplicate handling; it cannot be implemented as successive AND filters.

经典投影需要去重，可排序后线性扫描相邻值，也可哈希去重。课程采用排序模型P(N)=N(1+log N)：N log N为排序，N为扫描。若已排序，或数据库仅取列而不DISTINCT，费用不同。投影还减少每行宽度，即使行数不变也可能节省内存、临时文件和传输。

Classical projection removes duplicates, using sorting followed by adjacent-value elimination or hash-based deduplication. The course uses P(N)=N(1+log N): N log N for sorting and N for scanning. Already sorted input or SQL column selection without DISTINCT has different cost. Projection also narrows rows, saving memory, temporary storage and transfer even when cardinality stays unchanged.

三种连接算法与逐步示例Three join methods and a stepwise example

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week08-QPO-01(1).pdf · PDF p. 14-23（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

嵌套循环连接（nested-loop join）对R每行遍历S，朴素比较次数约N×M，适用于任意连接谓词，但大输入很贵。块式嵌套循环一次把若干外表页留内存再扫描内表，减少重复I/O。教学补充：若内存有K页，可用近似I/O bR+⌈bR/(K−2)⌉bS，预留内表与输出缓冲；这是另一单位的模型，不能与课件元组成本直接相加。

A nested-loop join scans S for each row of R, making roughly N×M comparisons in its simplest form. It supports arbitrary predicates but is costly for large inputs. Block nested loops retain several outer pages before scanning the inner input, reducing repeated I/O. Teaching supplement: with K buffer pages, approximate I/O by b R+⌈b R/(K−2)⌉b S, reserving inner and output buffers. This page-cost model has different units from the lecture’s tuple model and must not be added directly to it.

课程单循环连接（single-loop join）实际使用内表连接字段索引：对每个外表键probe一次内表。成本约为扫描外表+外表行数×每次索引查找与结果读取。外表较小常有利，但内表有无索引、键重复和匹配行是否聚簇同样重要。“只用一个循环”并不说明内部查找免费。

The course’s single-loop join probes an inner index on the join field for each outer key. Its cost is approximately the outer scan plus outer row count times indexed lookup and result fetching. A small outer input often helps, but inner indexing, duplicate keys and clustering also matter. One explicit loop does not make the inner search free.

排序合并连接（sort-merge join）先按连接键排序，比较当前键：左小则推进左，右小推进右，相等则输出匹配。输入已排序时合并近似线性。课程无重复例子R=[1,3,4,5]、S=[2,3,5,6]，依次跳过1、2，输出3，再跳4，输出5，结束。对重复键必须输出整组笛卡尔配对，例如两边各两个3要输出4行。

A sort-merge join first sorts both join keys, then advances the side with the smaller current key and emits matching pairs on equality. Already sorted inputs merge approximately linearly. In the course’s unique-key example R=[1,3,4,5], S=[2,3,5,6], skip 1 and 2, emit 3, skip 4 and emit 5. Duplicate groups require every pairing: two rows with key 3 on each side produce four joined rows.

交互：合并连接与重复键组（教学补充）Interactive: merge join and duplicate groups (teaching supplement)

阶段 / Phase状态与输出 / State and output
1

r=1<s=2，推进R。

Advance R.

2

r=3>s=2，推进S。

Advance S.

3

r=s=3：R组2行、S组2行，输出4行。

Two rows per group yield four matches.

4

跳到下一不同键，任一输入结束时停止。

Advance past the groups; stop when either input ends.

教学补充：哈希连接（hash join）按等值键建立较小输入的哈希表，再扫描较大输入probe。若内存不足可分区并写临时文件，因此不是所有场景都一次线性完成。非等值条件如E.deptno>D.deptno一般不能只靠普通等值哈希直接实现，Prac6因此展示嵌套循环。物理算法选择依赖连接条件和可用排序、索引与内存。

Teaching supplement: a hash join builds a hash table on the smaller equality-key input and probes it while scanning the larger input. Insufficient memory can require partitioning and temporary writes, so it is not always one linear pass. A non-equality predicate such as E.deptno>D.deptno cannot generally use an ordinary equality hash join directly, explaining Prac 6’s nested loop. Method selection depends on predicates and available order, indexes and memory.

模式约束决定结果基数Schema constraints determine cardinality

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

关系基数（cardinality）|R|是行数，属性的NDV是不同值数，两者要分开。选择行数在0与|R|之间；πA后的不同值数至多min(|R|,NDV(A))；乘积是|R||S|。若子表外键非空并引用唯一存在父键，子表⋈父表恰好每个子行配一个父行，结果等于子表行数。外键可空或父表先被过滤时则不能保证。

Relation cardinality |R| is a row count, whereas an attribute’s NDV counts distinct values. Selection yields between zero and |R| rows; projection onto A yields at most min (|R|, NDV(A)); a product yields |R||S|. If a non-null child foreign key references an existing unique parent key, each child joins exactly once and the result equals the child count. Nullable references or a filtered parent invalidate that guarantee.

Tutorial 8 Ex3只让依据模式与基数求界：11行Course_Taken筛Spring12，范围0–11，不能偷用给出的实际值算7。Course_Taken按非空有效Course_No外键连接Course，行数恰好11，列数4+3−1=6。把“选择后的确切数”“统计估计”“结构上保证的上下界”分开，是这题真正考查的能力。

Tutorial 8 Ex 3 asks for bounds based on schemas and counts: selecting Spring 12 from eleven Course_Taken rows gives 0–11, rather than using the actual values to calculate seven. Joining Course through its valid non-null Course_No foreign key gives exactly eleven rows and arity 4+3−1=6. Distinguishing exact evaluation, statistical estimates and structural bounds is the skill being tested.

完整成本比较与课件复算A complete cost comparison and re calculation

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week08-QPO-01(1).pdf · PDF p. 25-30（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

本段固定使用课件公式并统一log₂：S(N)=N，P(N)=N(1+log₂N)，J(N,M)=P(N)+P(M)。它们是抽象运算单位，不是毫秒，也没有计算缓存和索引。设Student=25,000、Enrolment=100,000、Course=4,000，5%选课记录Grade=7；课程文字偶写“5%的学生”，这里按筛选Enrolment行解释。

This comparison fixes the lecture formulas with log₂ throughout: S(N)=N, P(N)=N(1+log₂N), and J(N, M)=P(N)+P(M). Units are abstract operations, not milliseconds, and omit caching and indexes. Let Student have 25,000 rows, Enrolment 100,000 and Course 4,000, with 5% of enrolment rows having Grade=7. The slides sometimes say 5% of students; this calculation interprets the filter over Enrolment rows.

步骤 / Step原计划 / Original plan先筛选计划 / Filter-first plan
1J(25000,100000)=2,151,205.06S(100000)=100,000
2J(100000,4000)=1,812,827.18J(25000,5000)=456,679.57
3S(100000)=100,000P(5000)=66,438.56
4P(5000)=66,438.56J(5000,4000)=118,301.70

P(5000)=66,438.56

总和

4,130,470.81807,858.40

这是本次用Python实际复算的数学结果，不是数据库运行耗时。课件原计划写约4.143million，替代计划写743,445，且J(5000,4000)=52,000与其log₂公式不符。原值保留在来源图，教材按统一公式得上表。推理仍是先把100,000降到5,000，使后续连接处理更小输入。最终πTitle,Program可能去重，5,000只是课程假设的上界式估计，不应保证一定5,000个不同组合。

These mathematical values were actually recalculated in Python, not timed on a database. The lecture reports about 4.143 million and 743,445, with J(5000,4000)=52,000 inconsistent with its log₂ formula. Original values remain visible in the source figures; the table uses one consistent formula. The reasoning survives: reduce 100,000 to 5,000 before later joins. Final πTitle, Program can remove duplicates, so 5,000 is the course’s simplifying upper-bound estimate rather than a guaranteed count of distinct pairs.

比较时必须对两个计划采用同一log底数、去重假设和物理算法。若输入已经排序，不能每个连接又无条件重复收费一次排序；若采用流水线（pipelining），中间结果也不一定全部落盘。课程模型用于练习操作顺序影响，真实DBMS应阅读实际计划。

Compare plans using the same logarithm base, deduplication assumptions and physical algorithms. Already sorted inputs need not be charged for sorting again at every join, and pipe lining can avoid writing entire intermediate results. The course model teaches ordering effects; actual DBMS behaviour requires reading its plan.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

连接的结果行数由匹配语义决定，连接成本由实施方法和输入规模决定，二者不能混为一谈。外键能保证结果不膨胀，却不保证不用索引也只比较子表行数次。先估计各阶段行数与行宽，再按给定算法计算成本。

Join cardinality follows matching semantics; join cost follows implementation and input size. A foreign key can prevent result expansion without ensuring that an un indexed implementation makes only one comparison per child. Estimate row counts and widths at each stage before applying the stated cost model.

C8-S1

自编练习：500行子表连100行父表，外键有效，是否只需500次比较？

Does a500-row child joined to a100-row parent require only 500 comparisons?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

结果恰好500行，但朴素嵌套循环可能做50,000次配对检查；索引或哈希可以更有效。结果基数不能直接等同计算工作。

The result has exactly 500 rows, but a naive nested loop can test 50,000 pairs. An index or hash method can be more efficient. Output cardinality is not the same as computational work.

C8-S2

自编练习：合并连接双方各有两条相同键，为何输出四行？

Why does a merge join with two duplicate-key rows on each side output four rows?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

每条左行与每条右行都满足连接条件，共2×2。若只在相等时各推进一次，会只输出两行并遗漏合法配对。正确方法保留并处理整个重复键组。

Each left row matches each right row, giving 2×2 pairs. Advancing both pointers once per equality would emit only two and omit valid matches. Process the full duplicate-key groups.

INFS7903 · TUT08

Tutorial 8：等价变换、连接顺序与基数Tutorial 8: equivalence,join order and cardinality

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
来源 / Source: tut/Tut8-PPT.pdf · PDF p. 3-21（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex1共同模式Ex 1 shared schemas

R(A,B,C,D), S(D,E,F); ⋈ denotes natural join on D

T08-E1-A

两次投影能否化简为最后一次投影？

πA, B(πA, B, C, D(R⋈S)) = πA, B(R⋈S)

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

合法。外投影所需A,B包含在内投影A,B,C,D中，内投影去掉E,F不影响最终A,B。按集合语义重复消除也一致。

Valid. The outer attributes A, Bsurvive the inner projection. Dropping E, Fdoes not affect the final A, Bset; deduplication is consistent under set semantics.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex1A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T08-E1-B

OR选择是否可拆成三个串联选择？

σA>15 OR B<10 OR C=20(R) = σA>15(σB<10(σC=20(R)))

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

不合法。右式要求三个条件全部为真，变AND。反例A=16,B=100,C=0满足左式但右式C=20先拒绝。OR可按集合并表示σP(R)∪σQ(R)∪σT(R)。

Invalid. The right side requires all three conditions. A tuple A=16, B=100, C=0 satisfies the left but fails the first right-side Cfilter. Disjunction can be represented by a union of selected relations.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex1B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T08-E1-C

选择C<12能否放在只保留A,B的投影之后？

πA, B(σC<12(R)) = σC<12(πA, B(R))

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

不合法，右式已丢C无法求条件。保留A,B,C后先筛，再投影A,B即可。

Invalid. C is removed before evaluation. Retain A, B, C, apply the predicate, then project A, B.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex1C · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T08-E1-D

连接前可只保留最终A与F吗？

πA, F(R⋈S) = (πA(R))⋈(πF(S))

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

不合法，两侧均丢D，右式没有公共属性而变成笛卡尔积。正确下推保留A,D与D,F，再连D，最终πA,F。

Invalid. Both sides drop D, leaving no common join attribute and producing a Cartesian product. Retain A, D and D, F, join on D, and finally project A, F.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） Ex1D · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex2共同查询与统计Ex 2 query and statistics

Emp(eid INTEGER,did INTEGER,sal INTEGER,hobby CHAR(20))
Dept(did INTEGER,dname CHAR(20),floor INTEGER,phone CHAR(10))
Finance(did INTEGER,budget REAL,sales REAL,expenses REAL)
SELECT D.dname,F.budget FROM Emp E,Dept D,Finance F
WHERE E.did=D.did AND D.did=F.did AND D.floor=1 AND E.hobby='camping';

附加统计：Emp50,000行，200种均匀爱好；Dept5,000行、两层楼均匀；Finance5,000行，每部门对应财务行。可用B+索引Emp.did、Emp.sal、Dept.floor、Dept.did、Finance.did，连接方法为题中single nested loop。

Statistics: Emp 50,000 rows and 200 uniform hobbies; Dept 5,000 rows evenly across two floors; Finance 5,000 rows with one per department.B+indexes exist on Emp. did, Emp.sal, Dept.floor, Dept.did and Finance. did. The stated join method is single nested loop.

T08-E2-A

列出避免笛卡尔积的连接分组顺序。

List join orders avoiding plans that require cross-products.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

按原始显式连接图E—D—F，忽略交换左右的同构，先(E⋈D)再F，或先(D⋈F)再E。E与F没有显式连接边，所以不先直接做它们的乘积。若优化器推导E.did=F.did，可增加候选；课程此题按未推导的图只列两种。

For the explicit graph E—D—F and ignoring left/right commutations, the groupings are (E⋈D)⋈F and (D⋈F)⋈E. Do not start with an E/Fproduct. If equality inference derives E. did=F.did, more candidates arise; the course restricts this question to the original graph.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut08-QP-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-5（原资料留在本地版） E2-A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T08-E2-B

任选顺序给优化后的RA树或表达式。

For one join order, provide the RA tree or expression an optimiser would choose.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

选择下推：E先camping，D先floor1；投影保留E.did、D.did,dname、F.did,budget。先连接E,D再F，最后只输出dname,budget。中途不能删did；SQL的重复语义是否保留需按任务说明，此RA式按集合。

Push camping to E and floor 1 to D. Retain E. did, D.did, dname, and F. did, budget. Join E/Dthen F, and finally project dname, budget. Never drop did before its joins. This algebra uses set semantics; SQL multiplicity requires separate care.

E1 ← πdid(σhobby='camping'(E))
D1 ← πdid,dname(σfloor=1(D))
F1 ← πdid,budget(F)
RESULT ← πdname,budget((E1 ⋈ D1) ⋈ F1)

四表自然连接后多少行？

T08-E4-B

按给定顺序的逐步成本是什么？换序能更高或更低吗？

What is the cost step by step in the indicated join sequence? Could another sequence cost more or less?

T08-E4-C

七月购买Widget类型产品的客户类型，预计有多少不同类型？

How many customer types would you expect for customers buying a given product type (widget)in July?

Projection to distinct Type has at most 5 values. With uniform plentiful purchases, you may expect all five, but marginal NDV sdo not guarantee it; strict range 0–5. Filtered lines may be about 12 million×1/12×1/10=100000, not 100000 distinct customer types.
