INFS7903 · CH09

查询优化与执行计划Query optimisation and execution plans

学习目标与优化边界Learning objectives and optimisation boundaries

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week09-QPO-02.pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是用合法等价规则改写查询树，计算选择率和结果估计，独立解释Prac6执行计划。前置是RA、连接方法与成本单位。基于规则（rule-based）利用启发式缩小计划空间，基于成本（cost-based）比较估计代价；实际系统可以结合两者。课件“多数DBMS偏好规则而非成本”的概括不宜直接推广到PostgreSQL，其规划使用成本与统计量。

The goal is to rewrite query trees legally, calculate selectivity and result estimates, and explain Prac 6 plans independently. Prerequisites are RA, join methods and cost units. Rule-based heuristics reduce the plan space, while cost-based planning compares estimated expense; systems can combine them. The lecture’s claim that most DBMSs favour rules over costs should not be generalised to PostgreSQL, whose planning uses statistics and costs.

选择率、不同值数与分布假设Selectivity, distinct values and distribution assumptions

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week09-QPO-02.pdf · PDF p. 5-13（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

选择率（selectivity）S定义为满足条件的行数/总行数，范围[0,1]。S越小，过滤越强；口头说“highly selective”表示条件筛得少，不意味着比例S大。属性基数高通常使某个等值条件S较小，但有数据倾斜时不能只用NDV推断。统一用“选择率比例S”和“过滤能力强”能避免术语混乱。

Selectivity S is qualifying rows divided by total rows, within [0,1]. Smaller S means stronger filtering. The phrase highly selective means few matches, not a large numerical S. High NDV often implies a smaller equality fraction, but skew prevents inferring it from NDV alone. Distinguish the selectivity fraction S from filtering strength.

S = |σc(R)| / |R|　　estimated rows = |R|·S
uniform equality: S ≈ 1/NDV(A)
independent AND: S(P∧Q) ≈ S(P)S(Q)
independent OR: S(P∨Q) ≈ S(P)+S(Q)−S(P)S(Q)

假设50,000员工的200种爱好均匀分布，camping约250人；5,000部门在两层楼均匀，floor=1约2,500。若“露营爱好者”更集中在某楼，两个条件独立相乘就可能失真。直方图（histogram）记录值频率区间，常见值统计与多列统计能改善估计。估计错可让小表被当成大表或反之，导致计划选择失误。

With 50,000 employees and 200 uniformly distributed hobbies, camping matches about 250. With 5,000 departments evenly split over two floors, floor=1 matches about 2,500. If campers concentrate on one floor, independent multiplication can be wrong. Histograms describe frequency ranges, and common-value or multi column statistics improve estimates. Mis estimates can swap the apparent sizes of inputs and lead to poor plan choices.

投影与选择对低NDV的效果不同：选择一个低NDV值会保留较多行，但只投影该列并去重会得到很少行。例如100,000行CarOwner只有Yes/No，筛Yes约50,000行；投影CarOwner只有最多2行。不要因为都提到“选择性”而混用两个结论。

Selection and projection respond differently to low NDV. Selecting one low-NDV value retains many rows, whereas projecting that column with deduplication yields few. In 100,000 Car Owner rows with Yes/No, selecting Yes may retain 50,000 while projection yields at most two. Do not conflate the two conclusions because both mention selectivity.

四步查询树优化及其条件Four query-tree transformations and their conditions

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week09-QPO-02.pdf · PDF p. 14-23（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

第一步建立规范表达式π输出(σ所有条件(R×S×T))，检查每个条件的来源。第二步把只涉及单个输入的选择下推，AND可拆、OR不可随意拆。第三步把跨表相等条件与乘积组合成连接，并选择较好的连接顺序。第四步下推投影，保留最终输出列及所有后续筛选、连接、分组需要的列。

First build the canonical expression πoutput (σall predicates (R×S×T)) and identify each predicate’s inputs. Second push single-input selections down; conjunctions split, disjunctions cannot be split arbitrarily. Third replace product-plus-join predicates with joins and select a useful join order. Fourth push projections down while retaining final output columns and everything needed by later filters, joins and grouping.

以Student、Enrolment、Course为例：先σGrade=7(Enrolment)，保留StID,CrsID；Course保留Code,Title；连接CrsID=Code后保留StID,Title；Student保留ID,Program；再连StID=ID，最后πTitle,Program。若第一步把StID删掉，无法找到专业；若把CrsID删掉，无法找到课程标题。

For Student, Enrolment, Course: first select Grade=7 from Enrolment, retaining St ID, Crs ID. Retain Code, Title from Course; join Crs ID=Code, then retain St ID, Title. Retain ID, Program from Student, join St ID=ID and finally project Title, Program. Dropping St ID early loses the programme link, and dropping Crs ID loses the course-title link.

交互：筛选、连接与列保留Interactive: filtering, joining and retaining columns

树变换 / Transformation必须满足 / Required condition

σ下推

Push selection

条件仅引用该孩子拥有的列。

Predicate refers only to that child’s columns.

π下推

Push projection

保留上层用到的所有列与连接键。

Retain every later-needed column and join key.

连接换序

Reorder joins

连接语义和条件保持，外连接不能套用所有内连接规则。

Preserve predicates and semantics; outer joins have additional restrictions.

更早去重

De duplicate earlier

确认集合/袋语义以及聚合计数不会改变。

Check set/bag semantics and aggregate counts.

CNF（conjunctive normal form）是AND连接若干OR组，DNF（disjunctive normal form）是OR连接若干AND组。例如(P∨Q)∧R等于(P∧R)∨(Q∧R)，这是分配律，不是直接由De Morgan单独完成。改写用于暴露可下推条件或可分支访问路径，但DNF展开可能使表达式指数增长。

CNF is an AND of OR groups; DNF is an OR of AND groups. For example, (P∨Q)∧R equals (P∧R)∨(Q∧R) by distributivity, not by De Morgan alone. Rewriting exposes push able predicates or alternative branch access paths, but full DNF expansion can grow exponentially.

“先做最小表”只是启发式。一个较大表若有极便宜索引、现成排序或强过滤，可产生更便宜计划；连接输出规模也由键与分布决定。对外连接，把右侧WHERE条件下推到ON可能改变是否保留左侧未匹配行。必须先证明等价，再比较成本。

“Use the smallest table first” is a heuristic. A larger input can be cheaper with a useful index, existing order or restrictive predicate, and join output depends on keys and distributions. For outer joins, moving a right-side WHERE predicate into ON can change retention of unmatched left rows. Prove equivalence before comparing cost.

Practical 6：逐项读懂执行计划Practical 6: reading an execution plan field by field

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac6-V2.pdf · PDF p. 3-10（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

前提是EMPLOYEE 50,000行、DEPARTMENT 1,009行，prep-6.sql本地未找到。课程先对deptno=30做EXPLAIN，显示Seq Scan与Filter；SET enable_seqscan TO off只是提高顺序扫描的估计惩罚，没有索引时仍可出现Seq Scan。再加主键P_DEPTNO自动产生唯一索引，用EXPLAIN ANALYZE观察Index Scan。下列流程根据资料整理，尚未连接课程服务器验证。

Prerequisites are 50,000 EMPLOYEE rows and 1,009 DEPARTMENT rows; prep-6.sql is absent locally. The course first EXPLAIN s deptno=30, producing Seq Scan and Filter. SET enable_seqscan TO off penal is es sequential scans; without an alternative index they can still appear. Adding primary key P_DEPTNO creates a unique index, then EXPLAIN ANALYZE shows an Index Scan. This source-based workflow was not verified against the course server.

EXPLAIN SELECT * FROM department WHERE deptno=30;
SET enable_seqscan TO off;
ALTER TABLE department ADD CONSTRAINT p_deptno PRIMARY KEY(deptno);
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM department WHERE deptno=30;
ANALYZE department;
SET enable_seqscan TO on;

计划中的cost=a..b表示输出首行前的启动成本和输出全部行的总成本；单位是规划器的相对成本，不是毫秒。rows是节点一次执行估计输出行数，width是估计平均字节宽度。actual time才是实测时间；loops是执行次数，某些actual行数和时间按每次循环平均显示。不能把父子total cost简单相加，因为父成本包含孩子工作。

cost=a.. b gives startup cost before the first row and total cost for all rows, in planner-relative units rather than milliseconds. rows estimates output rows per execution and width estimates average bytes. actual time is measured time; loops counts executions, with some actual counts and times shown as per-loop averages. Do not simply sum parent and child total costs because parents include child work.

Index Cond是能用于索引定位的条件，Filter是访问后再筛的条件。Index Only Scan说明所需列可从索引取得，但可能仍有Heap Fetches检查MVCC可见性；Prac6示例Heap Fetches=2，并不表示索引少了所需DEPTNO列。ANALYZE更新统计，VACUUM与可见性维护是相关但不同的工作。

Index Cond identifies conditions used for index navigation, while Filter applies after accessing candidates. Index Only Scan means required columns are available from the index, but Heap Fetches may still check MVCC visibility. Prac 6’s Heap Fetches=2 does not mean DEPTNO is missing from the index. ANALYZE updates statistics; VACUUM and visibility maintenance are related but different tasks.

PostgreSQL 17 · Using EXPLAIN · Index-only scans

Prac6的非等值连接E.deptno>D.deptno用Nested Loop，内侧索引按部门范围找匹配，Memoize按重复的e.deptno缓存查找结果。删除主键后索引消失，内侧Materialize缓存整个部门输入再反复比较，Rows Removed by Join Filter很大。Materialize计划节点是该次执行中的缓存，并不是永久CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW。

Prac 6’s non-equality join E.deptno>D.deptno uses Nested Loop, with inner index range lookups and Memoize caching repeated e.deptno lookups. Dropping the primary key removes its index; an inner Materialize node then caches the department input for repeated comparisons, with many Rows Removed by Join Filter. This Materialize plan node is execution-time storage, not a persistent CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW.

实验记录应写出数据状态、索引、统计更新、会话设置、完整计划、实际行数和时间；重复执行时缓存会变化。课程不同计划约421.529ms与3301.387ms是示例观测，不保证每台机器同样比例。enable_seqscan应在结束后恢复，避免把实验惩罚留给后续题。EXPLAIN不执行普通查询，EXPLAIN ANALYZE会实际执行，修改语句需特别注意其副作用。

Record data state, indexes, statistics, session settings, full plans, actual rows and timings; repeated runs can change cache state. The course’s approximately 421.529 ms versus 3301.387 ms is an example observation, not a guaranteed ratio on every machine. Restore enable_seqscan afterwards so later tasks do not inherit the penalty. EXPLAIN does not execute an ordinary query, while EXPLAIN ANALYZE executes its statement, including modification side effects.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

优化按“含义不变—估计规模—挑选实现—对照测量”推进。常见错误是把S大说成过滤强、删掉连接键、把cost当ms、见到Index Only就认定完全不读heap、或用一次耗时推断普遍性能。Tutorial 9把这些能力组合到Customer–Invoice–LineItem–Product查询中。

Optimisation proceeds through preserved semantics, estimated sizes, chosen implementation and measured comparison. Common mistakes are treating large S as strong filtering, dropping join keys, reading cost as milliseconds, assuming Index Only never touches the heap and generalising from one timing. Tutorial 9 combines these skills in the Customer–Invoice–LineItem–Product query.

C9-S1

自编练习：百万行、NDV(type)=10，均匀时type=X预计多少行？若X实际占70%呢？

A million-row table has NDV(type)=10 under uniformity. Estimate type=X; what if X occurs in 70% of rows?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

均匀估计S=0.1、100,000行；实际S=0.7、700,000行。仅知道NDV无法看出倾斜。可能从强过滤索引计划变为扫描更便宜；需统计量验证。

Uniformity gives S=0.1 and 100,000 rows; actual S=0.7 gives 700,000. NDV alone hides skew. The preferable plan can change from indexed access to a scan, requiring statistics to assess.

C9-S2

自编练习：SET enable_seqscan=off能保证走索引吗？

Does SET enable_seqscan=off guarantee an index scan?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

不能。它抑制或惩罚顺序扫描，不创建索引，也不使不适用的索引变适用。Prac6在未建索引时仍显示高惩罚Seq Scan。最后恢复设置并解释可用路径。

No. It discourages or penal is es sequential scans without creating an index or making an inapplicable index usable. Prac 6 still shows a penal is ed Seq Scan before an index exists. Restore the setting and explain the available access paths.

INFS7903 · TUT09

Tutorial 9：四表查询的逐步优化Tutorial 9: stepwise optimisation of a four-table query

来源 / Source: tut/Tut09-Query-Op-2026-v1.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages
来源 / Source: tut/Tut9-PPT.pdf · PDF p. 6-16（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

沿用Tutorial8 Ex4四表模式与行数，本题数据年份2025，10种ProdType、5种Customer.Type。目标是七月买过Widget类型的客户类型。题目使用Invoice.Date.Month=July作为伪表达式；本教材SQL改为可执行的半开日期区间。所有统计估计基于题设均匀分布。

Use the four schemas/counts from Tutorial 8 Ex 4, but this question’s population year is 2025, with ten product types and five customer types. The target is types of customers buying Widget in July. The prompt uses Invoice. Date. Month=July as pseudo notation; this edition uses an executable half-open date interval. All estimates assume the stated uniformity.

Customer(CustID,Name,Type):10000
Invoice(InvID,CustID,Date,Amount):1200000
LineItem(InvID,LineNo,ProdID,Qty):12000000
Product(ProdID,Description,ProdType):1000

T09-E1-A

写SQL找七月购买Widget类型产品的客户类别。

Write an SQL query to determine types of customers purchasing a specific product type Widget in July.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

按三个FK连接，筛ProdType=Widget与2025年7月，再DISTINCT客户类型。课程SQL没DISTINCT，会重复输出Type；原伪日期表达式不能直接在PostgreSQL执行。用>=7月1日且<8月1日可涵盖所有7月时间，不漏7月31日稍晚时间。

Join the three FK paths, filter Widget and July 2025, and select distinct customer types. The course omits DISTINCT and can repeat Types. Its month syntax is not directly executable in PostgreSQL. Ahalf-open July 1–August 1 interval includes every July timestamp.

SELECT DISTINCT c.Type
FROM Customer c JOIN Invoice i ON i.CustID=c.CustID
JOIN LineItem l ON l.InvID=i.InvID
JOIN Product p ON p.ProdID=l.ProdID
WHERE p.ProdType='Widget'
 AND i.date>=DATE '2025-07-01' AND i.date<DATE '2025-08-01';

T09-E1-B

Customer.Type与Product.ProdType的等值选择率？

What is selectivity of Type in Customer and ProdType in Product?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

均匀时S(Type=v)=1/5=.2，S(ProdType=Widget)=1/10=.1。它是某个具体等值条件的比例，不是“列本身只有一个永恒的选择率”。本查询并未对Customer.Type筛某值，不能再给整体结果额外乘.2。

Under uniformity, S(Type=v)=1/5=.2 and S(ProdType=Widget)=1/10=.1. These are fractions for specific equality predicates, not universal properties of a column. The query does not filter one Customer. Type, so do not multiply its result by.2 again.

T09-E1-C

画查询树并逐步解释优化。

Draw a query tree and show step-by-step optimisation with explanations.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

规范树为πType(σ三连接条件∧Widget∧July(C×I×L×P))。①把Widget下推P、July下推I；②把乘积与等值条件换连接；③选I_July⋈C，再连L，再连P_Widget，或按成本选另一连接顺序；④向叶下推π：C留CustID,Type，I留InvID,CustID（筛完日期后），L留InvID,ProdID，P筛完后只留ProdID。各步都保留连接键。

Canonical:πType (σthree joins∧Widget∧July (C×I×L×P)). Push Widget to P and July to I; replace products with joins;chooseI_July⋈CthenLthenP_Widget, or another cost-justified order; push projections to leaves. Cretains CustID, Type; I retains InvID, CustID after the date filter; Lretains InvID, ProdID; Pretains ProdID after filtering. Keep every needed join key.

P1 ← πProdID(σProdType='Widget'(P))
I1 ← πInvID,CustID(σJuly2025(I))
C1 ← πCustID,Type(C)
L1 ← πInvID,ProdID(L)
R ← I1 ⋈ C1
S ← R ⋈ L1
T ← S ⋈ P1
RESULT ← πType(T)

T09-E1-D

有哪些投影可立即进行？

What projection operations occur? Can any be performed immediately?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

C可立即πCustID,Type；L可立即πInvID,ProdID。I必须先用Date筛，再πInvID,CustID；P先用ProdType筛再πProdID。若先πType(C)会丢CustID；先πInvID(L)会丢ProdID，无法筛Widget。最终πType负责去重，可在不再需要其他键后做。

Immediately project C to CustID, Type and L to InvID, ProdID. Filter Iby Date before dropping Date; filter Pby ProdType before keeping ProdID only. Projecting C only to Type or L only to InvID too early loses needed keys. FinalπTypededuplicates after all other keys become unnecessary.

T09-E1-E

哪些选择可立即做，输出约多大？

Which selections can happen immediately, and how large are their outputs?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

P上Widget：1000/10=100行；I上July：1200000/12=100000行。C和L没有本地过滤。若先I1⋈L，10明细/发票给约1million行，再P1保留约100000行；按Type去重至多5。独立分布是估计前提。

Widget on P:1000/10=100 rows; July on I:1200000/12=100000 rows. C and Lhave no local filter. Joining I 1 to Lfirst gives about 1 million lines, then P 1 about 100000; distinct Types are at most five. Independence is an estimation assumption.

T09-E1-F

讨论这些连接操作的成本及从低到高的顺序。

Discuss costs of the possible join operations in increasing order.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附教学推导与必要更正 / Course answer with derivation and necessary corrections

须先声明模型。对题解列出的三个输入，若J=N(1+log₂N)+M(1+log₂M)，J(10000,100000)<J(1000000,100)<J(100000,12000000)。若额外单独算R⋈T，J(100000,100000)介于第一与第二之间。题解某处把12000000×100000当成本，另处用J，混了nested loop与sort-merge。不同算法下不可宣称相同全局排名。也不应一边算I⋈L再算S⋈P，最后又无理由重复连接已在S中的I；上面C的线性连接树是完整可解释的一种。

State a model first. For the answer’s three inputs, with J=N(1+log₂N)+M(1+log₂M), J(10000,100000)<J(1000000,100)<J(100000,12000000). An additional R⋈Tcost J(100000,100000)falls between the first two. The reference mixes a product cost for nested loops with Jfor sort-merge; rankings are not universal across methods. Do not redundantly rejoin I without explanation after already including it in S. Part Cprovides one complete connected join tree.

INFS7903 · CH10

事务、调度与正确性Transactions, schedules and correctness

学习目标与事务的业务边界Learning objectives and transaction boundaries

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week10-TM(1).pdf · PDF p. 3-24（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是解释ACID，追踪交错读写的值，独立构造优先图并判断冲突可串行化，另外检查可恢复、无级联与严格性。前置是SQL修改、基本图论与“事务本地副本”观念。事务（transaction）是一项业务工作对应的逻辑读写单元，例如转账同时扣A、加B；只做一半会破坏业务不变量。

The goal is to explain ACID, trace values under interleaving, construct precedence graphs and test conflict serialisability, then separately test recoverability, cascadelessness and strictness. Prerequisites are SQL modifications, basic graphs and transaction-local copies. A transaction is a logical read/write unit implementing a business activity, such as debiting A and crediting B together; a partial transfer violates the business invariant.

课程订货流程把客户资格、库存检查、库存扣减、订单和应收账款更新组合为一个工作单元。崩溃可能发生在任一SQL之间，因此BEGIN/COMMIT划定整体成功边界，ROLLBACK撤销未完成的效果。提交不代表每个数据页立刻落盘，持久性也可由已持久化日志与恢复过程保证，第12章会补全。

The course ordering process combines customer eligibility, stock checking, stock reduction, ordering and accounts-receivable updates into one unit. A crash can occur between SQL statements, so BEGIN/COMMIT define all-or-nothing success and ROLLBACK removes incomplete effects. Commit does not imply that every data page immediately reaches disk; durable logging plus recovery can provide durability, as Chapter 12 explains.

属性 / Property要求 / Requirement主要机制与限制 / Mechanisms and limits

原子性

Atom i city

全部完成或全部撤销。

All effects or none.

日志与Undo；应用仍须正确划分事务。

Logging and Undo; applications must define the transaction properly.

一致性

Consistency

合法状态到合法状态。

Valid state to valid state.

声明约束加正确业务代码，DBMS不自动懂全部业务。

Constraints plus correct application logic; DBMS does not infer all business rules.

隔离性

Isolation

并发不产生不允许的相互干扰。

Concurrency avoids prohibited interference.

并发协议、隔离级别；最强目标是串行等价。

Protocols and isolation levels; serial equivalence is the strongest target.

持久性

Durability

提交后效果能经受后续失败。

Committed effects survive later failure.

持久日志、Redo与备份；不能靠RAM中的成功提示。

Durable log, Redo and backups; a success message in RAM is insufficient.

状态机中Active执行读写；最后一条操作后Partially committed仍可能因日志未持久化而失败；成功到Committed。出错转Failed，再回滚到Aborted/Terminated，按应用策略决定是否重启。不要把“执行完最后一条UPDATE”和“提交持久成功”当成同一个时刻。

In the state machine, Active performs reads and writes. After the final operation, Partially committed can still fail before durability is established; success reaches Committed. Errors move to Failed, followed by rollback to Aborted/Terminated and possible restart under application policy. Completing the last UPDATE is not the same moment as successful durable commit.

调度与本地计算：先画时间线Schedules and local computation: start with a timeline

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week10-TM(1).pdf · PDF p. 26-35（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

调度（schedule/history）是多个事务操作的总顺序，但必须保留每个事务内部的程序顺序。记rᵢ(X)、wᵢ(X)、cᵢ、aᵢ为读、写、提交、回滚。read把数据库X复制到事务自己的局部变量；中间算术只改局部副本，直到write才改数据库。混淆这两个空间会算错丢失更新。

A schedule/history is a total order of operations from several transactions that preserves each transaction’s program order. Use rᵢ(X), wᵢ(X), cᵢ, aᵢ for read, write, commit and abort. A read copies database X into the transaction’s local variable. Arithmetic changes only that copy until a write updates the database. Mixing local and shared state leads to incorrect lost-update calculations.

Tutorial 10 Ex1初值y=20，T1加50、T2乘3。串行T1→T2给(20+50)×3=210；T2→T1给20×3+50=110。原交错两者先读20，T1本地算70、T2本地算60，先写70再写60，最终60。60既不是210也不是110，T1更新被T2旧副本覆盖。

Tutorial 10 Ex 1 starts y=20, with T1 adding 50 and T2 multiplying by three. Serial T1→T2 gives (20+50)×3=210, while T2→T1 gives 20×3+50=110. In the interleaving both read 20, compute local 70 and 60, then write 70 followed by 60. The final 60 equals neither serial result: T2’s stale local copy overwrites T1’s update.

四类并发异常的本质Four concurrency anomalies

丢失更新（lost update）是一个事务根据旧值写回，覆盖另一个已做的修改。脏读（dirty read/uncommitted dependency）是读到另一个尚未提交事务的写；若写者回滚，读者依赖了不存在的效果。不可重复读（unrepeatable read）是同一事务两次读同项，中间别的事务修改，使结果不同。

A lost update occurs when a transaction writes a stale value over another transaction’s modification. A dirty read, or uncommitted dependency, reads another transaction’s uncommitted write; if the writer aborts, the reader depended on a nonexistent effect. An unrepeatable read occurs when another transaction changes an item between two reads by the same transaction.

不正确汇总（incorrect summary）是在汇总多个项目时混合了不同时间的状态。例如X=500、Y=500，转账100保持总额1000；汇总先读旧X=500，转账后再读新Y=600，得到1100。它可能没有读到脏值，却仍不是一致快照。每个单独读值都“曾经正确”并不保证整组结果一致。

An incorrect summary combines states from different times. With X=500, Y=500, transferring 100 preserves a total of 1000. A summary that reads old X=500 and later new Y=600 reports 1100. It may avoid dirty values yet still lack a consistent snapshot. Each individual value having been correct at some time does not make the combined result consistent.

串行、可串行化与冲突等价Serial, serialisable and conflict-equivalent schedules

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week10-TM(1).pdf · PDF p. 44-56（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

串行（serial）指一个事务做完再做下一个；n个不同事务有n!个串行排列。可串行化（serialisable）允许交错，但效果须与某个串行执行等价。不同串行顺序可以产生不同合法结果，如210与110；“正确”不是要求它们全部相同。一次具体初值恰好算出相同最终数值，也不证明对所有输入都等价。

Serial means completing one transaction before the next; n distinct transactions have n! serial orders. Serialisable permits interleaving whose effects are equivalent to some serial execution. Different serial orders can yield different valid results, such as 210 and 110; correctness does not require every serial order to agree. Coincidentally equal final values for one initial state do not prove equivalence for all inputs.

两操作冲突要同时满足：属于不同事务、访问同一数据项、至少一个是写。读读不冲突，同事务两操作不算跨事务冲突，不同数据项也不冲突。冲突等价（conflict equivalence）要求每一对冲突操作的相对顺序都一致；只比较最终值或无标签边集合不够。

Two operations conflict only if they belong to different transactions, access the same item and at least one writes. Read/read pairs, same-transaction pairs and different items do not form these inter transaction conflicts. Conflict equivalence requires the same relative order for every conflicting pair. Equal final values or identical unlabelled edge sets alone do not establish it.

优先图的构造、证明与拓扑序Precedence graphs, justification and topological order

优先图（precedence/serialisation graph）每个事务一个节点；若冲突操作中Ti的操作先于Tj，画Ti→Tj，并标记数据项与操作对。图无环当且仅当该调度冲突可串行化（conflict-serialisable）。理由是每条边约束串行先后，无环图有拓扑序满足全部约束；有环则要求某事务排在自己之前，无法满足。

A precedence/serialisation graph has one node per transaction. If Ti’s operation precedes Tj’s in a conflicting pair, add Ti→Tj and label the item and operation pair. A schedule is conflict-serialisable exactly when the graph is a cyclic. Every edge constrains serial order; an a cyclic graph has a topological order satisfying all constraints, while a cycle requires a transaction to precede itself.

例：r1(X),w1(X),r2(Y),w2(Y),c2,r1(Y),w1(Y),c1,r3(X),w3(X),c3。Y上T2写先于T1读写，得T2→T1；X上T1读写先于T3，得T1→T3。无环，唯一受约束顺序T2→T1→T3。若只有T1→T3和T2→T3，则T1与T2可交换，两个拓扑序都合法。

For r1(X), w1(X), r2(Y), w2(Y), c2, r1(Y), w1(Y), c1, r3(X), w3(X), c3, T2’s Y write precedes T1’s Y operations, giving T2→T1. T1’s X operations precede T3’s, giving T1→T3. The graph is a cyclic with order T2→T1→T3. If the only edges were T1→T3 and T2→T3, T1 and T2 could exchange places and both topological orders would work.

算法可逐数据项检查有序读写对，再做拓扑排序；朴素全配对O(m²)，图拓扑检测O(V+E)。一条边有多个冲突证据不影响是否有环。冲突可串行化是方便检验的充分类别，不等同所有可能的可串行化：第11章Thomas写规则会放过某些包含过时盲写的视图可串行化调度。

An algorithm can examine ordered read/write pairs per item and then topologically sort the graph. A naive all-pairs construction is O(m²), while graph detection is O(V+E). Multiple conflict witnesses on one edge do not change cyclicity. Conflict serialisability is a convenient sufficient class, not all possible serialisability: Thomas’s write rule in Chapter 11 can permit some view-serialisable histories with obsolete blind writes.

可恢复、无级联与严格性另行判断Test recoverability, cascadelessness and strictness separately

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week10-TM(1).pdf · PDF p. 36-42（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

可恢复调度（recoverable schedule）：若Tj读到了Ti写的值，Tj提交必须晚于Ti提交。关键是读自（read-from）关系，要找该次read之前最近的有效write；不是同一数据项上所有曾写过的事务都自动成为依赖。若Ti回滚，仍依赖它的Tj不能再正常提交，需要回滚或重做。

A recoverable schedule requires that if Tj reads Ti’s written value, Tj commits only after Ti commits. Identify the read-from relation by the relevant most recent preceding write, rather than treating every historical writer of the item as a dependency. If Ti aborts, a Tj still dependent on it cannot simply commit normally; it must be rolled back or re computed.

无级联调度（cascadeless schedule）要求读别人的写之前，该写者已经提交；因此不会因读脏值而级联回滚。严格调度（strict schedule）更强：别人写了X但尚未提交或回滚时，其他事务既不能读X也不能写X。于是严格⊆无级联⊆可恢复。严格排除脏写，避免回滚旧值覆盖别人的新写。

A cascadeless schedule reads another transaction’s write only after that writer commits, avoiding cascading aborts from dirty reads. A strict schedule additionally forbids other transactions from writing the item until the writer commits or aborts. Thus strict⊆cascadeless⊆recoverable. Strictness excludes dirty writes, preventing rollback of an old value from overwriting another transaction’s new write.

但这条包含链不是可串行化的包含链。例如r1(X),r2(X),w1(X),c1,w2(X),c2没有读脏值或覆盖未完成写，是严格调度；两者仍先读旧值，可能丢失更新，冲突图有T1→T2与T2→T1。Strict 2PL协议能同时保证冲突可串行化和严格性，但“严格调度”这个性质单独不能替代冲突图检查。

This inclusion chain is not a serialisability chain. For example, r1(X), r2(X), w1(X), c1, w2(X), c2 neither reads dirty values nor overwrites an unfinished write, so it is strict. Both transactions still read the old value and can lose an update, with graph edges both ways. The Strict 2 PL protocol guarantees both conflict serialisability and strictness, but strictness alone does not replace a graph test.

Tutorial 10 Ex6(A)参考答案只把c2移到a1之后，却仍保留T2读到T1脏值再提交，这并未真正修复依赖。可选修复：把a1改成c1且放c2之前；若坚持T1回滚，则让T2在它回滚后重读重算或一起回滚。不要把“提交发生在写者终止之后”误当成“写者已经提交”。

In Tutorial 10 Ex 6(A), moving c2 after a1 while retaining T2’s dirty read does not genuinely repair the dependency. One repair changes a1 to c1 before c2. If T1 must abort, T2 must reread and re compute afterwards or also abort. Committing after a writer terminates is not the same as committing after that writer commits.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-7（原资料留在本地版） Ex6(A) correction · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

总结、英文论证与自测Summary, English reasoning and self-check

判断顺序：先检查事务内部顺序合法；再追踪值与异常；再逐对构造冲突图；另列read-from与commit/abort检查恢复性质。英文可写：“The edge T1→T2 is caused by w1(X) preceding r2(X). Since the graph is acyclic, the schedule is conflict-serialisable in the order …”。图有环时应精确说“not conflict-serialisable”，不要在没有进一步依据时排除更宽的等价类别。

Check program order first, trace values and anomalies, build conflicts pair by pair, then separately examine read-from and commit/abort order. A useful English justification is: “The edge T1→T2 is caused by w1(X) preceding r2(X). Since the graph is a cyclic, the schedule is conflict-serialisable in the order …”. With a cycle, state “not conflict-serialisable”; broader equivalence categories need further analysis.

C10-S1

自编练习：判断w1(X),r2(X),c1,c2的可恢复、无级联与严格性。

Classify w1(X), r2(X), c1, c2 for recoverability, cascadelessness and strictness.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

T2读自T1，c1早于c2，所以可恢复；r2发生在c1之前，所以不无级联，也不严格。若把c1移到r2之前，该数据项上的三项性质都满足。

T2 reads from T1 and c1 precedes c2, so it is recoverable. r2 precedes c1, so it is neither cascadeless nor strict. Moving c1 before r2 satisfies all three properties for this item.

C10-S2

自编练习：相同的优先图边集合能否证明冲突等价？

Can identical precedence edge sets prove conflict equivalence?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

不能。边可由多对操作产生，图压缩了各操作对顺序。Tutorial10的S3与S4都双向有边，却有某些冲突对先后不同。必须检查每一对冲突操作。

No. Many operation pairs can produce the same edge, and the graph compresses their individual order. Tutorial 10 S3 and S4 both have edges in both directions but differ on some conflicting pairs. Check every conflicting pair.

INFS7903 · TUT10

Tutorial 10：全部事务调度题Tutorial 10: every transaction-schedule exercise

Ex1给定调度Ex 1 given schedule

initial y=20
1:r1(y);2:r2(y);3:T1 y:=y+50;4:T2 y:=y*3;5:w1(y);6:w2(y)

T10-E1-A

两个串行顺序分别得到什么y？

What are correct y values under serial T1 followed by T2 and T2 followed by T1?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1→T2：(20+50)×3=210。T2→T1：20×3+50=110。顺序影响最终值，但两者都符合相应串行语义。

T1→T2:(20+50)×3=210.T2→T1:20×3+50=110. Both are valid serial outcomes despite differing values.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-7（原资料留在本地版） E1-A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E1-B

原交错时刻7的y是多少？

What is actual y at time 7?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

两事务各读20；本地分别70和60，写顺序70后60，数据库最终60。第10章逐步表展示共享与局部值。

Both read 20, compute local 70 and 60, and write 70 then 60. The final database value is 60. Chapter 10 traces shared and local state separately.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-7（原资料留在本地版） E1-B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E1-C

发生什么异常，为什么？

What concurrency anomaly occurred? Explain.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

丢失更新。T2按旧20计算并写60，覆盖T1写入70；最终60不等于任何串行结果。不是只看最后一个事务完成就认为正确。

Lost update: T2 uses stale 20 and writes 60 over T1’s70. The result matches neither serial outcome. The last writer’s completion does not establish correctness.

Ex2事务与四个序列Ex 2 transactions and four schedules

T1:r1(A);A:=A+100;w1(A);r1(B);B:=B+100;w1(B)
T2:r2(A);A:=A*2;w2(A);r2(B);B:=B*2;w2(B)
S1:r1(A);w1(A);r1(B);w1(B);r2(A);w2(A);r2(B);w2(B)
S2:r1(A);w1(A);r2(A);w2(A);r1(B);w1(B);r2(B);w2(B)
S3:r1(A);w1(A);r2(A);w2(A);r2(B);w2(B);r1(B);w1(B)
S4:r2(A);w2(A);r1(A);r2(B);w1(A);r1(B);w2(B);w1(B)

T10-E2-A

哪些调度冲突可串行化？

Which schedules are conflict serialisable?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

S1与S2在A、B上只有T1→T2，均可串行化，S1还本来就是串行。S3在A为T1→T2、B为T2→T1，有环。S4在A有T2→T1；B上r1(B)先w2(B)给T1→T2，同时w2(B)先w1(B)给T2→T1，有环。这里检验冲突可串行化，不涉及提交恢复性。

S1/S2 have only T1→T2 on A and B, and are conflict-serialisable; S1 is already serial. S3 has T1→T2 on A and T2→T1 on B, a cycle. S4 has T2→T1 on A; on B, r1 before w2 gives T1→T2 and w2 before w1 gives T2→T1, a cycle. This tests conflicts rather than commit recoverability.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-7（原资料留在本地版） E2-A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E2-B

哪些调度优先图相同？是否冲突等价？

Are there schedules with the same precedence graphs (conflict equivalent)?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

S1与S2所有冲突对顺序一致，冲突等价，等价串行T1→T2。S3与S4无标签图都双向有边，但r1(B)/w2(B)在S3是w2先，在S4是r1先，故不冲突等价。题目括号把“图相同”与“冲突等价”等同不严谨，图压缩了每对操作证据。

S1 and S2 preserve every conflicting pair and are conflict-equivalent to T1→T2. S3 and S4 share bidirectional unlabelled graphs but differ on r1(B)/w2(B): w2 first in S3, r1 first in S4. So they are not conflict-equivalent. The question’s parenthesis equating graph equality with conflict equivalence is imprecise.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-7（原资料留在本地版） E2-B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E3-S1

用给定数值检查Ex3 Schedule1及异常。

Check Schedule 1 for serialisability and identify any problems, using the given values.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1和T2都在对方写前读Y=30，各自按X=80算110。T2先wY、T1后wY，最终110而顺序做两次加80应190，丢失T2更新；Y上r1→w2给T1→T2，r2→w1给反向，非冲突可串行化。T2另将Z=56+12=68。图中T2算Y+X却未单列read(X)，此数值演示按给定X=80处理，缺列不影响Y冲突结论。

Both transactions read Y=30 before the other writes and each computes 110 using X=80. T2 writes first and T1 overwrites it, result 110 instead of serial 190, losing T2’s update. Yconflicts give edges both ways. T2 also produces Z=68. The figure uses Xin T2’s arithmetic without a separate read; the numeric trace uses given X=80, and the Ycycle is unaffected.

来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 2-4（原资料留在本地版） E3-S1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E3-S2

检查Ex3 Schedule2。

Check Schedule 2 for serialisability and problems.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1把Y30→42后，T2读42并加M=8得到50，随后T1回滚，T2依赖脏值。课程称“not serializable”，但按图列出的读写冲突只要求T1→T2，图无环；真正问题是脏依赖与恢复，不能由脏读直接推出有冲突环。T2需回滚/重算；T1的X:=X+N没有write(X)且回滚，不能把92当持久结果。

T1 writes Y 42, T2 reads it and adds M 8 to obtain 50, then T1 aborts. T2 has a dirty dependency. The course calls it nonserialisable, but the listed read/write conflicts only give T1→T2, an a cyclic graph. The issue is dirty dependency and recovery, not proof of a conflict cycle. T2 must abort/re compute.T1’s local X:=X+N has no write and aborts, so 92 is not a durable X.

T10-E3-S3

检查Ex3 Schedule3。

Check Schedule 3 for serialisability and problems.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1只写Y，T2只读写Z，跨事务没有同项写冲突。图无边，任意串行顺序都等价；Y=30+80=110，Z=56+12=68，X=80。读写不同对象不因时间交错自动产生异常。

T1 writes Ywhile T2 accesses Z, so there are no inter transaction same-item write conflicts. The graph has no edges and either serial order works. Y=110, Z=68, X=80. Interleaving different objects is not itself an anomaly.

T10-E4-S1

画Ex4 Schedule1优先图并判断。

Draw the precedence graph of Ex 4 Schedule 1 and test conflict serialisability.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

X上r1先w3给1→3，r2先w3给2→3；Q上r1先w2给1→2，r2/w2先w1给2→1；Z上T3读写先T2读写给3→2。存在1↔2、2↔3等环，所以非冲突可串行化。应从图中的实际操作顺序提取边，不因算式里的未读变量另造操作。

On X, r1 before w3 gives 1→3 and r2 before w3 gives 2→3. On Q, r1 before w2 gives 1→2, and r2/w2 before w1 gives 2→1. On Z, T3 accesses precede T2’s, giving 3→2. Cycles include 1↔2 and 2↔3, so it is not conflict-serialisable. Use the drawn operation order rather than inventing reads from arithmetic variables.

T10-E4-S2

画Ex4 Schedule2的图。

Draw the precedence graph of Schedule 2.

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

共享项Y上T2先写后T1读写，边T2→T1，没有反向。T1读X、T2改Z没有跨事务同项冲突。因此冲突可串行化为T2→T1。

On shared Y, T2 writes before T1 reads/writes, giving T2→T1 with no reverse edge.T1’s Xread and T2’s Zwrites do not conflict. The schedule is conflict-serialisable as T2→T1.

T10-E4-S3

画Ex4 Schedule3的图。

Draw the precedence graph of Schedule 3.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

两事务都先读Y，随后图中T1先写Y、T2再写Y。r2(Y)先w1(Y)给2→1，r1(Y)先w2(Y)给1→2，w1先w2也给1→2；有环。课程文字某些写对顺序与图不一致，但有环结论相同；图中实际顺序优先。

Ex5程序顺序Ex 5 program order

T1:r1(Y);Y:=Y-K;w1(Y);r1(X);X:=X+K;w1(X);c1
T2:r2(X);X:=X+J;w2(X);c2

下面省略算术行，但读写与提交顺序保留；算术应放在相应read与write之间。分类可以重叠，不要求每例仅满足一个标签。Serial本身不可能是交错的，原题统一称interleaved时存在措辞张力。

Arithmetic is omitted below but belongs between its read and write. Program and commit order are retained. Categories overlap and need not be exclusive. Serial is necessarily non-interleaved, despite the prompt’s general interleaved wording.

T10-E5-A

给串行例子。

Give a serial schedule.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

先T2完整结束，再T1。无交错，因此也满足可串行化。

Execute all of T2 then T1. No interleaving; also serialisable.

r2(X);w2(X);c2;r1(Y);w1(Y);r1(X);w1(X);c1;

T10-E5-B

给交错但可串行化例子。

Give an interleaved serialisable schedule.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1先做Y，再T2完整处理X，T1最后做X。唯一X边2→1，等价串行T2→T1，Y提前不影响T2。

T1 handles Y, then T2 completes X, then T1 handles X. The only Xedge is 2→1, equivalent to T2→T1; moving Yearlier does not affect T2.

r1(Y);w1(Y);r2(X);w2(X);c2;r1(X);w1(X);c1;

T10-E5-C

给可恢复调度，可包含脏读。

Give a recoverable schedule.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T1读T2未提交写，因此依赖T2，但c2早于c1，满足可恢复。它不无级联，因为r1(X)早于c2；若T2回滚，T1也需回滚。

T1 reads T2’s uncommitted write, butc 2 precedes c1, sothe schedule is recoverable. It is not cascadeless because r1(X)precedes c2; if T2 aborts, T1 must too.

r1(Y);w1(Y);r2(X);w2(X);r1(X);w1(X);c2;c1;

T10-E5-D

给无级联例子。

Give a cascadeless schedule.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

把c2放在r1(X)之前，所有跨事务读取只来自已提交写。此例也严格，可恢复。

Placec 2 before r1(X), so all cross-transaction reads use committed writes. This example is also strict and recoverable.

r1(Y);w1(Y);r2(X);w2(X);c2;r1(X);w1(X);c1;

T10-E5-E

给严格例子。

Give a strict schedule.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T2写X到c2之间没有T1读或写X，故严格。严格比无级联还禁止脏写，但在本两个事务都先读再写的例子里可使用同一序列。

Between T2’s Xwrite and c2, T1 neither reads nor writes X, so strictness holds. Strictness additionally prevents dirty writes, but the same example can illustrate both properties here.

r1(Y);w1(Y);r2(X);w2(X);c2;r1(X);w1(X);c1;
来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-6（原资料留在本地版） E5-E · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E6-A

S1可恢复吗？若否修改：r1(X);w1(X);r2(X);r1(Y);w2(X);w1(Y);c2;a1。

Is S1 recoverable? If not, repair it: r1(X);w1(X);r2(X);r1(Y);w2(X);w1(Y);c2;a1.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

不可恢复，T2读T1后在T1回滚前提交。下列保留a1，把T2读写移到回滚后；此时读的是已恢复X，可正常提交。另一种改变业务结局的修复是把a1改c1并放c2之前。课程把c2移到a1后但不重读，仍保留脏依赖，不是充分修复。

Not recoverable: T2 reads T1 then commits before T1 aborts. The repair below preserves a 1 and moves T2 after rollback, so it reads restored X. Another repair changes a 1 to c1 before c2, changing the outcome. Movingc 2 after a 1 without rereading leaves the dirty dependency and is insufficient.

r1(X);w1(X);r1(Y);w1(Y);a1;r2(X);w2(X);c2;
来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-7（原资料留在本地版） E6-A · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E6-B

S2是否无级联？修改该调度。

Is S2 cascadeless? Repair: r1(X);w1(X);r4(X);r1(Y);w4(X);w1(Y);a1;a4.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

r4(X)读T1未提交写，所以不是。让T1完成回滚后T4才读X，保留每事务内部顺序。T4本来就回滚，不会让此例自动无级联，性质检查读发生的时刻。

r4(X)reads T1’s uncommitted write, sothe schedule is not cascadeless. Move T4’s read after T1 rollback while preserving each program order.T4’s later abort does not erase the dirty-read occurrence.

r1(X);w1(X);r1(Y);w1(Y);a1;r4(X);w4(X);a4;
来源 / Source: tut/Tut10-TM-2026.pdf · PDF p. 3-7（原资料留在本地版） E6-B · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

T10-E6-C

S3中T3写5、T4盲写6，T3回滚会丢6。它是否无级联，怎样修？

S3:r3(X);w3(X);w4(X);a3;c4. T3 writes 5, T4 overwrites 6. Is it cascadeless? Repair the lost 6 problem.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

无级联，因为没有别的事务读未提交写；但不严格，因为T4写了T3未完成的数据项。把a3放w4前，先撤销T3，再写6，避免Undo旧值覆盖T4。不能把“没有脏读”当成“没有恢复问题”。

It is cascadeless because no other transaction reads an uncommitted write, but not strict because T4 overwrites an unfinished writer. Movea 3 before w4: undo T3 first, then write 6. Do not equate absence of dirty reads with absence of recovery hazards.

r3(X);w3(X);a3;w4(X);c4;

INFS7903 · CH11

锁、死锁与时间戳排序Locks, deadlocks and timestamp ordering

学习目标与锁的作用Learning objectives and the purpose of locking

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 3-17（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是给事务加入合适读写锁，比较四种2PL，画等待图检测死锁，并逐行执行时间戳排序。前置是第10章的冲突、图与恢复性质。锁（lock）是对数据访问权限的协调状态，DBMS锁管理器记录谁持有什么、谁在等待；锁不是把数据复制到另一个表，也不是简单看到冲突就必须终止。

The goal is to add read/write locks, compare four 2 PL variants, detect deadlocks with wait-for graphs and execute timestamp ordering stepwise. Prerequisites are conflicts, graphs and recoverability from Chapter 10. A lock coordinates access permissions; the lock manager records holders and waiters. Locking does not copy data into another table, nor does every conflict require immediate abortion.

共享锁（shared/read lock，S）允许多个事务同时读；排他锁（exclusive/write lock，X）只允许一个事务访问受保护项以修改。S与S兼容，S与X、X与X不兼容。持X通常已足够读，无需再申请S。S升级X时必须等其他读者释放；若两个读者都想升级，可能互等。

A shared/read lock (S) permits concurrent readers. An exclusive/write lock (X) grants one transaction modification access. S is compatible with S, but S/X and X/X are incompatible. An X holder can normally read without requesting S. Upgrading S to X must wait for other readers, and two upgrading readers can wait for one another.

已持有 / Held请求S / Request S请求X / Request X
S

允许

Grant

等待

Wait

X

等待

Wait

等待

Wait

仅在每次读写前加锁、事后随手解锁仍可能不串行化。例如T1读完X就解锁，T2改X，T1之后又获取Y锁继续依赖旧状态，可能形成冲突环。协议的关键是约束加锁与解锁的相对位置，而非只有“存在锁”。

Locking before each operation and casually unlocking afterwards can still produce nonserialisable schedules. If T1 releases X after reading, T2 changes it, and T1 later acquires Y while relying on old state, a conflict cycle can arise. The protocol must constrain relative lock and unlock positions; lock existence alone is insufficient.

两阶段锁：增长、锁点与收缩Two-phase locking: growth, lock point and shrinkage

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 18-24（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

两阶段锁（two-phase locking，2PL）把每个事务分成增长期和收缩期：第一次释放锁之前可申请/升级，之后只能释放/降级，不能再申请新锁或升级。锁点（lock point）是获取最后一个锁的时刻。把所有事务按锁点排序，可得到它们冲突等价的串行顺序，因此2PL保证冲突可串行化。

Two-phase locking (2 PL) divides each transaction into growth and shrinkage. Before the first release it can acquire or upgrade locks; afterwards it can only release or downgrade, without new acquisitions or upgrades. The lock point is its final acquisition. Ordering transactions by lock points gives a conflict-equivalent serial order, explaining why 2 PL guarantees conflict serialisability.

锁降级X→S已释放一部分访问权，属于收缩；升级S→X属于增长。若先降级Y再获取X的锁，就违反2PL。可把X锁提前到降级前，或把降级推迟，但必须保留原读写的程序顺序。2PL只限制锁动作，不要求事务一定先把所有数据读完才开始写。

Downgrading X→S releases permission and starts shrinkage; upgrading S→X belongs to growth. Downgrading Y before acquiring X violates 2 PL. Acquire X earlier or postpone the downgrade while preserving the original read/write program order. 2 PL constrains lock actions, not a rule that all reads must precede all writes.

协议 / Protocol附加规则 / Additional rule可恢复性与死锁 / Recovery and deadlock
Basic 2PL

增长后收缩。

Growth followed by shrinkage.

保证冲突可串行化；可能脏读、级联与死锁。

Conflict-serialisable; dirty reads, cascades and deadlocks remain possible.

Conservative 2PL

开始前一次性取得全部所需锁，否则不持部分锁地等待。

Acquire all required locks at start, or wait holding none of that set.

避免死锁；需预知集合，可能降低并发。

Deadlock-free; needs advance knowledge and can reduce concurrency.

Strict 2PL

满足2PL，并持全部X锁到提交/回滚。

2 PL plus retain every X lock until commit/abort.

严格且可恢复，仍可能死锁。

Strict and recoverable, but deadlocks possible.

Rigorous 2PL

全部S与X锁都持到结束。

Retain all S and X locks to the end.

更易按提交顺序理解，但持锁时间更长；可能死锁。

Commit-order reasoning is simpler, with longer lock retention; deadlocks possible.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 35-37（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Strict与Rigorous不可混称：Strict允许某些S锁在满足2PL的条件下提前释放，Rigorous把所有锁留到结束。保守2PL消除的是持部分资源再等另一部分造成的环；若实现成逐个尝试并一直持住部分锁，就失去其“全有或全无”的保证。

Strict and Rigorous differ: Strict can release some S locks early while respecting 2 PL, whereas Rigorous retains all locks until termination. Conservative 2 PL removes cycles caused by holding partial resources while waiting for more. An implementation that incrementally acquires and retains partial sets loses its all-or-nothing guarantee.

死锁、饥饿与等待图Deadlock, starvation and wait-for graphs

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 25-39（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

死锁（deadlock）是互相等待，等待图（wait-for graph）Ti→Tj表示Ti正在等Tj释放锁；有环即有死锁。它与优先图不同：优先图边表示已经发生的冲突先后，等待图边表示当前阻塞依赖。T1先写X，T2要读X时，优先约束可能是T1→T2，而当前等待边是T2→T1。

A deadlock is circular waiting. In a wait-for graph, Ti→Tj means Ti is waiting for Tj to release a lock; a cycle indicates deadlock. A precedence graph instead describes ordering constraints from operations already performed. If T1 writes X and T2 wants to read it, the precedence constraint can be T1→T2 while the current waiting edge is T2→T1.

完整例子：T1持X(A)，T2持X(B)。T1申请X(B)被阻塞，画T1→T2；T2申请X(A)被阻塞，画T2→T1，形成环。DBMS选择牺牲者回滚，释放它的锁让另一方继续；不能假装被阻塞的写已经执行后再画图。牺牲者选择与重启时机依实现，除非有实际日志，不应断言一定回滚T1。

Worked example: T1 holds X(A), T2 holds X(B). T1 requests X(B), creating T1→T2; T2 requests X(A), creating T2→T1 and a cycle. The DBMS aborts a victim and releases its locks so another transaction can continue. Do not treat blocked writes as completed. Victim choice and restart timing are implementation-dependent; without logs, do not claim T1 must be the victim.

T1 holds A, waits B → T2
T2 holds B, waits A → T1
cycle: T1 → T2 → T1

饥饿（starvation/livelock）是某事务长期得不到资源，但系统其他事务一直在进展，不必有等待环。公平队列或提高久等事务优先级可缓解。死锁检测是出现环后选择回滚；超时可能把仅仅很慢的事务也回滚，因此超时不是严格证明死锁。统一按A再B的资源顺序申请锁则可预防这种循环，代价是较少灵活性与可能提前持锁。

Starvation/live lock means one transaction waits indefinitely while others keep progressing, without requiring a cycle. Fair queues or ageing priorities help. Deadlock detection aborts after a cycle appears; timeouts can also abort merely slow transactions and are not proof of deadlock. Requesting resources consistently in A-then-B order prevents this cycle at the cost of reduced flexibility and potentially earlier lock retention.

等待—死亡（wait-die）：老请求者遇年轻持有者则等，年轻请求者遇老持有者则回滚。伤害—等待（wound-wait）：老请求者回滚年轻持有者，年轻请求者等老持有者。两者约束等待方向所以不会形成环，重启保留原时间戳以减轻饥饿。它们是基于时间戳的死锁预防，不能和下一节完全不用等待的TO算法混为一谈。

Wait-die lets an older requester wait for a younger holder, but aborts a younger requester facing an older holder. Wound-wait lets an older requester abort a younger holder, while a younger requester waits for the older holder. Their directed waiting prevents cycles; retaining the original timestamp on restart helps avoid starvation. These timestamp-based deadlock-prevention policies differ from the non waiting TO algorithm below.

时间戳排序：每步先检查再改状态Timestamp ordering: validate before changing state

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 40-45（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

基本时间戳排序（timestamp ordering，TO）给每个事务固定TS(T)，按它规定串行顺序。每数据项X存RTS(X)，表示允许读过X的最大事务时间戳；WTS(X)表示最后允许写者的时间戳。“最后”指时间戳序，不必等于最近墙上时间。允许晚到的老读者读时，RTS取max，不能直接改成这个更小的老时间戳。

Basic timestamp ordering (TO) assigns fixed TS(T) values defining the serial order. Each item X stores RTS(X), the maximum timestamp of an allowed reader, and WTS(X), the allowed last writer’s timestamp. Last refers to timestamp order, not necessarily wall-clock arrival. When an older reader arrives later, update RTS with max rather than replacing it with the smaller timestamp.

read_T(X):
　if TS(T)<WTS(X): abort T
　else read X; RTS(X)←max(RTS(X),TS(T))
write_T(X):
　if TS(T)<RTS(X) or TS(T)<WTS(X): abort T
　else write X; WTS(X)←TS(T)

读检查WTS，因为不能让老事务看到属于未来串行位置的写。写检查RTS，因为年轻事务已经读过旧状态，老事务再写会使其读错；写也检查WTS，防止老写覆盖年轻写。事务局部计算不改变数据库值或RTS/WTS。拒绝的操作不写入；事务回滚后需按恢复机制撤销它此前已做的有效写。

A read checks WTS to prevent an older transaction seeing a write from its future serial position. A write checks RTS because a younger transaction has already read the preceding state, and checks WTS to avoid an older write replacing a newer one. Local computations change neither database values nor RTS/WTS. Rejected operations do not write; recovery must undo earlier effective writes of an aborted transaction.

交互：允许老读者，拒绝过时写者Interactive: allow an older reader and reject an obsolete writer

操作 / Operation条件 / Condition结果 / Result
r93(Y)93≥WTS(Y)=0

允许，RTS(Y)=93。

Allow; RTS(Y)=93.

r91(Y)91≥WTS(Y)=0

允许，RTS(Y)=max(93,91)=93。

Allow; RTS stays 93.

r92(Z)92≥WTS(Z)=0

允许，RTS(Z)=92。

Allow; RTS(Z)=92.

w91(Z)91

回滚T91；不更新Z。

Abort T91; do not write Z.

基本TO不会为锁等待，所以无死锁，但冲突可能反复回滚造成饥饿，而且基本规则本身不保证无级联或严格性。通常重启给新时间戳，这与wait-die/wound-wait保留老时间戳的策略不同。课程把“年轻事务优先”作为直观总结，更准确的操作依据仍是上面逐项不等式。

Basic TO does not wait for locks and is deadlock-free, but repeated aborts can starve transactions; the basic rules alone do not guarantee cascadelessness or strictness. Restart normally assigns a new timestamp, unlike wait-die/wound-wait’s retained timestamp. The course’s “younger transactions win” is intuition; the precise decisions follow the inequalities above.

Thomas写规则与被忽略的写Thomas’s write rule and ignored writes

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week11-CC.pdf · PDF p. 46-48（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Thomas写规则先检查TS(T)<RTS(X)，若是仍回滚；否则若TS(T)<WTS(X)，忽略这次已过时写；否则正常写。顺序非常重要：不能因为有更新的写就忽略一个已经破坏读者顺序的操作。它允许部分基本TO会拒绝的历史，属于更宽的视图等价讨论，不保证原始所有操作构成的冲突图都无环。

Thomas’s write rule first aborts if TS(T)<RTS(X). Otherwise, if TS(T)<WTS(X), ignore the obsolete write; otherwise execute it normally. The order matters: a newer write does not justify ignoring an operation that already violates a reader’s ordering. The rule admits histories basic TO rejects and involves broader view-equivalence reasoning; the original history’s full conflict graph need not be a cyclic.

例如r1(A),w2(A),c2,w1(A),c1，TS1<TS2：RTS(A)=TS1且WTS(A)=TS2，T1的最后写被忽略，最终保留T2值。若保留原w1画图有T1→T2与T2→T1；忽略后的实际有效写历史不再有反向覆盖。不要把“没有执行的写”仍算进最终数据库值。

For r1(A), w2(A), c2, w1(A), c1 with TS1<TS2, RTS(A)=TS1 and WTS(A)=TS2, so T1’s final write is ignored and T2’s value remains. Including the original w1 gives graph edges both ways; the effective history after ignoring it has no reverse overwrite. A write that was not executed must not affect the final database value.

与PostgreSQL实际隔离的联系Connection to PostgreSQL isolation

A2 Q4明确补充：PostgreSQL普通读基于多版本快照（MVCC），默认READ COMMITTED每条语句取新快照，不等同把每个SELECT都加课程S锁。更新仍持行锁，可能互等死锁。不要从2PL课堂规则直接推断所有PostgreSQL读写阻塞。第13章用两会话快照例子连接理论与实际。

A2 Q4 adds that ordinary PostgreSQL reads use multiversion snapshots (MVCC). Default READ COMMITTED takes a new snapshot for each statement and does not put the course’s S lock on every SELECT. Updates still hold row locks and can deadlock. Do not directly infer all PostgreSQL blocking behaviour from classroom 2 PL. Chapter 13 connects the theory through a two-session snapshot example.

来源 / Source: A2/a2-2026-v1.pdf · PDF p. 7-8（原资料留在本地版） Q4 background and experiments · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

总结与自测Summary and self-check

锁协议通过等待控制交错，TO通过拒绝违反时间戳顺序的操作控制交错。优先图判断已执行历史的冲突等价，等待图判断当前阻塞能否继续。每次解题写明使用Basic、Strict、Conservative还是Rigorous，是否允许升级/降级、是否含提交，否则同一读写序列可能有不同可行性。

Lock protocols control interleaving through waiting; TO rejects operations violating timestamp order. Precedence graphs test conflict equivalence of executed histories; wait-for graphs test current blocking. State Basic, Strict, Conservative or Rigorous, conversion rules and commit points, because feasibility can differ for the same read/write sequence.

C11-S1

自编练习：T1、T2都持S(X)，然后都请求升级X(X)，会怎样？

T1 and T2 both hold S(X), and both request X(X). What happens?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

每个升级都被另一人的共享锁阻止，形成等待环。2PL不会自动消除这种升级死锁。可通过预先申请写锁、保守全量申请或检测后回滚等策略处理。

Each upgrade is blocked by the other shared holder, creating a wait cycle. 2 PL does not automatically prevent upgrade deadlock. Options include requesting write locks initially, conservative all-at-once acquisition, or detection followed by abort.

C11-S2

自编练习：TS(T)=8，RTS(X)=10，WTS(X)=12。Thomas规则能否忽略该写而让T继续？

TS(T)=8, RTS(X)=10, WTS(X)=12. Can Thomas’s rule ignore T’s write?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

不能，先检查8<10，已违反读时间戳，必须回滚。只有TS≥RTS但TS<WTS时才允许把过时写忽略。

No. The first check finds 8<10, violating RTS, so T must abort. Ignoring an obsolete write is allowed only when TS≥RTS but TS<WTS.

INFS7903 · TUT11

Tutorial 11：锁操作与完整时间戳表Tutorial 11: lock operations and a complete timestamp table

来源 / Source: tut/Tut11-CC-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-4（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Does Basic 2 PL allow S exactly as shown? Explain.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

不能。两者read后都持S(X)。T1要升级X(X)时被T2共享锁挡住；T2不能先放锁再为w2获取X，因为放后再获违反2PL。原读写序列本身双向冲突，也不可能由2PL完整生成。

No. Both reads hold S(X), so T1’s upgrade is blocked by T2. T2 cannot release and later acquire Xfor its write without violating 2 PL. The read/write history itself has a conflict cycle and cannot be completed under 2 PL.

Does Conservative 2 PL allow S exactly as shown?

Ex2两个交错Ex 2 interleavings

A:r1(X);r1(Y);r2(Z);w1(X);r2(X);w1(Y);w2(X)
B:r2(Z);r2(X);r1(X);r1(Y);w1(X);w2(X);w1(Y)

题目开头只写对象X,Y，但T2又访问Z；按实际操作包含Z处理。原序列没有提交，Strict2PL改写必须明确插入提交点。

The introduction names X, Ybut T2 also accesses Z; treat Zas part of the actual object set. The histories omit commits, so Strict 2 PL repairs must explicitly add them.

T11-E2-A

判断A异常并给Strict2PL修复。

Identify the anomaly in schedule A and modify using Strict 2 PL.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T2在w1(X)后、T1提交前读X，可能脏读。T1的X锁保留到c1，T2的读必须等；T1读Y与写Y仍按程序顺序。下列让T2的Z读提前但X读延后。

T2 reads Xafterw 1 but before T1 commits, a possible dirty read. T1 retains X(X)to c1, blocking T2’s Xread.T1’s Yoperations retain program order. The repair lets T2 read Zearly but delays X.

r1(X);r1(Y);r2(Z);w1(X);w1(Y);c1;r2(X);w2(X);c2;

T11-E2-B

判断B异常并修复。

Identify the anomaly in schedule B and modify using Strict 2 PL.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

两者先读旧X再写，后写覆盖前写，是丢失更新，也可能脏写。T2先完成X并提交，再让T1读最新X。若双方都已拿共享锁再升级，可能死锁，修复序列可让T1在开始X读前等待而非假设升级必成功。

Both read old X before writing, so a later write loses the earlier update and can be a dirty write. Complete T2’s Xwork and commit before T1 reads it. If both first hold shared locks, upgrades can deadlock; the repaired sequence can delay T1’s initial Xread instead of assuming an upgrade succeeds.

r2(Z);r2(X);w2(X);c2;r1(X);r1(Y);w1(X);w1(Y);c1;

Ex3三个原事务Ex 3 original programs

T3:r(Z);Z:=Z+N;r(X);X:=X+Z;r(Y);Z:=Z+Y;w(Z);w(X)

T11-E5

T91<T92<T93，逐步给X,Y,Z的值与读写时间戳。

For T91<T92<T93, show X, Y, Z values and read/write timestamps after every step using basic timestamp ordering.

初始(x,W0,R0)、(y,W0,R0)、(z,W0,R0)，函数f1…f4未定义数值，答案必须保留符号。下表给全部13步；局部算术不改共享状态。t7老T91读Y允许，RTS仍93；t9读Z使RTS变92；t11的T91写因91<RTS(Z)=92被拒绝，且此前T91没有共享写需Undo。t13 T92写f4(z)允许，不能写成f4(f3(z))，因为f3从未成功写入。

Initially (x, W0, R0),(y, W0, R0),(z, W0, R0). Functions f1…f4 lack numeric definitions, so retain symbols. The table covers all 13 steps; local arithmetic leaves shared state unchanged. At t7, T91 may read Ywhile RTS stays 93; t 9 sets Z’s RTS to 92; t 11 rejects T91’s write since 91<92, and T91 has no earlier shared writes to Undo. At t13, T92 writes f4(z), notf 4(f3(z)), since f 3 never reached the database.

INFS7903 · CH12

日志、检查点与恢复Logging, checkpoints and recovery

学习目标与失败类型Learning objectives and failure types

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 4-11（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

目标是根据缓冲策略决定Undo/Redo，按日志顺序恢复值，解释WAL与检查点为何必要，并区分备份与崩溃恢复。前置是块/缓冲、第10章提交回滚及第11章严格性。事务失败通常回滚一个事务；系统崩溃丢失易失内存但磁盘仍在；介质失败可能丢磁盘内容，需要备份和保留下来的日志。不能只用内存里的日志恢复内存丢失。

The goal is to determine Undo/Redo from buffer policy, recover values in log order, explain WAL and checkpoints, and distinguish backups from crash recovery. Prerequisites are pages and buffers, commit/abort from Chapter 10 and strictness from Chapter 11. A transaction failure usually aborts one transaction; a system crash loses volatile memory while disk remains; media failure can destroy disk contents and requires backups plus surviving logs. A log existing only in lost RAM cannot recover that RAM.

缓冲管理器（buffer manager）把磁盘块复制到内存页，修改后内存页成为脏页（dirty page）。脏表示内存与磁盘版本不同，并非业务值非法。把页写回磁盘叫刷新（flush）。一个事务提交时，其数据页是否刷新，与日志是否持久化是两个独立问题；把它们分开，才能理解恢复。

The buffer manager copies disk blocks into memory pages. Modified pages are dirty: memory and disk differ, not necessarily that the business values are invalid. Writing a page back is a flush. Whether data pages flush at commit is separate from whether the log becomes durable. This separation is essential to recovery reasoning.

备份、转储与日志Backups, dumps and logs

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 12-17（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

逻辑备份（logical backup）导出逻辑对象与数据，如SQL定义和行；单独CSV只保存部分内容，不自动包含约束、函数和权限。物理备份（physical backup）复制数据库存储表示，必须满足一致性要求并配合相关日志。冷备份在停止服务时做，热备份在运行时做但需要协调机制；直接随意复制正在变化的文件不保证可恢复。

A logical backup exports logical objects and data, such as SQL definitions and rows. A CSV alone is partial and does not automatically preserve constraints, functions or privileges. A physical backup copies storage representation and must meet consistency requirements with relevant logs. Cold backups stop service; hot backups run online but require coordination. Arbitrarily copying changing database files does not guarantee recovery.

类型 / Type保存 / Contents恢复链 / Restore chain

完整

完整选定备份范围。

Entire selected backup scope.

自上次相关备份以来变化。

Changes since the preceding relevant backup.

完整+所需的连续增量链。

Full plus every required incremental in sequence.

备份是较早状态的副本；日志（log/journal）按顺序描述发生过的更新，能把恢复推进到备份之后。日志记录可包含事务ID、数据项、旧值前像（before image，BFIM）、新值后像（after image，AFIM）、开始、提交、回滚和检查点。实际系统不一定记录每次普通read，课件的read记录属于教学模型。

A backup copies an earlier state; a log/journal records ordered updates and can advance recovery beyond it. Records may include transaction ID, data item, before image (BFIM), after image (AFIM), begin, commit, abort and checkpoint. Real systems need not log every ordinary read; the lecture’s read records belong to its teaching model.

四种缓冲策略推导恢复需求Deriving recovery needs from four buffer policies

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 27-30（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

偷写（steal）允许尚未提交事务修改过的页被写回磁盘，可能因缓冲空间不足。于是崩溃时磁盘可能含失败事务效果，需要Undo。No-steal阻止这些未提交效果进入数据磁盘，所以不需要为它们做物理Undo。它说的是修改页是否可落盘，不是“把BFIM写回去”；脏页通常含修改后的值。

Steal permits pages modified by uncommitted transactions to reach disk, for example under buffer pressure. A crash can therefore leave failed effects on disk and require Undo. No-steal prevents those uncommitted effects reaching data storage, avoiding their physical Undo. The policy concerns whether modified pages may flush; it does not mean flushing BFIM, since dirty pages normally contain modified values.

强制写（force）要求事务提交时其数据修改已写到数据磁盘，所以提交效果无需Redo；No-force允许提交后部分数据仍只在缓冲中，崩溃要用Redo恢复持久结果。No-force仍必须满足WAL的日志持久性，不能把“不强制数据页”误读成“不强制日志”。

Force requires a transaction’s data updates to be on data storage at commit, avoiding Redo for those effects. No-force allows some committed data to remain buffered, requiring Redo after a crash. No-force still requires durable WAL; not forcing data pages does not mean not forcing the log.

策略 / Policy未提交页可落盘？ / Uncommitted pages may flush?提交时全写数据？ / All data forced at commit?恢复 / Recovery
Steal / No-force

No-Undo / No-Redo

No-steal/Force最容易恢复，却需要容纳未提交修改的内存且每次提交写很多数据；Steal/No-force通常更灵活，代价是恢复逻辑更复杂。恢复表由两个独立问题推出，比死背四格可靠。No-Undo/No-Redo只是在该缓冲模型下不需要这两种崩溃动作，不表示介质失败不需要备份。

No-steal/Force simplifies recovery but needs memory for uncommitted changes and many data writes at commit. Steal/No-force is more flexible but requires more complex recovery. Deriving the matrix from two independent questions is better than memo rising it. No-Undo/No-Redo only concerns these crash actions under the model, not the need for backups after media failure.

WAL：先保留恢复证据，再允许不可撤回的动作WAL: preserve recovery evidence before risky actions

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 18-20（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

预写日志（write-ahead logging，WAL）有两个核心顺序：数据页落盘前，它对应的恢复日志必须先持久化；向用户确认提交前，事务所需日志及提交记录必须持久化。第一条让未提交数据即使被偷写也能Undo，第二条让提交数据即使没写页也能Redo。日志可以顺序批量写，通常比每次提交随机写所有数据页更经济。

Write-ahead logging (WAL) enforces two orders: recovery log records must be durable before their data pages flush, and required transaction log records plus the commit record must be durable before acknowledging commit. The first enables Undo of stolen uncommitted pages; the second enables Redo of committed but un flushed data. Sequential batched log writes can be cheaper than forcing every modified data page at each commit.

原理例子：磁盘X=100，T1拟改200。先把(X,100,200)日志可靠保存，再允许X=200落盘；若T1失败，用100撤销。若T1已提交但X=200尚未落盘，重启读提交日志用200重做。如果先写页后写日志，崩溃可能既没有旧值也没有新值的可靠证据。

Example: disk X=100 and T1 proposes 200. Persist the log (X,100,200) before allowing X=200 to flush; if T1 fails, restore 100. If T1 commits but 200 has not flushed, recovery uses the committed log to redo 200. Writing the page before its log risks a crash without reliable evidence of the old or required new value.

为什么Undo反向、Redo正向Why Undo runs backwards and Redo forwards

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 23-26（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

同一事务把X从100改200再改400，撤销时必须先恢复第二次写的BFIM=200，再恢复第一次写的BFIM=100。若正向Undo，先设100再设200，错误停在中间状态。重做则先200再400，保持原先因果顺序与最后写值。读日志时要按记录顺序而非简单按事务编号排序。

If one transaction changes X from 100 to 200 to 400, Undo must first restore the second write’s BFIM=200, then the first write’s BFIM=100. Forward Undo would set 100 then 200 and leave an intermediate state. Redo applies 200 then 400, preserving update order and the final value. Follow log-record order rather than sorting only by transaction ID.

课程p26日志中T1提交，T2与T3崩溃时未提交。T2对N先20→10再10→40，应反向40→10→20；T3把Y40→50，应恢复40；T1把X100→200→400，在No-force且页未可靠落盘的条件下重做到400。Last_lsn把同事务记录连起来，便于从最后记录向前追踪Undo。

In the p26 log, T1 committed while T2 and T3 were unfinished at the crash. T2 changes N20→10→40, so Undo reverses 40→10→20. T3 changes Y40→50 and restores 40. T1 changes X100→200→400 and redoes to 400 under No-force when its page is not known durable. Last_lsn links a transaction’s records for backward Undo travers al.

检查点的保证与不能跳过的旧日志Checkpoint guarantees and old log records that still matter

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 21-22（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

检查点（checkpoint）减少恢复扫描范围，但要先读题给定保证。本课程多用简化完整刷新：暂停事务、按WAL顺序写必要日志与脏页、写检查点、继续。它不自动使活跃事务提交。真实系统可用模糊检查点（fuzzy checkpoint），不要求同时把全部脏页写完；本教材仅作必要区别，不扩展到完整ARIES。

A checkpoint reduces recovery work, but first identify its guarantees. The course mostly uses a simplified full-flush checkpoint: pause transactions, persist required logs and dirty pages in WAL order, record the checkpoint and resume. It does not commit active transactions. Real systems can use fuzzy checkpoints without flushing every dirty page together. This necessary distinction does not extend the syllabus into full ARIES.

“Undo只回到最近检查点”不能当成通则。若T4检查点前写了A=20但还没提交，完整刷新可能已把20落盘；崩溃后要Undo到15，其BFIM日志在检查点前，仍必须读取。类似地，延迟更新下T7检查点前写入日志、检查点后才提交，检查点时未提交效果不能被当成已持久化；Redo需要找到那条检查点前记录。

“Undo only back to the latest checkpoint” is not a general rule. If active T4 wrote A=20 before a full-flush checkpoint, that 20 may already be on disk; restoring 15 after a crash requires its pre-checkpoint BFIM record. Similarly, under deferred update, T7 can log a write before a checkpoint and commit afterwards. Its uncommitted effect is not durable at the checkpoint, so Redo must locate that earlier record.

延迟更新、立即更新与完整例子Deferred update, immediate update and a worked recovery

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 31-36（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

延迟更新（deferred update）在提交前不把事务效果写入数据磁盘，常按No-steal/No-force教学模型处理：失败者忽略，不做物理Undo；提交者可能需Redo。立即更新（immediate update）允许提交前写页，按Steal/No-force需Undo失败者与Redo必要提交者。不要把延迟更新等同“完全不写缓冲”或“日志无须提前保存”，具体实现与教学抽象应区分。

Deferred update prevents transaction effects reaching data storage before commit, commonly modelled as No-steal/No-force: ignore losers without physical Undo, while winners may need Redo. Immediate update permits pre-commit data writes; Steal/No-force requires Undo of losers and Redo of required winners. Deferred update does not universally mean no buffer modification or no advance durable logging; distinguish implementations from teaching abstractions.

完整例子来自p24与pp33–36：T1改D10→20并在检查点前提交；T3改B20→15、T4改A15→20后检查点；之后T2改D20→15、T5改C15→30，T3与T2提交，随后崩溃。立即更新且完整刷新时：T1无需动作；T3的B已在检查点持久化；T2可能Redo D=15；T5、T4逆日志Undo为C=15、A=15。

Worked example from p24 and pp 33–36: T1 changes D10→20 and commits before the checkpoint. T3 changes B20→15 and T4 changes A15→20 before it. Afterwards T2 changes D20→15, T5 changes C15→30, T3 and T2 commit, then a crash occurs. With immediate update and full flushing, T1 needs nothing; T3’s B is already checkpoint-durable; T2 may require Redo D=15; Undo T5 then T4 to restore C=15 and A=15.

课件p36把“Redo已提交”写成Steal/Force，和其四格表矛盾；这里按No-force才需要Redo解释。延迟更新时T3的B必须Redo，即使写日志在检查点前，因为当时未提交。Tutorial12的T7同理，不能只从检查点后扫描就漏C=31。重启失败事务通常是上层策略，不是每个恢复算法都强制立即重新执行。

The p36 slide associates Redo of committed work with Steal/Force, contradicting its own matrix; this text uses No-force as the reason for Redo. Under deferred update, T3’s B requires Redo even though its log predates the checkpoint, because it was uncommitted then. Tutorial 12 T7 likewise needs C=31 from a pre-checkpoint record. Restarting losers is normally a higher-level policy, not an unconditional immediate step in every recovery algorithm.

影子分页与ADR的课程范围The course scope of shadow paging and ADR

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week12-Recovery.pdf · PDF p. 37-38（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

影子分页（shadow paging）保留不变的影子页表，更新使用新的页与当前页表；提交时可靠地切换指向新页表的根，失败则仍用旧影子。它把“覆盖旧页”变成“写新页后切换引用”，但有页复制、碎片、垃圾回收和并发管理成本。根指针切换也必须原子且持久，不能假设普通内存赋值就完成提交。

Shadow paging preserves an unchanged shadow page table while updates use new pages and a current table. Commit durably switches the root to the new table; failure retains the old shadow. This replaces overwriting with copy-on-write and reference switching, at the cost of copying, fragmentation, reclamation and concurrency handling. The root switch itself must be atomic and durable; an ordinary RAM assignment is insufficient.

加速数据库恢复（accelerated database recovery，ADR）是课程提到的SQL Server特定恢复技术，目标包括更快恢复与回滚。理解其产品背景与目标即可，资料没有给出可手算的ADR内部算法，不应编造考试步骤。课程“瞬时回滚”的表述是产品特征描述，不能无条件推成任意数据库、任意操作都0耗时。

Accelerated database recovery (ADR) is the SQL Server-specific technique introduced by the course for faster recovery and rollback. Understand its product context and goals; the materials provide no detailed hand-calculation algorithm for ADR, so none is invented here. The slide’s instantaneous-rollback phrasing describes a product feature and should not become a zero-time guarantee for every database operation.

总结、英文作答与自测Summary, English answer patterns and self-check

恢复题先写缓冲策略、检查点保证与崩溃时提交集合，再区分需要哪些日志、哪些值已可靠落盘。英文论证：“Undo is required because steal may have propagated uncommitted changes to disk. Redo is required because no-force may leave committed changes only in volatile buffers.”接着给出具体事务、日志方向与目标值。

Begin recovery answers with buffer policy, checkpoint guarantees and the committed set at crash time. Then identify necessary log records and values already known durable. An English justification is: “Undo is required because steal may have propagated uncommitted changes to disk. Redo is required because no-force may leave committed changes only in volatile buffers.” Follow with transaction ID s, log direction and target values.

C12-S1

自编练习：为什么恢复可能还要读取检查点前日志？

Why is a pre-checkpoint log record sometimes needed during recovery?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

活跃事务的修改可能在检查点被刷新，后来未提交就崩溃，需要旧BFIM撤销；延迟更新事务也可能检查点前记日志、之后提交，需要旧AFIM重做。能否跳过取决于检查点保证与事务状态，不仅取决于写发生的日期。

An active transaction’s update may flush at the checkpoint and later fail, requiring its earlier BFIM for Undo. A deferred transaction may log before the checkpoint and commit afterwards, requiring its earlier AFIM for Redo. Skipping depends on checkpoint guarantees and transaction state, not merely when the write occurred.

C12-S2

自编练习：No-steal/Force下，仅丢内存的崩溃需要Undo或Redo吗？

Under No-steal/Force, do you need Undo or Redo after a memory-only crash?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

按该简单模型不需要：未提交效果没有落数据磁盘，提交效果都已强制写入。但若介质损坏或提交协议未原子完成，需讨论其他机制；题目所给假设界定结论范围。

Under this simple model, neither is required: uncommitted effects never reached data storage and committed effects were forced. Media failure or an incomplete atomic commit protocol raises other recovery questions. The stated assumptions define the conclusion’s scope.

Tutorial 12：检查点与恢复全过程Tutorial 12: checkpoints and complete recovery

来源 / Source: tut/Tut12-Rec-2026.pdf · PDF p. 1-2（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

Ex1明确默认Steal/No-force，检查点表示修改缓冲已刷新。Ex2另明确用延迟更新，不能沿用Ex1的“未提交页也可落盘”前提。先写政策再判断动作。

Ex 1 explicitly assumes Steal/No-force and a checkpoint flushing modified buffers. Ex 2 instead uses deferred update; do not carry over Ex 1’s permission for uncommitted effects to reach disk. State policy before deciding actions.

T12-E1-A

T1检查点后开始、崩溃前提交，需什么恢复？

T1 started after the checkpoint and committed before the crash. What recovery action is needed?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

Redo。No-force使提交后的数据页可能仍未写盘，持久日志可重放AFIM保证持久性。并不是“开始在检查点后”本身导致Redo，而是提交与数据页持久状态。

Redo. No-force can leave committed pages un flushed; durable log AFIMs restore durability. Starting after the checkpoint alone is not the reason; commit status and page durability are.

T12-E1-B

T2检查点前开始，崩溃前未提交，需什么？

T2 started before the checkpoint but did not commit before the crash.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

Undo。Steal及本题检查点刷新可能使未提交值在盘中，需反向BFIM撤销，可能追到检查点之前。是否重启由上层决定；不能因为“检查点前”就当T2已经完成。

Undo. Steal and the stated checkpoint flush can place uncommitted values on disk; reverse BFIMs, possibly from before the checkpoint. Restart is a higher-level choice; pre-checkpoint start does not imply completion.

T12-E2-t5

t5崩溃时按何顺序恢复？

At the crash, what is recovered and in what order?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

T5在检查点已持久，无需重做。Redo提交T7的C=31与T6的Z=65，按原有效日志顺序；必须能追溯T7检查点前那条记录。忽略未提交T8(D13)和T9(X43)，因为延迟更新不让它们上盘，无需Undo。最终C31、Z65；D与X保留此前值，但题目未给旧值，不能编数字。课程“从检查点开始”描述会漏T7，需要上述补充。

T5 is already durable. Redo committed T7’s C=31 and T6’s Z=65 in effective log order, including T7’s pre-checkpoint record. Ignore uncommitted T8(D13)and T9(X43), since deferred update kept them off data storage and no Undo is required. Final C31, Z65; D/Xretain prior values, which are unspecified. Do not invent numbers. The course’s scan-from-checkpoint wording needs this correction.

T12-E2-immediate-extension

教学补充：若改为立即更新会怎样？

Teaching extension: what changes if immediate update is assumed instead?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程提供答案；附逐步推导与更正 / Course answer with stepwise derivation and corrections

Steal/No-force时T8、T9可能已写盘，需Undo；但本日志没有D/X的BFIM，无法求具体恢复旧值。完整刷新检查点可能已持久化T7的C31，因此它的该项可无需Redo；T6仍需考虑Redo。必须一并改变日志需求和检查点语义，不能只改一个策略名称。

Under Steal/No-force, T8/T9 may have reached disk and need Undo, but the log lacks D/XBFIMsfor numerical restoration. Afull-flush checkpoint may already have made T7’s C31 durable, while T6 still needs Redo consideration. Change logging requirements and checkpoint semantics together, not just the policy name.

INFS7903 · CH13

综合实践与 SQL 扩展Integrated practice and SQL extensions

学习目标与综合应用的边界Learning objectives and integrated application

本章把前12章连接到A1、A2的实际任务：先解释规则与结果语义，再选择SQL结构，最后用可复现证据解释行为。补充窗口函数、递归CTE、反连接和MVCC，只扩展作业明确需要的部分。作业原题、分值与模板均已登记；已有学生SQL与报告是个人材料，不作为课程官方答案。这里提供学习迁移与实验设计，不把未运行结果写成截图或提交成绩。

This chapter connects Chapters 1–12 to A1/A2: define rules and output semantics, choose SQL structures, and explain behaviour with reproducible evidence. Existing student SQL/reports are personal materials, not official solutions. The chapter develops transfer and experiments without inventing screenshots or marks for unrun work.

A1：把规则分解成可测试不变量A1: decompose rules into testable in variants

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 1-6（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

ShiftDB用ROLES、SHIFTS、VOLUNTEERS、SIGNUPS描述角色、班次、志愿者与报名。年龄按班次开始日而非今天或报名日；取消报名不占名额；积分只来自Attended。原始完整数据脚本实际解析到14角色、350志愿者、900班次、7702报名；A1/1同名数据脚本是0字节，应使用A1/2的完整文件。这里只读取和统计，未执行重建数据库的脚本。

ShiftDB models roles, shifts, volunteers and sign ups. Age is measured at the shift start date, not today or signup date. Cancelled signups do not occupy capacity, and credits come only from Attended. The complete script was parsed as 14 roles,350 volunteers,900 shifts and 7702 signups. The copy in A1/1 is empty; the complete copy is in A1/2. This was reading/statistical parsing, not execution of the database-rebuilding script.

Comments(2)

解释为何这样设计。

Explain the design rationale.

每个对象说明用途、边界与选择。

Purpose, boundaries and choices for each object.

两字母序列编码有676种组合：((n/26) mod26,n mod26)，所以n与n+676的后缀相同。加日期能区分不同天，不能保证同一天无限唯一。序列不重复与编码结果不重复是不同性质；主键仍是最后防线。学生已有脚本的注释不能代替这项数学检验。

A two-letter code has 676 combinations:((n/26)mod 26, nmod 26), so n and n+676 share a suffix. Adate distinguishes days but does not guarantee unlimited uniqueness within one day. An on repeating sequence and a non repeating encoding are different properties; the primary key remains the final defence. Comments in a personal script do not replace this mathematical check.

积分可用“移除OLD贡献、加入NEW贡献”理解，或按受影响志愿者重新求和。UPDATE要覆盖从Attended变出与变入，DELETE只能看OLD，INSERT只能看NEW。全量重算对串行测试较直观，但并发快照和父行更新顺序仍须单独审查。课程要求的触发事件范围与“所有可能修改岗位费率”的更大系统规则应分开。

Understand credits as removing OLD contribution and adding NEW, or re computing affected volunteers’ totals. UPDATE handles entering/leaving Attended, DELETE uses OLD and INSERT uses NEW. Full re computation is intuitive for serial tests, but concurrent snapshots and parent-update ordering need separate review. Distinguish required signup-trigger coverage from a larger system that also permits changing role rates.

A2：数据规模与任务链A2: data scale and task dependencies

来源 / Source: A2/a2-2026-v1.pdf · PDF p. 1-12（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

FleetDB脚本实际解析结果：60个站点、90种车型、900辆车、24,000成员、196,440事件。事件Reserve67697、Cancel8158、Collect59539、Return59539、Incident1507；赞助链最长3条边，解析未见环。事件成员与车辆引用均能在解析数据中找到。这些是本地脚本静态审计结果，不代表已加载服务器或验证所有业务约束。

Static parsing of FleetDB found 60 stations,90 models,900 vehicles,24,000 members and 196,440 events: Reserve 67697, Cancel 8158, Collect 59539, Return 59539, Incident 1507. Sponsorship paths reach three edges and no cycle was found in the parsed data. All event member/vehicle references exist. These are local script-audit findings, not proof of server loading or every business constraint.
