前置知识补充：分组最高项与窗口函数Prerequisite supplement: top-per-group and windows

窗口函数（window function）在一组相关行上计算，同时保留每行身份；GROUP BY则通常把一组压为一行。每地区最高销售门店可先按地区、门店汇总，再ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY region ORDER BY total DESC,shop)编号，外层保留rn=1。RANK会把并列最高都标1；若只要一店，ROW_NUMBER需明确次排序以保证可重复。

A window function computes across related rows while preserving each row’s identity; GROUP BY normally collapses groups. Top sales per region can first aggregate by region/shop, then useROW_NUMBER()OVER(PARTITION BY region ORDER BY total DESC, shop)and filter rn=1 outside. RANK labels tied winners 1; if exactly one winner is required, ROW_NUMBERneeds a deterministic tie-breaker.

-- Author-created example / 自编例子
WITH sales(region,shop,amount) AS (
 VALUES ('North','A',10),('North','B',10),('South','C',7)
), ranked AS (
 SELECT region,shop,amount,
 ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY region ORDER BY amount DESC,shop) AS rn
 FROM sales
)
SELECT region,shop,amount FROM ranked WHERE rn=1 ORDER BY region;

该自编例预期North选A、South选C；若只按amount排序，North两店并列时结果不唯一。窗口结果rn通常不能在同一层WHERE中使用，所以再套CTE/子查询。数据来自VALUES，读者可独立复算；本教材不把这个结果冒称FleetDB的真实最忙站点。

The example expects North/A and South/C. Without the shop tie-breaker, the North winner is undetermined. The computed rn usually needs an outer CTE/sub query filter. It is a self-contained VALUES example, not a claim about FleetDB ’s actual busiest station.

PostgreSQL 17 · Window functions

前置知识补充：递归CTE遍历层级Prerequisite supplement: recursive CTEs traverse hierarchies

公共表表达式（common table expression，CTE）给查询中的中间结果命名，类似RA临时关系。递归CTE包含起始项和递归项：先生成根，再把上一轮发现的节点扩展到孩子，直到没有新行。对树形赞助关系，可以从parent为空的根向下传播root；只做COALESCE(parent,id)只看一跳，会把孙节点归到中间节点。

A common table expression (CTE)names an intermediate query result, like an RA temporary relation. A recursive CTE has an initial term and recursive term: start at roots, expand the preceding round to children, and stop when no new rows appear. In a tree, propagate root identifiers down from NULL parents. COALESCE(parent, id)examines only one edge and mis assigns grandchildren to intermediates.

-- Author-created hierarchy / 自编层级
WITH RECURSIVE org(id,parent) AS (
 VALUES ('R',NULL::text),('T','R'),('S','T'),('U','R')
), tree(id,root,depth) AS (
 SELECT id,id,0 FROM org WHERE parent IS NULL
 UNION ALL
 SELECT c.id,t.root,t.depth+1 FROM org c JOIN tree t ON c.parent=t.id
)
SELECT * FROM tree ORDER BY depth,id;

逐轮得到R/R/0；T/R/1与U/R/1；S/R/2。终止依赖数据形成有限无环树；若存在环或无根分量，要检查循环并定义处理策略，不能指望UNION ALL自动去重。先建“每成员属于哪个根”的唯一映射，再聚合费用，避免在递归每层重复计同一笔费用。

Rounds yield R/R/0; T/R/1 and U/R/1; then S/R/2. Termination assumes a finite a cyclic rooted structure. Cycles or rootless components require explicit handling; UNION ALL does not de duplicate automatically. Create one member-to-root mapping before aggregating charges to avoid counting a charge once per recursive level.

PostgreSQL 17 · WITH and recursive queries

反连接、表达式与复合索引的迁移Transferring anti-joins, expressions and composite indexes

反连接（anti-join）寻找没有匹配的左行。NOT EXISTS与LEFT JOIN…WHERE右键IS NULL可等价，前提是右侧测试列能可靠表示匹配，且筛选“Collect”写在ON里保留无匹配左行。若放WHERE event_type=Collect，会先删掉NULL扩展行，破坏“从未Collect”的需求。优化器可能把两种SQL改成相同物理anti join，需看实际计划而非SQL表面。

An anti-join finds left rows without matches. NOT EXISTS and LEFT JOIN…WHEREright_keyIS NULL can be equivalent if the tested right key reliably indicates a match. The Collect predicate belongs in ON to retain unmatched left rows. Putting it in WHERE removes NULL-extended rows and breaks never-Collect semantics. The optimiser may turn both forms into the same physical anti join; inspect the actual plan.

复合B-tree索引(a,b)按先a后b排序，因此a等值加b范围常可缩小连续区间。只筛b、或a范围覆盖大多数行时，未必同样有效。表达式索引按f(email)排序，完整email唯一索引按完整地址排序，不能直接按域后缀等值定位。域需大小写规范化，并保证查询与索引表达式对应；汇总全体成员仍需读取很多值，稀有域查找则可能显著减少访问。

A composite B-tree (a, b)orders by a and then b, so equality on a and range on b can identify a narrow contiguous interval. Filtering only bor matching most a values need not benefit equally. An expression index orders f (email), while a unique email index orders entire addresses, not domain suffixes. Normalise case and match expressions. Afull-domain aggregation still reads many values; rare-domain lookup can reduce access substantially.

快照与触发器：为什么先查再写会失效Snapshots and triggers: why check-then-write can fail

来源 / Source: A2/a2-2026-v1.pdf · PDF p. 7-8（原资料留在本地版） · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

多版本并发控制（multiversion concurrency control，MVCC）让普通SELECT按快照看已提交版本。PostgreSQL默认READ COMMITTED为每条语句取新快照；同事务两次查询之间，其他事务提交可改变第二次所见。普通快照读通常不挡普通写，读到某行并不预留它；SELECT FOR UPDATE是显式加锁的另一个操作，DDL等也有不同锁。

MVCC lets ordinary SELECT read committed versions through snapshots. PostgreSQL’s default READ COMMITTED takes a fresh snapshot per statement, so another commit between statements can change the second result. Ordinary snapshot reads normally do not block ordinary writes and do not reserve rows. SELECT FOR UPDATE explicitly locks; DDL has other locking behaviour.

自编库存例：库存1，S1查1但不锁；S2查1后扣到0并提交；S1仍按先前结论尝试购买，新的语句/触发器可能看到0。旧查询正确不代表后来条件仍真。可把检查与修改合为带条件UPDATE…WHERE stock>0并检查影响行数，或锁同一资源行并在锁后重查；使用更强隔离也要处理冲突重试。

In a stock example, S1 reads 1 without locking; S2 reads 1, decrements to 0 and commits; S1 then acts on its earlier conclusion, but a new statement/trigger can see 0. A correct earlier query does not keep a condition true. Combine checking with conditional UPDATE…WHERE stock>0 and verify affected rows, or lock the resource and recheck. Stronger isolation also requires conflict retries.

时刻 / StepSession1Session2解释 / Explanation
1BEGIN; SELECT stock → 1—

仅快照读

Snapshot only

2

尚未提交

Still open

UPDATE stock→0; COMMIT

没有预留资源

Resource not reserved

3

新语句看到0

New statement sees 0

—

新快照

New snapshot

4

有条件UPDATE影响0行

Conditional update affects 0 rows

—

业务必须处理失败

Application handles failure

A2脚本BI_COLLECT按event_time DESC,event_id DESC找“最新事件”，它在后来的INSERT语句内读快照。若另一会话已提交未来时间的Reserve，按给定代码预期该Reserve可能成为最新、并拒绝其他成员Collect；这是逻辑推导，本次没有执行两会话实验。Q4.2开头“only one blocked”不应替代实际证据：异常拒绝、事务失败与锁等待是不同现象，要记录真正输出。

A2’sBI_COLLECTfinds the latest event byevent_timeDESC, event_idDESCfrom its later INSERT snapshot. If another session committed a future Reserve, it can become the latest event and reject a different member’s Collect under the supplied code. This is a logical expectation, not a two-session observation here.Q4.2’s“only one blocked”wording does not replace evidence: exception rejection, failed transaction state and lock waiting differ. Record the real output.

PostgreSQL 17 · Transaction isolation

怎样形成可以复现的实验解释Producing a reproducible experimental explanation

用固定次序记录：数据准备版本与行数，当前索引，ANALYZE时间，所有SET值，完整SQL，完整计划，估计/实际行数与loops、耗时、观察结论。两会话实验每步注明连接1/2和阻塞点；错误后先确认事务是否仍可继续，再ROLLBACK清理。不要把某次运行的EXPLAIN估计行数抄成真实结果。

Record preparation version and row counts, indexes, ANALYZE, all SET values, SQL, full plans, estimated/actual rows, loops, timing and interpretation in a fixed order. Label session 1/2 and blocking points in concurrency experiments. After errors, check transaction state and ROLLBACK for cleanup. Never report estimated EXPLAIN rows as observed results.

练习迁移时改变一个条件：稀有值换常见值、等值换范围、单层父关系换三层、普通视图换物化视图，先预测再跑实验。预测和观测有分歧时，检查输入、统计、表达式匹配、缓存及会话设置，而不是改写观测来迎合理论。

For transfer practice, change one condition: rare to common, equality to range, one parent level to three, or ordinary to materialised view. Predict before executing. If observation differs, inspect inputs, statistics, expression matching, caching and session settings rather than altering evidence to fit theory.

总结与自测Summary and self-check

综合问题常同时需要语义与机制：找对根账户是逻辑，访问费用是优化；用触发器拒绝非法状态是完整性，两个会话争同资源是并发。把一题分成这些可以解释的小问题，再组合回完整方案，才具备迁移能力。

Integrated problems combine semantics and mechanisms: root-account identification is logic, access expense is optimisation; trigger rejection concerns integrity, while two sessions competing for a resource concern concurrency. Decompose the task into explain able pieces and recombine them to develop transfer ability.

C13-S1

自编练习：A赞助关系指B，B指C，C为根。COALESCE(parent,id)为何对A错误？

A→B→C is a sponsorship chain with Cthe root. Why does COALESCE(parent, id)fail for A?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

它返回直接父B，不会继续到C。需递归或已验证根映射；每笔费用只按最终根累计一次。

It returns immediate parent Bwithout reaching C. Use recursion or a verified root mapping, and count each charge once at its final root.

C13-S2

自编练习：域表达式索引为何更帮助稀有域查找，而非全域汇总？

Why might a domain-expression index help a rare lookup much more than a grouped count over all domains?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习 / Author-created exercise

稀有等值只找少量候选，可能避免绝大多数数据访问；全域计数必须覆盖全部成员，表达式索引或可省计算/排序，却不自动免去读全体值。实际取决于计划、覆盖与可见性。

Arare equality lookup finds few candidates and can avoid most data access. Afull grouped count still covers all members; an expression index may save computation/order work but does not eliminate reading all values. The actual effect depends on plan, coverage and visibility.

DIY完整教学示例与边界Complete teaching DIY examples and boundaries

经理工资示例假设emp(empno,mgr,sal)。BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE查经理，若自己有下属，自己的降薪也要检查。它适合单会话学习；并发时仍需一致锁定协议，且NULL经理表示无经理、NULL工资是否允许需先定义。MGR外键若存在，改EMPNO类型时必须同步；Prac原始MGR没有自引用外键，不应擅自假设已建。

The salary example assumes emp (empno, mgr, sal). BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE checks both the employee’s manager and subordinates when a manager changes pay. It is a single-session teaching example; concurrency needs a consistent locking protocol. Define NULL salary semantics and treat NULL manager as no manager. If a MGRFK exists, it must follow an EMPNO type change; the original practical does not create that self-FK.

CREATE FUNCTION check_employee_salary() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE manager_salary NUMERIC;
BEGIN
 IF NEW.mgr IS NOT NULL THEN
   SELECT sal INTO manager_salary FROM emp WHERE empno=NEW.mgr;
   IF NEW.sal>manager_salary THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Above manager salary'; END IF;
 END IF;
 IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM emp e WHERE e.mgr=NEW.empno AND e.sal>NEW.sal)
 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Below subordinate salary'; END IF;
 RETURN NEW;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_employee_salary BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON emp
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION check_employee_salary();

部门前缀编号示例在单独练习副本清空EMP后使用。假设前三字母在部门间唯一，DNAME稳定，MGR已改为文本以引用文本编号。计数表按部门原子递增，用RETURNING取本次号码，比SELECT MAX+1更能抵抗并发重复。本计数是事务内计数表，不是PostgreSQL独立SEQUENCE对象；若严格要求每部门sequence对象，则每部门建一个序列并用安全标识符引用，不能靠一个全局nextval表达每部门独立计数。

Use the prefix example after clearing EMP only in a separate practice copy. Assume department prefixes are unique, DNAME stable, and MGR converted to text for textual identifiers. Anatomic per-department counter with RETURNING avoids the SELECT MAX+1 race. This is a transactional counter table, not a standalone SEQUENCE object. If the task strictly requires separate sequence objects, create one per department and reference identifiers safely; one global next val does not provide per-department counts.

-- In an isolated practice copy / 仅在隔离练习副本
DELETE FROM emp;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS bi_emp ON emp;
ALTER TABLE emp ALTER COLUMN empno TYPE VARCHAR(10) USING empno::text;
ALTER TABLE emp ALTER COLUMN mgr TYPE VARCHAR(10) USING mgr::text;
CREATE TABLE dept_emp_seq(deptno INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,n INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE FUNCTION prefixed_empno() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE prefix TEXT; number INTEGER;
BEGIN
 SELECT upper(left(dname,3)) INTO prefix FROM dept WHERE deptno=NEW.deptno;
 IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unknown department'; END IF;
 INSERT INTO dept_emp_seq VALUES(NEW.deptno,1)
 ON CONFLICT(deptno) DO UPDATE SET n=dept_emp_seq.n+1
 RETURNING n INTO number;
 NEW.empno:=prefix||number::text;
 RETURN NEW;
END; $$;
-- Remove the old numeric BI_EMP in the practice copy before attaching this one
CREATE TRIGGER bi_emp_prefix BEFORE INSERT ON emp
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION prefixed_empno();

下面是严格使用SEQUENCE对象的替代版本，与上面的计数表版本二选一。先按上例在隔离副本清空EMP、将EMPNO/MGR转为文本，并移除旧bi_emp和bi_emp_prefix。准备时为每个已存在部门建立独立序列；新建部门之后也要建立对应序列。序列取号不会因事务回滚而归还，出现空号是预期行为。编号前缀唯一仍需另行保证。

The following alternative strictly uses SEQUENCE objects. Choose this or the counter-table version. First clear EMP in an isolated copy, convert EMPNO/MGR to text as above, and remove old bi_emp and bi_emp_prefix triggers. Preparation creates one independent sequence for each existing department; newly created departments also need a sequence. Sequence values are not returned after rollback, so gaps are expected. Prefix uniqueness remains a separate requirement.

-- Alternative in the same isolated practice copy; do not attach both generators
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS bi_emp ON emp;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS bi_emp_prefix ON emp;
DO $$
DECLARE d RECORD;
BEGIN
 FOR d IN SELECT deptno FROM public.dept LOOP
   EXECUTE format('CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS public.%I START 1',
                  'emp_dept_'||d.deptno::text||'_seq');
 END LOOP;
END $$;
CREATE FUNCTION prefixed_empno_sequence() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE prefix TEXT; seqname REGCLASS; number BIGINT;
BEGIN
 SELECT upper(left(dname,3)) INTO prefix
 FROM public.dept WHERE deptno=NEW.deptno;
 IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unknown department'; END IF;
 seqname:=to_regclass(format('public.%I','emp_dept_'||NEW.deptno::text||'_seq'));
 IF seqname IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Department sequence is missing'; END IF;
 number:=nextval(seqname);
 NEW.empno:=prefix||number::text;
 RETURN NEW;
END; $$;
CREATE TRIGGER bi_emp_prefix_sequence BEFORE INSERT ON public.emp
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION prefixed_empno_sequence();

例如ENG与ACC对应不同DEPTNO，先后插ENG1、ACC1、ENG2。先查dname前三字母是否重名，再测试实际结果。上例假定public模式和已有部门名；在别的模式必须同步修改限定名。VARCHAR(10)限制前缀加数字的长度，因此不能声称无限可用。函数体只做静态审查，本次未在PostgreSQL运行。

For different department numbers with prefixes ENG and ACC, successive inserts yield ENG1, ACC1 and ENG2. Check for duplicate three-letter prefixes before testing. This example assumes the public schema and existing department names; qualify another schema consistently. VARCHAR(10) bounds the prefix-plus-number length, so the scheme is not unlimited. The function body was statically reviewed, not run on PostgreSQL here.

Prac5 DIY5.1可用下列约束和目录查询。Prac6 DIY用第9章的连接SQL前后对照，再恢复设置。所有代码是根据资料整理的教学解法，本次只进行静态语法与部分逻辑验证，未声称课程PostgreSQL实例全部运行成功。

Prac 5 DIY 5.1 uses the constraint/catalogue query below. Prac 6 DIY compares the Chapter 9 join before/after dropping the key and restores settings. These are source-derived teaching solutions with static syntax and selected logic checks, not claimed successful runs on the course PostgreSQL instance.

ALTER TABLE project ADD CONSTRAINT un_projectname UNIQUE(projectname);
SELECT indexname,indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE tablename='project';
SET enable_seqscan TO on;

L07-18

比较直接自然连接与先投影SSN,Dno并改名MgrSSN,Dnumber再自然连接。

Compare EMPLOYEE*DEPARTMENT with EMP(MgrSSN, Dnumber)←πSSN, Dno (EMPLOYEE); RESULT←EMP*DEPARTMENT.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程例题；表达式经教学整理 / Course example with a teaching-formulated expression

直接两模式无同名列，得到乘积。第二式在MgrSSN与Dnumber两列同时匹配，相当于找“员工自己是该部门经理且属于该部门”的部门记录，不是普通部门员工连接。结果模式为MgrSSN,Dnumber,DName,MgrStart。

The original schemas share no names, so direct natural join is a product. The second matches both MgrSSN and Dnumber, requiring the employee to be the department’s manager and member, not any employee. Output MgrSSN, Dnumber, DName, MgrStart.

EMP(MgrSSN,Dnumber) ← πSSN,Dno(E)
RESULT ← EMP ⋈ D

其他Lecture明确任务与正文解答位置Other explicit lecture tasks and answer locations

L01-FD

找XYZ样本满足的函数依赖及冗余。

Find all functional dependencies in the X, Y, Z sample and identify redundancies.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

样本中X唯一，X→Y和X→Z成立；每个(Y,Z)组合也唯一，所以YZ→X成立。Y→Z、Y→X、Z→X、Z→Y均有反例。可取最小覆盖{X→Y,X→Z,YZ→X}，XY→Z和XZ→Y由前两条推出而冗余。x1为(y1,z1)，x2为(y1,z2)，两行Z不同，不能误读成同一组合。样本满足不证明业务永恒FD。

X is unique, so X→Y and X→Z hold. Each (Y, Z) pair is also unique, so YZ→X holds. Y→Z, Y→X, Z→X, Z→Y have counterexamples. A minimal cover is {X→Y, X→Z, YZ→X}; XY→Z and XZ→Y are redundant. X1 has (y1, z1), X2 has (y1, z2), not identical pairs. This instance check does not prove a permanent business FD.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week01-Intro.pdf · PDF p. 36（原资料留在本地版） L01-FD · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

L01-Q1

找出版business书籍的出版社，比较相关与非相关查询。

Find publishers who publish business books; compare correlated and uncorrelated queries.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

第2章EXISTS思路：出版社p满足EXISTS titles t WHERE t.pubid=p.pubid AND t.type=business。非相关可先取business的pubid集合再用IN；两者须以同一键匹配。

Use EXISTS titles t with t.pubid=p.pubid and t.type=business. Alternatively form business pu bids and use IN. Both must match the same publisher key.

L02-UPDATE

判断GPA更新、Jane插入、聚合更新和连接视图删除。

Reason about GPA updates, inserting Jane, aggregate updates and deletion through a computer/supervisor join view.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

简单列更新需满足基表约束，CHECK OPTION防止改出视图；Jane缺必填SID无默认则失败；平均值逆映射不唯一；连接视图删除无法唯一指定删电脑还是管理员。第3章详解，并区分课程保守PK规则与PostgreSQL。

Simple updates must satisfy base constraints; CHECK OPTION prevents leaving the view. Jane’s missing mandatory SID fails without a default. Aggregate inverse updates and join view deletions are ambiguous. See Chapter 3 and its course-versus PostgreSQL distinction.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week02-Views.pdf · PDF p. 34-43（原资料留在本地版） L02-UPDATE · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

L04-CLASS

分类工资不降、收入还款联动、到期晚于发行、限时存款、总工资低于预算。

Classify salary non-reduction, income/repayment co-update, expiry after issue, time-restricted deposits, and total salary below budget.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

前两项动态转移约束；Expiry>Issue是同状态静态跨列；限时存款是操作时间动态规则；总工资<预算是跨行静态状态规则。分别采用第4章触发器、CHECK、受控操作入口、跨行检查，不能因跨表就一律叫动态。

The first two are transition rules; Expiry>Issue is a static same-row rule; time-restricted deposits are operational/temporal; salary sum<budget is across-row state constraint. Use Chapter 4’s appropriate mechanisms rather than calling every cross-table rule dynamic.

来源 / Source: Lecture/Week04-Constraints-2026.pdf · PDF p. 12（原资料留在本地版） L04-CLASS · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

L05-CAP

按给定参数求道、面、盘容量。

Compute track, surface, disk capacities for 512-byte sectors,50 sectors/track,2000 tracks/surface,5 double-sided platters.

L06-INSERT

阶3B+树插23,20,6,22,18,27,24,21，再25。

Insert 23,20,6,22,18,27,24,21 into an order 3 B+tree, then 25.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

与Tut6序列每键加3同构，故可按第6章八步表所有键加3复原。插21后根20，内部18与22,23；叶6,18→20→21,22→23→24,27。再25使最右叶分裂，父分裂后根20,23，内部18、22、25，叶6,18→20→21,22→23→24,25→27。本答案沿用课件的左子树最大值约定，保留原图供核对。

The first eight keys are Tutorial 6’s keys plus 3, so translate its states. After 21, root 20, internal 18 and 22,23; leaves[6,18],[20],[21,22],[23],[24,27]. Adding 25 splits right leaf and parent, giving root 20,23, internal 18/22/25, leaves[6,18],[20],[21,22],[23],[24,25],[27]. This follows the lecture’s left-maximum convention; originals remain in the source atlas.

L06-DELETE

从给定阶3树依次删8、15、12。

Delete 8,15,12 from the given order 3 B+tree.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

删8后首相关叶8空，需按给定叶最小容量处理合并/再分配；课件把该叶去掉并更新父。删15后12叶仍可留1；删12后右侧只剩10,11，需要借或合并并更新内部节点，最后可压成根[4,9]、叶[4],[9],[10,11]，也可合法重分配成根[9,10]、叶[4,9],[10],[11]。删除策略不唯一，逐步检查占用、边界与等高。

Deleting 8 empties its leaf, requiring redistribution/merge and parent update under the occupancy convention. Deleting 15 leaves 12; deleting 12 forces right side rebalancing with 10,11. A valid collapsed tree is root[4,9]with leaves[4],[9],[10,11], or after redistribution root[9,10]with[4,9],[10],[11]. Policies can vary; check occupancy, separators and equal depth.

L11-WAIT

按所列锁序列画等待图。

Build the wait-for graph for s1(A), r1(A), x2(B), w2(B), s1(B), s3(C), r3(C), x2(C), x4(B), x3(A).

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

课程任务；根据资料推导并与原图核对 / Course task,derived and checked against the original figure

T1等T2的B：1→2；T2等T3的C：2→3；T4等T2的B：4→2。最后T3请求X(A)被T1的S(A)挡，3→1，环1→2→3→1。T4不是环成员但也被阻塞。

Edges 1→2 for B,2→3 for C,4→2 for B, and 3→1 when T3 requests X(A)held shared by T1. Cycle 1→2→3→1 is a deadlock. T4 waits but is not on that cycle.

t 1 sets A.RTS76, t 3 sets B.RTS78, t 4 sets A.WTS76; t 5 sets C.RTS77, t 7 raises it to 78, t 8 rejects T77’s Cwrite; t 9 allows T76’s Bread without lowering RTS; t 11 sets B.WTS78; t 13 rejects T76’s Bwriteandundoesits A write. Functions remain symbolic.

A1-Q1

（3分）写一个命名为CK_SHIFT_TIMES的约束：结束严格晚于开始，时长不超过8小时。

(3 marks) Write one named CK_SHIFT_TIMES constraint enforcing ends strictly after starts and duration at most 8 hours.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

同一行两条件用AND。测试0小时、负数、恰8小时及8小时加1秒；若时间列可NULL，CHECK本身不排除UNKNOWN，结合原模式NOT NULL。对应第4章CHECK。

Combine the two same-row conditions with AND. Test zero, negative duration, exactly eight hours and eight hours plus one second. CHECK alone permits UNKNOWN, so use the schema’s NOT NULL requirements. See Chapter 4.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 3（原资料留在本地版） Q1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q2.1

（4分）创建BI_SIGNUP/UDF_BI_SIGNUP：新增报名须达到班次开始日最低年龄，非取消报名不得超容量。

(4 marks) Create BI_SIGNUP/UDF_BI_SIGNUP to enforce age at shift start and non-cancelled capacity on a new signup.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

先由shift找role与开始日，再由volunteer生日判断周年边界；按同shift计非Cancelled，加入本次有效占位后不能超capacity。Cancelled是否占位必须按题目定义；并发必须串行化同班次容量检查。生日等于开始日可，晚一天不可。

Obtain the role and start date from the shift, then test the birthday anniversary boundary. Count non-Cancelled signups for that shift and include the new occupied place where applicable. Follow the given cancellation semantics and serialize capacity checks for the same shift under concurrency. The birthday on the shift date qualifies; the following day does not.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 4（原资料留在本地版） Q2.1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q2.2

（4分）创建更新触发器与函数，禁止更换志愿者或班次；状态只允许不变，或Booked转Attended/NoShow/Cancelled。

(4 marks) Create BU_SIGNUP/UDF_BU_SIGNUP enforcing immutable volunteer_id/shift_ref and only unchanged status or Booked→Attended/No Show/Cancelled.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

比较OLD与NEW引用，状态按有向边判断而非仅检查枚举。终态到终态也不允许，终态保持不变允许。使用IS DISTINCT FROM可稳健比较NULL，但原列非空时普通比较也有前提。列出所有状态对建立测试矩阵。

Compare OLD/NEW references and test directed state transitions, not merely membership in an enumeration. Final-to-final changes are prohibited, while an unchanged final state is permitted. IS DISTINCT FROM handles NULL robustly; ordinary comparison relies on non-null columns. Build a full transition test matrix.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 4（原资料留在本地版） Q2.2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q3.1

（2分）建立SHIFT_REF_SEQ，最小3000、最大999999、步长1。

(2 marks) Create SHIFT_REF_SEQ with minimum 3000, maximum 999999 and increment 1.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

声明范围与起点，通常不循环；下一题只在插入时取nextval。查序列元数据验证，不因一次失败便期待号码回收。

State bounds/start and normally avoid cycling. The next part consumes nextval on insertion. Inspect sequence metadata; a failed transaction does not imply that a consumed value returns.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 4（原资料留在本地版） Q3.1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q3.2

（4分）建BI_SHIFT_REF/UDF_BI_SHIFT_REF，生成YYMMDD-两字母班次编号。

(4 marks) Create BI_SHIFT_REF/UDF_BI_SHIFT_REF generating YY MM DD plus a two-letter code from n.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

日期取starts_at；n/26用整数商，再mod26；第二字母n mod26。n=3000得到115、11=L、10=K，所以260914-LK。每676个n后缀重现，同日不能无限保证唯一，主键仍需检查。

Use starts_at for the date. Integer division n/26 gives 115 at n=3000; 115 mod 26=11 maps to L, and 3000 mod 26=10 maps to K, producing 260914-LK. Suffixes repeat every 676 values, so same-day uniqueness is bounded and the key must still be enforced.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Q3.2 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q3.3

（2分）查询四表上的自建触发器，列触发器名、表名、调用函数。

(2 marks) List every created trigger on the four tables, its table and called function.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

把pg_trigger、pg_class、pg_proc连接，按schema与表过滤并排除tgisinternal。截图是实际运行要求，本次未生成伪截图。对应第4章元数据查询。

Join pg_trigger, pg_class and pg_proc; filter schema/tables and exclude tgisinternal. A real execution screenshot is required by the assignment and was not fabricated here. See Chapter 4 metadata queries.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Q3.3 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q4.1

（5分）用AIUD_CREDITS及函数维护credits，覆盖报名插入、更新、删除，只累计Attended时长×角色费率。

(5 marks) Maintain VOLUNTEERS.credits as total Attended hours×role rate for INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE using AIUD_CREDITS/UDF_AIUD_CREDITS.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

根据课程内容整理的解题指南；未提供官方实现 / Course-derived solution guide; no official implementation supplied

INSERT加NEW有效贡献；DELETE减OLD有效贡献；UPDATE移除OLD再加NEW，或重算受影响者总和。空集合总和需归零。测试每个状态变更和删除，注意函数返回行与触发时机。官方分值为插1、改2、删1、计算1。

INSERT adds qualifying NEW contribution; DELETE removes qualifying OLD contribution; UPDATE removes OLD and adds NEW, or re computes affected totals. Empty sums become zero. Test every transition/deletion and return the appropriate trigger row. The stated marks are insert 1, update 2, delete 1 and calculation 1.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Q4.1 · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q4.2a

（1分）连接Signups、Volunteers、Shifts、Roles建立V_SIGNUP_SHEET，输出规定七列。

(1 marks) Create V_SIGNUP_SHEET over Signups, Volunteers, Shifts, Roles with the seven specified columns.

Join along the foreign keys and output signup_id, volunteer_name, shift_ref, role_name, starts_at, status and recorded_at. Avoid accidental natural joins on unrelated same-named columns.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Q4.2a · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

A1-Q4.2b

（3分）用INSTEAD OF触发器把姓名、班次、登记时间映射为Booked报名；姓名匹配不恰一人则拒绝。

(3 marks) Use IOI_SIGNUP_SHEET/UDF_IOI_SIGNUP_SHEET so an INSERT with name, shift_ref, recorded_at creates a Booked signup; reject non unique names.

Count zero/one/multiple name matches and use the id only for exactly one. Insert into Signups so its existing BI_SIGNUP still validates age/capacity. Do not bypass those rules; let the base default generate signup_id.

来源 / Source: A1/1/A1-Specification-2026-v1.0.pdf · PDF p. 5（原资料留在本地版） Q4.2b · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

另有2分代码注释；总30分，占课程15%。说明文件要求按实际设计解释对象用途与原因，并测试误拒绝和漏拒绝。A1已存个人SQL有两版及重复副本，完整数据在A1/2，A1/1数据文件为空。个人截图报告只有观察材料，不能用来证明所有边界正确。

There are two further marks for code comments, giving 30 marks and 15% of the course. Explain each object’s purpose and rationale and test false positives/negatives. Personal SQL exists in two versions plus a duplicate. The full data is in A1/2 and the A1/1 data file is empty. Personal screenshot reports are evidence of particular observations, not proof of every boundary case.

Q1.1.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）每种body type选取Collect次数最多的单一站点，建视图输出类型、站名、次数并降序。

(1 marks) Create V_BUSIEST_BAYS: for each body type, one station with most collections; report type, station, count ordered descending.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

先按 body_type 和 station 汇总 Collect 次数，再在每种 body type 内用 ROW_NUMBER 排名。只保留 rn = 1，恰好选出一个站点；station_id 是次数相同时的确定性规则。最后按获胜次数降序排列。

The first CTE counts Collect events for each body type and station. ROW_NUMBER partitions those counts by body type and picks the largest count; station_id resolves ties deterministically so exactly one station is returned per represented body type. The final ORDER BY puts the largest winner first.

BOOKING_EVENTS 没有事件发生站点字段，所以这里使用 VEHICLES 记录的站点。这是现有 schema 能支持的站点语义。

The schema records a station on VEHICLES, rather than on BOOKING_EVENTS. The station reported is therefore the vehicle's recorded station, which is the location available in the supplied schema.

A2_1.1.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
CREATE VIEW V_BUSIEST_BAYS AS
WITH collection_counts AS (
    SELECT m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name,
           COUNT(*) AS collections
    FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    JOIN VEHICLES v ON v.vehicle_id = e.vehicle_id
    JOIN MODELS m ON m.model_code = v.model_code
    JOIN STATIONS s ON s.station_id = v.station_id
    WHERE e.event_type = 'Collect'
    GROUP BY m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name
), ranked_stations AS (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
        PARTITION BY body_type
        ORDER BY collections DESC, station_id
    ) AS rn
    FROM collection_counts
)
SELECT body_type, station_name, collections
FROM ranked_stations
WHERE rn = 1
ORDER BY collections DESC, body_type;

每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 22 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02CREATE VIEW V_BUSIEST_BAYS AS

    CREATE VIEW 建立名为 V_BUSIEST_BAYS 的普通视图，AS 后面的 SELECT 是它保存的查询定义。普通视图不是结果快照，读取时由数据库规划并计算这条查询。

    CREATE VIEW names the ordinary view V_BUSIEST_BAYS; AS introduces its stored query definition. It stores a query rather than a snapshot of result rows.

  3. 03WITH collection_counts AS (

    WITH 定义只在当前查询内可见的 CTE collection_counts。括号内先解决“每种车型在每站取过几次车”，把计数和后面的排名分开；CTE 不等于永久表，也不保证一定物化。

    WITH introduces the query-local CTE collection_counts. It separates counting from ranking; a CTE is neither a permanent table nor necessarily materialized.

  4. 04 SELECT m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name,

    取出车型类别，以及站点 ID 和名称。m、s 是后面 JOIN 定义的别名；保留 station_id 能区分同名站点，也能在排名次数相同时确定顺序。

    Select the body type and station identifier/name. The aliases m and s are defined by later joins; station_id distinguishes equal names and provides a deterministic tie-breaker.

  5. 05 COUNT(*) AS collections

    COUNT(*) 数分组中的事件行，并把结果列命名为 collections。后面 WHERE 只保留 Collect，连接目标的主键又是唯一的，因此这里数的是取车事件次数，不是车辆数。

    COUNT(*) counts event rows in each group and names the column collections. The Collect filter and joins to unique keys make this a count of collections, not distinct vehicles.

  6. 06 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e

    从事件日志 BOOKING_EVENTS 开始，给它别名 e。车可以多次被取用，所以不能只从 VEHICLES 数车辆来替代事件次数。

    Read BOOKING_EVENTS as e. A vehicle may be collected repeatedly, so counting fleet vehicles cannot replace counting events.

  7. 07 JOIN VEHICLES v ON v.vehicle_id = e.vehicle_id

    按 vehicle_id 把每条事件接到它对应的车辆 v。车辆主键唯一，因而一条事件最多接到一辆车；这一步取得车辆的 model_code 与 station_id。

    Join each event to its vehicle v through vehicle_id. The unique vehicle key prevents multiplication and exposes the model and recorded station.

  8. 08 JOIN MODELS m ON m.model_code = v.model_code

    把车辆的 model_code 接到 MODELS 主键，给车型表别名 m，从而取得 body_type。事件日志本身没有车型类别，必须经过车辆再找到车型。

    Join the vehicle model_code to MODELS as m to obtain body_type; the event log does not store this attribute directly.

  9. 09 JOIN STATIONS s ON s.station_id = v.station_id

    把车辆记录的 station_id 接到 STATIONS，给站点表别名 s，取得站名。原 schema 没有事件发生地点字段，所以这里使用车辆所记录的站点。

    Join the vehicle’s station_id to STATIONS as s for its name. This uses the vehicle’s recorded station because events have no location column.

  10. 10 WHERE e.event_type = 'Collect'

    只保留事件类型等于字符串 Collect 的行；单引号表示字符串，e.event_type 是列。Reserve、Return 等其他事件不进入后续计数。

    Keep only rows whose event_type equals the string Collect. Single quotes delimit a string literal; other event types are excluded before counting.

  11. 11 GROUP BY m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name

    按“车型类别＋站点”分组，再计算每组 COUNT(*)。站点 ID 和名称一起写清输出语义；名字相同但 ID 不同的站点不会被合并。

    Group by body type and station, then count each group. Including the station identifier keeps distinct stations separate even if names match.

  12. 12), ranked_stations AS (

    前面的右括号结束 collection_counts；逗号开启第二个 CTE ranked_stations。两个 CTE 属于同一条 WITH 查询，不是两条独立执行的 SQL。

    Close collection_counts and introduce ranked_stations after the comma. Both CTEs belong to one WITH query, not separate SQL commands.

  13. 13 SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (

    SELECT * 保留上一层的所有列；ROW_NUMBER() OVER 开始添加一个窗口排名列。窗口函数不会把这些计数行再次合并，而是为每行增加序号。

    Retain all CTE columns and add a ROW_NUMBER window result. Window processing assigns a number to each row without collapsing rows into new groups.

  14. 14 PARTITION BY body_type

    PARTITION BY body_type 让每种车型分别从 1 开始排名。如果省略这一行，就会把所有车型混在同一个总排名里。

    PARTITION BY body_type restarts numbering for each body type. Without it, all body types would compete in a single global ranking.

  15. 15 ORDER BY collections DESC, station_id

    每种车型内部按 collections 降序，次数最多的排前面；相同次数时 station_id 默认升序。这个额外规则使 ROW_NUMBER 恰好选出一个确定的站点，而 RANK 可能产生多个并列第一。

    Within each body type, sort counts descending and station_id ascending for ties. The extra key gives ROW_NUMBER one deterministic winner; RANK could retain tied winners.

  16. 16 ) AS rn

    关闭 OVER 的窗口定义，并把排名结果命名为 rn。rn 是这一层新增的列，下一层才能用 WHERE rn = 1 过滤，不能直接在同层 WHERE 中引用窗口结果。

    Close OVER and name the window output rn. An outer query can filter rn; the same SELECT level’s WHERE cannot directly filter its window result.

  17. 17 FROM collection_counts

    第二个 CTE 的输入是刚才已经计数的 collection_counts。排名作用于“每站的计数行”，不是直接对 59,539 条原始 Collect 事件编号。

    Rank the already aggregated collection_counts rows, rather than numbering all raw Collect events.

  18. 18)

    结束 ranked_stations 的查询定义；下面的 SELECT 是整条 WITH 查询的最终输出。括号只划定语法范围，不是事务结束。

    Close ranked_stations. The following SELECT is the final WITH-query output; this parenthesis is a scope boundary, not a transaction boundary.

  19. 19SELECT body_type, station_name, collections

    最终只输出题目要求的 body_type、station_name 和 collections。station_id 与 rn 是实现排名所需的辅助列，不暴露为视图的结果列。

    Project only the required body type, station name and count. station_id and rn are internal ranking helpers, not final view columns.

  20. 20FROM ranked_stations

    从含有每组序号的 ranked_stations 读取。此时每一行仍代表一个车型与站点组合，但已经知道它在本车型中的排名。

    Read ranked_stations, where each body-type/station row now has its within-type rank.

  21. 21WHERE rn = 1

    每种车型仅保留 rn 为 1 的行，所以结果是一种车型一个站点。这里是每组取第一，不是全表 LIMIT 1；本数据返回 6 种车型。

    Keep rank one within each body type. This is one winner per group, not LIMIT 1 over the whole table; the dataset yields six body types.

  22. 22ORDER BY collections DESC, body_type;

    把六个获胜站点按取车次数从多到少排列；次数相同再按 body_type 排序。分号结束 CREATE VIEW 整条语句，后续查询该视图即可执行这个定义。

    Sort the six winners by count descending and then body type. The semicolon ends CREATE VIEW; subsequent reads use this definition.

CREATE VIEW V_BUSIEST_BAYS AS
WITH collection_counts AS (
    SELECT m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name,
           COUNT(*) AS collections
    FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    JOIN VEHICLES v ON v.vehicle_id = e.vehicle_id
    JOIN MODELS m ON m.model_code = v.model_code
    JOIN STATIONS s ON s.station_id = v.station_id
    WHERE e.event_type = 'Collect'
    GROUP BY m.body_type, s.station_id, s.station_name
), ranked_stations AS (
    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
        PARTITION BY body_type
        ORDER BY collections DESC, station_id
    ) AS rn
    FROM collection_counts
)
SELECT body_type, station_name, collections
FROM ranked_stations
WHERE rn = 1
ORDER BY collections DESC, body_type;

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）查询视图并报告结果、完整计划，解释耗时、成本及行数。

(0.5 marks) Query V_BUSIEST_BAYS and report/explain results and plan using times, costs, row counts.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

结果是 6 行：Sedan 973、SUV 838、Hatchback 794、People Mover 510、Van 384、Ute 333。顶层估计 2 行，实际 6 行；cost 为 4782.24..4782.25，实测执行 31.959 ms。

The view returns six rows. Sedan at Carindale Park leads with 973 collections, followed by SUV 838, Hatchback 794, People Mover 510, Van 384 and Ute 333. The top node estimates 2 rows but returns 6; its cost is 4782.24..4782.25 and execution takes 31.959 ms.

Parallel Seq Scan 过滤 Collect，29,769.5 行/loop × 2 = 59,539 次取车。Hash Join 补齐车型和站点，聚合为 279 个组，WindowAgg 挑每组第一，最后排序 6 个赢家。结果很少并不代表底层扫描工作很少。

A Parallel Seq Scan filters BOOKING_EVENTS to Collect events. Its 29,769.5 actual rows per loop across two loops represent 59,539 collections in total. Hash joins attach vehicle, model and station attributes. Partial aggregation and Finalize GroupAggregate reduce these events to 279 body-type/station groups; incremental sorting and WindowAgg select one winner per body type, and the final sort orders the six winners. The small final result does not avoid processing the underlying event log.

A2_1.1.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
SELECT * FROM V_BUSIEST_BAYS;
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM V_BUSIEST_BAYS;

Q1.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）建视图列2026年1月1日至6月30日费用最高的十个最终根账户及其总金额。

(1.5 marks) Create V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY with the ten root accounts liable for the largest charges dated 1 Jan–30 Jun 2026 inclusive.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

递归基础层把无 sponsor 的账号映射到自己；递归层让 child 继承 parent 的根账号。每名成员只映射到最终 root，因此每条收费事件只计一次，不在中间 sponsor 上重复累加。

The recursive seed maps each unsponsored account to itself. Each recursive step maps a sponsored child to the same root account as its parent, so every member in the supplied sponsorship forest maps to exactly one ultimately liable account. Joining charges to this mapping bills personal charges and every level of sponsored charges to the root, without counting an event at several intermediate accounts.

只计 Return 和 Incident 的收费。日期范围为 2026-01-01（含）到 2026-07-01（不含），覆盖 6 月 30 日全天；按金额降序取前 10。另一套独立的向上递归校验得到同样结果。

Only Return fares and Incident damage charges are included. The half-open timestamp range from 2026-01-01 up to, but excluding, 2026-07-01 includes all of 30 June. Totals are sorted descending, with member_id as a deterministic tie-breaker, and limited to ten accounts. The result is shown in Q1.3 Query A. An independent upward traversal produced the same ten rows.

A2_1.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
CREATE VIEW V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY AS
WITH RECURSIVE account_tree AS (
    SELECT member_id, member_id AS account_id
    FROM MEMBERS
    WHERE sponsor_id IS NULL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT child.member_id, parent.account_id
    FROM MEMBERS child
    JOIN account_tree parent ON child.sponsor_id = parent.member_id
)
SELECT root.member_id, root.member_name,
       SUM(e.amount) AS total_amount
FROM account_tree t
JOIN MEMBERS root ON root.member_id = t.account_id
JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e ON e.member_id = t.member_id
WHERE e.event_type IN ('Return', 'Incident')
  AND e.event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01'
  AND e.event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-07-01'
GROUP BY root.member_id, root.member_name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC, root.member_id
LIMIT 10;

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02CREATE VIEW V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY AS

    创建普通视图 V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY，结果列由后面的 SELECT 决定。它保存“最终由谁付款”的查询定义，不把当前前十名永久冻结。

    Create V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY as an ordinary view. Its SELECT determines the columns and recalculates ultimately liable accounts when read.

  3. 03WITH RECURSIVE account_tree AS (

    WITH RECURSIVE 允许 account_tree 在后半段引用自己的当前递归结果。这里从无赞助人的根账号往下找孩子，把每个成员映射到最终付款账号。

    WITH RECURSIVE lets account_tree refer to its current recursive rows. The traversal starts at unsponsored roots and propagates the ultimate payer down to descendants.

  4. 04 SELECT member_id, member_id AS account_id

    基础层把根账号的 member_id 同时输出为 member_id 和 account_id。前者是“当前成员”，后者是“一直保留的最终付款根”，例如根 13943 对应 (13943,13943)。

    The seed emits a root’s member_id twice: the current member and its ultimate account_id. Root 13943 therefore begins as (13943,13943).

  5. 05 FROM MEMBERS

    基础层从 MEMBERS 成员表读取，尚未访问收费日志。先建立成员到根的对应关系，再连接收费，能清楚区分层级关系与金额汇总。

    Read MEMBERS for the seed, before joining any charges. This separates hierarchy resolution from monetary aggregation.

  6. 06 WHERE sponsor_id IS NULL

    sponsor_id 为 NULL 的成员没有上级，需要自己付款，因此是根节点。判断 NULL 必须用 IS NULL，写 = NULL 不会得到期望的真值。

    Members with sponsor_id IS NULL are self-paying roots. IS NULL is required because ordinary equality with NULL does not test for nullness.

  7. 07 UNION ALL

    UNION ALL 合并基础层和后面一轮轮找出的孩子，不做去重。对给定的无环、每人至多一个 sponsor 的层级，每个成员只有一条根路径；这段代码本身不是通用的环检测器。

    UNION ALL appends recursive descendants without deduplication. In the supplied acyclic single-sponsor hierarchy, each member has one root path; this query is not a general cycle detector.

  8. 08 SELECT child.member_id, parent.account_id

    每次输出孩子自己的 member_id，但 account_id 仍复制父行携带的根账号。不能在此改成 parent.member_id，否则深层成员会被错误归到直接父母。

    Emit the child’s own member_id but retain the parent row’s ultimate account_id. Using parent.member_id instead would incorrectly bill deeper descendants to an intermediate sponsor.

  9. 09 FROM MEMBERS child

    把成员表中的候选孩子命名为 child。它们可能属于任意一层；是否接上当前递归结果，由下一行的连接条件决定。

    Read candidate child members from MEMBERS. The next join decides which ones descend from the current recursive frontier.

  10. 10 JOIN account_tree parent ON child.sponsor_id = parent.member_id

    child.sponsor_id 等于当前 parent.member_id 时，说明这个成员直接受该 parent 赞助；递归继续往下扩展。parent 是 CTE 的递归行别名，不一定是最终根。

    Match a child’s sponsor_id to a current parent member_id and extend the traversal. parent names a recursive row, which may itself be an intermediate account.

  11. 11)

    结束递归 CTE。此时给定数据中的 24,000 名成员各有一个 account_id；后面的聚合使用这份映射，递归本身不负责金额相加。

    Close the recursive CTE. The supplied data maps all 24,000 members to account_id; monetary aggregation happens afterward.

  12. 12SELECT root.member_id, root.member_name,

    输出根账号的 ID 和姓名，而不是产生费用的下属姓名。root 是下一步连接得到的最终付款成员记录。

    Select the root account’s identifier and name, not the descendant who incurred the charge. root will identify that ultimately liable member.

  13. 13 SUM(e.amount) AS total_amount

    SUM(e.amount) 把同一根账号下的有效收费相加，结果列叫 total_amount。SUM 会忽略 NULL；下面仍显式限定 Return 和 Incident 来表达业务含义。

    Sum charge amounts for each root as total_amount. SUM ignores NULL, while the explicit event-type filter states which events are charges.

  14. 14FROM account_tree t

    给 account_tree 结果别名 t。t.member_id 表示收费成员，t.account_id 表示该成员最终归属的付款账号。

    Read account_tree as t: t.member_id is the charged member and t.account_id is the ultimately liable account.

  15. 15JOIN MEMBERS root ON root.member_id = t.account_id

    用 t.account_id 找到 MEMBERS 中的根账号 root，取得它的姓名。不能把 root 接到 t.member_id，否则输出会变成每位驾驶成员的个人账单。

    Join t.account_id to the root member record for its name. Joining root to t.member_id instead would report individual drivers rather than ultimate payers.

  16. 16JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e ON e.member_id = t.member_id

    把每条收费事件按 e.member_id 接到映射中的收费成员 t.member_id。一次事件只接到它唯一的根映射，避免在多个祖先账户重复累加。

    Join each event’s member_id to the charged member in the mapping. Its unique root mapping prevents counting one event once at each ancestor.

  17. 17WHERE e.event_type IN ('Return', 'Incident')

    只保留 Return 车费和 Incident 损坏收费。Reserve、Collect、Cancel 不属于本题费用事件，即使它们与同一成员有关也不应计入收费总额。

    Keep Return fares and Incident damage charges, excluding Reserve, Collect and Cancel from the monetary totals.

  18. 18 AND e.event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01'

    使用带类型的 TIMESTAMP 字面量，从 2026-01-01 零点起包含记录。>= 保留恰好在年初零点发生的收费。

    Use a typed TIMESTAMP lower bound and include records at or after midnight on 1 January 2026.

  19. 19 AND e.event_time < TIMESTAMP '2026-07-01'

    排除 2026-07-01 零点及之后，因而包含 6 月 30 日的全部时间。这种半开区间不会像 <= 2026-06-30 零点那样漏掉最后一天后续事件。

    Exclude midnight on 1 July and later, thereby including all of 30 June. The half-open interval avoids truncating the final day at its midnight.

  20. 20GROUP BY root.member_id, root.member_name

    按最终根账号分组，同一个根名下的自己、子账号及更深层成员的费用合成一行。member_id 是主键，也避免把同名但不同 ID 的人合并。

    Group charges by the ultimate root account, including its own and all descendants’ charges. The identifier keeps distinct people with equal names separate.

  21. 21ORDER BY total_amount DESC, root.member_id

    先按总金额降序，金额相同再按根 member_id 升序。这个稳定的次排序让恰好前十名在并列边界时仍有确定选择。

    Sort total liability descending, then member_id ascending to make selection deterministic at a tied top-ten boundary.

  22. 22LIMIT 10;

    LIMIT 10 在排序后只保留最高的十个根账户。不能先截取十条收费事件或十个成员再求和，否则会改变题目要计算的全体费用。

    After sorting, retain the ten highest-liability root accounts. Limiting charge rows or members before aggregation would compute the wrong population.

实际输出 / Captured output · 1.2_1
CREATE VIEW V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY AS
WITH RECURSIVE account_tree AS (
    SELECT member_id, member_id AS account_id
    FROM MEMBERS
    WHERE sponsor_id IS NULL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT child.member_id, parent.account_id
    FROM MEMBERS child
    JOIN account_tree parent ON child.sponsor_id = parent.member_id
)
SELECT root.member_id, root.member_name,
       SUM(e.amount) AS total_amount
FROM account_tree t
JOIN MEMBERS root ON root.member_id = t.account_id
JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e ON e.member_id = t.member_id
WHERE e.event_type IN ('Return', 'Incident')
  AND e.event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01'
  AND e.event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-07-01'
GROUP BY root.member_id, root.member_name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC, root.member_id
LIMIT 10;

Q1.3.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0分）用与普通视图相同的查询建立物化视图。

(0 marks) Create MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY using the same query as the ordinary view.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

用与 V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY 完全相同的递归查询创建 MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY。CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW 执行时就计算并保存结果，本次保存 10 行；之后读取不必每次重算。

Create MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY using the same recursive query as the ordinary view. Creation computes and stores ten rows, which later reads can scan without repeating the derivation.

A2_1.3.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY AS
WITH RECURSIVE account_tree AS (
    SELECT member_id, member_id AS account_id
    FROM MEMBERS
    WHERE sponsor_id IS NULL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT child.member_id, parent.account_id
    FROM MEMBERS child
    JOIN account_tree parent ON child.sponsor_id = parent.member_id
)
SELECT root.member_id, root.member_name,
       SUM(e.amount) AS total_amount
FROM account_tree t
JOIN MEMBERS root ON root.member_id = t.account_id
JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e ON e.member_id = t.member_id
WHERE e.event_type IN ('Return', 'Incident')
  AND e.event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01'
  AND e.event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-07-01'
GROUP BY root.member_id, root.member_name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC, root.member_id
LIMIT 10;

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY AS
WITH RECURSIVE account_tree AS (
    SELECT member_id, member_id AS account_id
    FROM MEMBERS
    WHERE sponsor_id IS NULL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT child.member_id, parent.account_id
    FROM MEMBERS child
    JOIN account_tree parent ON child.sponsor_id = parent.member_id
)
SELECT root.member_id, root.member_name,
       SUM(e.amount) AS total_amount
FROM account_tree t
JOIN MEMBERS root ON root.member_id = t.account_id
JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e ON e.member_id = t.member_id
WHERE e.event_type IN ('Return', 'Incident')
  AND e.event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01'
  AND e.event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-07-01'
GROUP BY root.member_id, root.member_name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC, root.member_id
LIMIT 10;

Q1.3.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）比较普通与物化视图的查询及计划。

(0.5 marks) Compare Query A ordinary-view SELECT and Query B materialised-view SELECT, including both plans.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

两个查询最初都返回相同 10 行，Ana Lopez（14881）以 6,214.77 居首。普通视图展开为递归、连接、聚合和 top-N 排序；建立 24,000 人的映射，处理 30,275 条期间内收费事件；cost 389551.20..389551.22，执行 48.775 ms。

Both queries initially return the same ten accounts, led by Ana Lopez (14881) with 6,214.77. Query A expands the ordinary view into Recursive Union, joins, HashAggregate and a top-N sort. It builds the 24,000-member account mapping and aggregates 30,275 eligible events. Its top cost is 389551.20..389551.22; execution takes 48.775 ms.

物化视图只扫描已存的 10 行，cost 0.00..18.50，执行 0.055 ms。新关系还未 ANALYZE，估计 850 行不等于实际存了 850 行。读取节省重算；若要求保证显示顺序，读取保存的关系时仍应明确 ORDER BY。

Query B scans only the stored ten-row materialized result, with cost 0.00..18.50 and execution time 0.055 ms. The plan estimates 850 rows but actually returns 10 because the newly created materialized relation has not been analyzed. This estimate does not mean that 850 result rows were stored. The materialized view avoids recomputing recursion, joins and aggregation at read time. SQL consumers needing guaranteed output order should use an explicit ORDER BY when reading a stored relation.

A2_1.3.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- Query A
SELECT * FROM V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
-- Query B
SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    EXPLAIN 显示“the ordinary liability view”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    Read all result columns from MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY. This SELECT displays tuples; the separate EXPLAIN ANALYZE studies execution, and the two runs may see different cache state and timing.

  7. 07EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    EXPLAIN 显示“the materialized liability view”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for the materialized liability view. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

Q1.3.3

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）插入指定90000损坏费用，重查两视图，刷新后再查物化视图并解释取舍。

(0.5 marks) Insert the supplied 90000 Incident, re query both views, refresh and re query the materialised view; explain the trade-off.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

新增 90,000 费用属于 member 24000；沿 24000 → 22086 → 18148 → 13943 归到 Tuan Haddad，总额变为 90,673.75。普通视图立刻把他排第一；物化视图刷新前仍显示以 Ana Lopez 居首的旧榜单，刷新后才一致，原第 10 名账号 397 被挤出。

The supplied INSERT chooses member 24000. Its sponsorship chain is 24000 -> 22086 -> 18148 -> 13943, so the additional 90,000.00 is billed to Tuan Haddad (13943). Query A immediately puts that account first at 90,673.75. Query B still shows the earlier top ten, led by Ana Lopez, and does not include Tuan Haddad. After REFRESH, Query B shows the same updated ten rows as Query A; account 397 drops out of the top ten.

物化视图以额外存储、刷新开销和刷新间隔内的数据陈旧换取更快的读取；普通视图根据每次语句可见的数据重新计算。

A materialized view trades faster reads for extra storage, refresh work and potentially stale answers between refreshes. An ordinary view computes an answer from the data visible to each query.

A2_1.3.3.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
INSERT INTO BOOKING_EVENTS (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)
SELECT member_id, 'VEH-0001', 'Incident', TIMESTAMP '2026-05-04 09:15:00', 90000.00
FROM MEMBERS WHERE sponsor_id IS NOT NULL ORDER BY member_id DESC LIMIT 1;
-- Query A reflects the inserted charge immediately.
SELECT * FROM V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
-- Query B still contains its stored result.
SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;
-- Query B after refresh.
SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02INSERT INTO BOOKING_EVENTS (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)

    INSERT INTO 指定要往事件表写入的五列，后面的 SELECT 为它产生一行值。没有写 event_id，让 SERIAL 的默认序列自动生成事件 ID。

    INSERT INTO lists the five target event columns; the following SELECT supplies a row. Omitted event_id uses its SERIAL default sequence.

  3. 03SELECT member_id, 'VEH-0001', 'Incident', TIMESTAMP '2026-05-04 09:15:00', 90000.00

    为选中的成员生成 VEH-0001、Incident、指定时间和 90000.00 五个值，与 INSERT 列顺序对应。这里用题目指定的 5 月时间，属于 Q1.2 的计费区间；不是把损坏费分摊给所有成员。

    Build one row with the selected member, VEH-0001, Incident, the specified May timestamp and 90000.00 in target-column order. The charge lies within the liability window and is not assigned to every member.

  4. 04FROM MEMBERS WHERE sponsor_id IS NOT NULL ORDER BY member_id DESC LIMIT 1;

    从有 sponsor 的成员中按 member_id 降序取 1 人，实际为 24000。WHERE 确保选中被赞助成员，ORDER BY DESC 与 LIMIT 1 决定唯一被收费的人；递归再把费用传到根 13943。

    Choose the highest-ID sponsored member, 24000. WHERE requires sponsorship and descending order plus LIMIT 1 selects one person; recursion later bills root 13943.

  5. 05-- Query A reflects the inserted charge immediately.

    注释提醒下一次查询应反映新收费。它本身既不刷新视图，也不产生任何结果。

    This comment introduces the ordinary-view rerun; it performs no refresh or query itself.

  6. 06SELECT * FROM V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    读取 V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY 的全部结果列。这个 SELECT 才用于展示结果；下一条 EXPLAIN ANALYZE 另用于研究执行方式，两次运行的缓存和计时可能不同。

    Read all result columns from V_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY. This SELECT displays tuples; the separate EXPLAIN ANALYZE studies execution, and the two runs may see different cache state and timing.

  7. 07-- Query B still contains its stored result.

    注释指出此刻物化视图仍保存创建时的旧结果，用于对照陈旧性。

    The comment introduces the still-stale stored result for comparison.

  8. 08SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    读取 MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY 的全部结果列。这个 SELECT 才用于展示结果；下一条 EXPLAIN ANALYZE 另用于研究执行方式，两次运行的缓存和计时可能不同。

    Read all result columns from MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY. This SELECT displays tuples; the separate EXPLAIN ANALYZE studies execution, and the two runs may see different cache state and timing.

  9. 09REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    REFRESH 重新执行物化视图的定义并替换其已保存结果。它刷新的是整个定义结果，不是只手动给某行加 90000；刷新后前十名单也可能变化。

    REFRESH reruns the defining query and replaces the stored result, rather than manually adding 90000 to one row. Membership of the top ten may change.

  10. 10-- Query B after refresh.

    注释标记刷新后的最后一次读取，以便与刷新前的 Query B 区分。

    Label the final read after refresh, distinct from the stale Query B read.

  11. 11SELECT * FROM MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY;

    读取 MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY after refresh 的全部结果列。这个 SELECT 才用于展示结果；下一条 EXPLAIN ANALYZE 另用于研究执行方式，两次运行的缓存和计时可能不同。

    Read all result columns from MV_ACCOUNT_LIABILITY after refresh. This SELECT displays tuples; the separate EXPLAIN ANALYZE studies execution, and the two runs may see different cache state and timing.

Q2 索引与选择率Indexes and selectivity · 8 marks

回到课程专题：索引与 B+ 树 / Related course topic →

Q2.1.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）按event_type,event_time顺序建指定复合索引。

(0.5 marks) Create IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME on BOOKING_EVENTS(event_type, event_time) in that order.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

创建 B-tree 复合索引，键顺序必须是 event_type 在前、event_time 在后。它先按事件类型排序，再在同一种类型内部按时间排序。

This B-tree index has event_type as its first key and event_time as its second key, in the required order. It orders timestamps within each event type.

A2_2.1.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
CREATE INDEX IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME
ON BOOKING_EVENTS (event_type, event_time);
下载本题 SQL / Download SQL ↗
每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 3 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02CREATE INDEX IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME

    创建名为 IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME 的索引。没有指定 USING 时 PostgreSQL 默认使用 B-tree；创建索引增加一种可选访问路径，不强迫查询使用它。

    Create IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME using PostgreSQL’s default B-tree access method. It adds an available path rather than forcing its use.

  3. 03ON BOOKING_EVENTS (event_type, event_time);

    在 BOOKING_EVENTS 上依次索引 event_type、event_time。先固定类型，再限制其内部时间范围很有效；两列不是两份独立单列索引，后续写入也要维护这棵索引。

    Index event_type first and event_time second on BOOKING_EVENTS. Type equality plus an internal time range is effective; this is one composite index, and writes must maintain it.

Q2.1.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）重跑最忙站点查询，比较索引前后计划。

(1.5 marks) Rerun the busiest-bays query and compare its plan with Q1.1.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

结果仍是 6 行，计划仍是并行顺序扫描、Hash Join、聚合与排名，没有使用新索引。顶层 cost 从 4782.24..4782.25 到 4782.25..4782.25，实测从 31.959 到 31.582 ms。这点差异不能证明索引带来稳定收益。

The six result rows are unchanged. The plan remains a Parallel Seq Scan of the event log followed by hash joins, aggregation, ranking and sorting; IDX_EVENT_TYPE_TIME is not used. Its top cost is 4782.25..4782.25 versus 4782.24..4782.25 before the index, and execution is 31.582 ms versus 31.959 ms. These small timing differences are single-run variation, not evidence of an index benefit.

Collect 占 59,539 / 196,441，约 30.31%，分散在大量 heap 页上，且还要取索引不含的 vehicle_id。索引访问会加上索引遍历和大量 heap 读取；并行顺序读更合适。查询没有时间条件，所以第二列也无法缩小范围。

Collect matches 59,539 of the 196,441 events now present, about 30.31 percent. Those events are distributed throughout the heap, and the query also needs vehicle_id, which this index does not contain. An index path would traverse many index entries and fetch many heap pages, whereas a parallel sequential scan reads the table efficiently. There is no timestamp condition, so the second index column does not narrow this query. Adding an index gives the planner another option; it does not require its use.

A2_2.1.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
SELECT * FROM V_BUSIEST_BAYS;
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM V_BUSIEST_BAYS;

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Repeat exactly the Q1.1 SELECT after adding the composite index. Holding query semantics constant makes the access-path comparison meaningful.

  3. 03EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM V_BUSIEST_BAYS;

    EXPLAIN 显示“V_BUSIEST_BAYS”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

Q2.1.3

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）解释2026年3月Incident计数及金额查询的索引使用。

(1 marks) Explain count (*) and sum (amount) for Incident in March 2026 using the composite index.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

3 月 Incident 为 116 次，总金额 86,382.67。聚合估计与实际都是 1 行，cost 410.92..410.93，执行 0.292 ms。Bitmap Index Scan 找到 116 个位置（估计 132），随后访问 79 个 exact heap blocks。

The query returns count 116 and sum 86,382.67. The aggregate estimates and returns one result row, with cost 410.92..410.93 and execution time 0.292 ms. Its child Bitmap Index Scan finds 116 matching entries, versus 132 estimated, and Bitmap Heap Scan visits 79 exact heap blocks before computing the aggregate.

event_type 等值固定第一列，3 月上下界限定第二列，形成很短的索引范围。只匹配约 0.059% 的事件，远比全部 Collect 更有选择性。amount 不在索引中，所以仍需要 heap 访问。

Incident equality fixes the leading key, and the lower and upper March timestamp bounds restrict the second key to a short contiguous range within Incident entries. These 116 events are about 0.059 percent of the table, so the bitmap index path avoids most rows and pages. The plan needs heap access for amount, which is not stored in the index. This is much more selective than the all-time Collect query in Q2.1 part 2.

A2_2.1.3.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
SELECT count(*), sum(amount) FROM BOOKING_EVENTS
WHERE event_type = 'Incident'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-03-01'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-04-01';
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT count(*), sum(amount) FROM BOOKING_EVENTS
WHERE event_type = 'Incident'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-03-01'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMP '2026-04-01';

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02SELECT count(*), sum(amount) FROM BOOKING_EVENTS

    COUNT(*) 数匹配事件，SUM(amount) 加总这些事件的金额；没有 GROUP BY 时对全部匹配行返回一个聚合结果。本次为 116 与 86382.67，不是 116 行结果。

    Count matching events and sum their amounts. Without GROUP BY this returns one aggregate row: 116 and 86382.67, not 116 output rows.

  3. 03WHERE event_type = 'Incident'

    固定复合索引的第一列为 Incident，先排除其他事件类型。它不是 LIKE 模糊匹配。

    Fix the composite index’s leading event_type to Incident using equality, excluding other types.

  4. 04 AND event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-03-01'

    时间下界含 2026-03-01 零点，约束索引的第二列。

    Apply an inclusive lower bound at midnight on 1 March 2026 to the second index key.

  5. 05 AND event_time < TIMESTAMP '2026-04-01';

    时间上界不含 2026-04-01 零点，完整包含 3 月最后一天，不依赖写出当月最后一秒。

    Apply an exclusive upper bound at midnight on 1 April, including all of March without guessing its last representable second.

  6. 06EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT count(*), sum(amount) FROM BOOKING_EVENTS

    EXPLAIN 显示“the same March Incident aggregate”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for the same March Incident aggregate. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

  7. 07WHERE event_type = 'Incident'

    固定复合索引的第一列为 Incident，先排除其他事件类型。它不是 LIKE 模糊匹配。

    Fix the composite index’s leading event_type to Incident using equality, excluding other types.

  8. 08 AND event_time >= TIMESTAMP '2026-03-01'

    时间下界含 2026-03-01 零点，约束索引的第二列。

    Apply an inclusive lower bound at midnight on 1 March 2026 to the second index key.

  9. 09 AND event_time < TIMESTAMP '2026-04-01';

    时间上界不含 2026-04-01 零点，完整包含 3 月最后一天，不依赖写出当月最后一秒。

    Apply an exclusive upper bound at midnight on 1 April, including all of March without guessing its last representable second.

Q2.2.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）提取不区分大小写的邮箱域，按成员数降序统计。

(1 marks) Extract case-insensitive provider domains and count members per domain, most popular first.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) 先取域名，再统一小写，避免把 Bigpond.com 与 bigpond.com 分成两组。12 个域名的总数是 24,000；gmail.com 以 8,164 人最多，exemail.com.au 为 248 人。人数相同时按域名排序，便于复查。

LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) extracts the part after @ and normalizes its case before grouping. The query reports 12 domains accounting for all 24,000 members, with gmail.com first at 8,164 members. exemail.com.au has 248 members. The secondary alphabetical ordering makes equal counts reproducible.

A2_2.2.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,
       COUNT(*) AS member_count
FROM MEMBERS
GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))
ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;

每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 6 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,

    SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2) 取 @ 分隔后的第二段，再由 LOWER 统一小写；provider_domain 是输出列名。本数据使用正常的单 @ 邮箱，表达式不负责校验任意格式的邮箱。

    Extract the second @-separated field and normalize it with LOWER, naming it provider_domain. This assumes the dataset’s normal single-@ addresses; it is not an email validator.

  3. 03 COUNT(*) AS member_count

    COUNT(*) 数每个规范化域名下的成员行，输出名为 member_count。每位成员一行，即使 local part 不同，只要域名相同也会进入同一组。

    COUNT(*) counts member rows per normalized domain as member_count. Different address prefixes still contribute to the same domain group.

  4. 04FROM MEMBERS

    从 MEMBERS 读全部成员。这里没有稀有域名 WHERE，因为问题要求统计所有不同 provider，而不只某一家。

    Read every member from MEMBERS. There is no rare-domain WHERE because the question asks for all providers.

  5. 05GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))

    按同一个 LOWER(SPLIT_PART(...)) 表达式分组。若只在 SELECT 中 LOWER、分组却用原字符串，大小写变体仍可能先被拆成不同组。

    Group by exactly the same normalized expression. Normalizing only the displayed SELECT value would not correctly merge groups formed from differently cased raw strings.

  6. 06ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;

    按 member_count 降序把最常用域名排前；同人数按域名升序。最终 12 组共覆盖 24,000 人；索引按域名排序也不能直接提供按人数排序。

    Sort counts descending and domain names ascending for ties. Twelve groups cover 24,000 members; domain index order does not directly order aggregate counts.

Q2.2.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）报告并解释全域聚合计划。

(1 marks) Report and explain the plan of the domain aggregation.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

计划为 Seq Scan → HashAggregate → Sort：扫描 24,000 人，计算域名并聚合，最终排序 12 组。顶层 cost 2864.09..2924.09，执行 4.736 ms。估计 24,000 组，实际 12 组，反映当时缺少派生表达式的统计信息。

The plan performs a Seq Scan of all 24,000 MEMBERS rows, computes the normalized domain, uses HashAggregate for the counts, and sorts the resulting 12 groups by count descending. The top cost is 2864.09..2924.09 and execution time is 4.736 ms. It estimates 24,000 groups but actually returns only 12, reflecting the lack of statistics for this derived expression at this point.

所有人都要贡献到全体域名计数，所以完整扫描合理。HashAggregate 只有一个 batch，最终排序只用 25 kB，本次都没有溢出到磁盘。

Every member contributes to the requested whole-population counts, so a complete scan is appropriate. Hash aggregation has a single batch and the final quicksort uses only 25 kB; neither step spills to disk in this run.

A2_2.2.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,
       COUNT(*) AS member_count
FROM MEMBERS
GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))
ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;

Q2.2.3

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）解释完整email唯一索引为何不能直接按域条件定位。

(1 marks) Explain why the existing UNIQUE email index cannot directly answer provider-domain predicates.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

email 的 UNIQUE B-tree 按完整邮箱从第一个字符开始排序，同域名地址可能散落在不同用户名后面，因此不能按规范化域名直接定位或提供域名分组顺序。即使完整扫描 email 索引后再提取域名，也仍要检查所有成员。

The UNIQUE B-tree on email orders complete addresses from their first character, so members sharing a domain can be scattered across many different address prefixes. It cannot provide a selective lookup or grouping order on the normalized domain expression; although a full index-only scan could still read every email and compute the domain, it would still have to examine the whole population.

Q2.2.4

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）以相同域表达式创建指定索引。

(0.5 marks) Create IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN on the same domain expression.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

表达式索引必须与查询使用同一个 LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) 表达式，包括大小写规范化。这样优化器才能用计算好的域名键直接定位。

The expression in IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN exactly matches LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) in the queries, including the case folding. PostgreSQL can therefore look up a normalized domain directly in this index.

A2_2.2.4.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
CREATE INDEX IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN
ON MEMBERS (LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)));

每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 3 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02CREATE INDEX IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN

    创建函数/表达式索引 IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN。它保存计算出来的域名键，区别于已有 email UNIQUE 索引保存完整邮箱。

    Create expression index IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN, whose keys are computed domains rather than complete email addresses.

  3. 03ON MEMBERS (LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)));

    对 MEMBERS 的 LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) 建索引，表达式要与查询一致。双层括号区分索引列列表与表达式；此处不会新增一个实体 provider_domain 列。

    Index the matching normalized-domain expression on MEMBERS. Parentheses delimit the index key expression; this does not add a stored provider_domain column.

CREATE INDEX IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN
ON MEMBERS (LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)));

原始日志 / Raw transcript ↗（原资料留在本地版）输出截图 / Output screenshots ↗（原资料留在本地版）

Q2.2.5

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）有表达式索引时比较全域汇总与稀有exemail.com.au查找。

(1.5 marks) Compare plans with the expression index for aggregation and rare ex email. com. au lookup.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

全量域名汇总仍是 Seq Scan → HashAggregate → Sort，估计 24,000 组，实际 12 组；cost 仍为 2864.09..2924.09，执行 4.825 ms，索引前为 4.736 ms。每个人都要参与计数，按域名排好的索引也不能直接满足 COUNT(*) 的降序。

The domain-count query keeps its Seq Scan -> HashAggregate -> Sort plan, estimates 24,000 groups and returns 12. Its top cost stays 2864.09..2924.09; execution is 4.825 ms compared with 4.736 ms before the index. All 24,000 members still have to contribute to the counts, and an index ordered by domain cannot directly satisfy ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC.

稀有域名查询使用 Bitmap Index Scan + Bitmap Heap Scan，返回 248 人（约 1.03%，估计 120），cost 5.22..226.08，执行 0.440 ms，访问 164 个 exact heap blocks。仍需从 heap 取 id、姓名和邮箱，但省去了对全体成员逐个计算和判断域名。

The rare-provider lookup instead uses Bitmap Index Scan on IDX_MEMBER_DOMAIN followed by Bitmap Heap Scan. It returns 248 members, about 1.03 percent of MEMBERS, versus 120 estimated, with cost 5.22..226.08 and execution time 0.440 ms. The bitmap batches access to 164 exact heap blocks; heap access is needed for member_id, member_name and email. The index avoids evaluating the domain predicate on every member. Its advantage is selective access, rather than speeding every query that mentions the expression.

A2_2.2.5.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,
       COUNT(*) AS member_count
FROM MEMBERS
GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))
ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;
SELECT member_id, member_name, email FROM MEMBERS
WHERE LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) = 'exemail.com.au';
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT member_id, member_name, email FROM MEMBERS
WHERE LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) = 'exemail.com.au';

每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 10 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,

    EXPLAIN 显示“the full provider aggregation with the expression index present”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for the full provider aggregation with the expression index present. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

  3. 03 COUNT(*) AS member_count

    COUNT(*) 数每个规范化域名下的成员行，输出名为 member_count。每位成员一行，即使 local part 不同，只要域名相同也会进入同一组。

    COUNT(*) counts member rows per normalized domain as member_count. Different address prefixes still contribute to the same domain group.

  4. 04FROM MEMBERS

    从 MEMBERS 读全部成员。这里没有稀有域名 WHERE，因为问题要求统计所有不同 provider，而不只某一家。

    Read every member from MEMBERS. There is no rare-domain WHERE because the question asks for all providers.

  5. 05GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))

    按同一个 LOWER(SPLIT_PART(...)) 表达式分组。若只在 SELECT 中 LOWER、分组却用原字符串，大小写变体仍可能先被拆成不同组。

    Group by exactly the same normalized expression. Normalizing only the displayed SELECT value would not correctly merge groups formed from differently cased raw strings.

  6. 06ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;

    按 member_count 降序把最常用域名排前；同人数按域名升序。最终 12 组共覆盖 24,000 人；索引按域名排序也不能直接提供按人数排序。

    Sort counts descending and domain names ascending for ties. Twelve groups cover 24,000 members; domain index order does not directly order aggregate counts.

  7. 07SELECT member_id, member_name, email FROM MEMBERS

    稀有域名查询只输出成员 ID、姓名、完整邮箱。它需要索引键以外的数据，所以这份表达式索引本身不能直接提供全部输出列。

    The selective lookup projects member ID, name and full address. These columns are not all supplied by the domain expression index alone.

  8. 08WHERE LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) = 'exemail.com.au';

    WHERE 使用与索引完全相同的规范化表达式，只要 exemail.com.au。匹配 248 人而不是全体 24,000 人，因此与全量聚合的访问需求不同。

    Match the indexed normalized expression to exemail.com.au. Selecting 248 of 24,000 members differs materially from aggregating the whole table.

  9. 09EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT member_id, member_name, email FROM MEMBERS

    EXPLAIN 显示“the rare-provider lookup”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for the rare-provider lookup. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

  10. 10WHERE LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) = 'exemail.com.au';

    EXPLAIN 所研究的 WHERE 与刚才普通 SELECT 一样，保证计划和所展示的结果对应同一谓词。本次使用 bitmap 索引与 heap 访问，而不是全表逐个计算域名。

    Use the same predicate in EXPLAIN as in the displayed SELECT. The observed plan uses bitmap index and heap access rather than evaluating every member’s domain.

完整执行计划 / Full query plan · 2.2.5_1
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2)) AS provider_domain,
       COUNT(*) AS member_count
FROM MEMBERS
GROUP BY LOWER(SPLIT_PART(email, '@', 2))
ORDER BY member_count DESC, provider_domain;
