Q3 查询改写与执行计划Query formulation and plans · 7 marks

回到课程专题：查询优化与执行计划 / Related course topic →

Q3.1.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）分别用NOT EXISTS与不含NOT EXISTS/NOT IN的方法列从未Collect车辆，两者结果相同。

(1.5 marks) List never-Collected vehicle ids with NOT EXISTS and without NOT EXISTS/NOT IN, returning identical rows.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

NOT EXISTS 与 LEFT JOIN 后保留 e.vehicle_id IS NULL 都返回相同的 75 辆车。外连接中的 Collect 条件必须放在 ON 中；若放入 WHERE，会错误丢掉没有匹配的车辆。

Both statements return the same 75 vehicle IDs, ordered by vehicle_id. Statement A uses a correlated NOT EXISTS test. Statement B places the Collect predicate in the LEFT JOIN condition and keeps rows with no matching event vehicle_id. The matched-side vehicle_id is NOT NULL in the schema, so NULL identifies an unmatched vehicle unambiguously.

匹配侧的 vehicle_id 在 schema 中非空，因此 NULL 能可靠表示没有匹配。独立的双向 EXCEPT ALL 检查未发现差异；这不是依靠肉眼比较前几行得出的结论。

Putting event_type in WHERE instead of ON would remove the unmatched rows and break the outer-join solution. Independent EXCEPT ALL checks in both directions found no differences. The screenshots show the first ten and last ten rows of each 75-row result.

A2_3.1.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- Statement A
SELECT v.vehicle_id
FROM VEHICLES v
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
      AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
)
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;
-- Statement B
SELECT v.vehicle_id
FROM VEHICLES v
LEFT JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e
  ON e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
WHERE e.vehicle_id IS NULL
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;
下载本题 SQL / Download SQL ↗
每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 18 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02-- Statement A

    注释把接下来的查询标为 Statement A，方便与 B 比较；-- 到行末都不执行。

    This comment labels Statement A for comparison with B; text following -- is not executed.

  3. 03SELECT v.vehicle_id

    只输出题目要求的车辆编号。查询外层的 v 表示“正在判断是否从未被取用的那辆车”。

    Return only the required vehicle identifier. Outer alias v is the candidate vehicle whose collection history is being tested.

  4. 04FROM VEHICLES v

    从完整 VEHICLES 表出发，包含没有事件或只有预约/取消事件的车辆。若从 Collect 日志出发，反而找不到“从未出现”的车辆。

    Start from the entire fleet, including vehicles with no events or only reservations/cancellations. Starting from Collect events would omit the very absences being sought.

  5. 05WHERE NOT EXISTS (

    NOT EXISTS 要求括号内找不到任何匹配行。它回答“是否不存在一次 Collect”，而不是检查车辆的最后一个事件。

    NOT EXISTS requires no matching inner row. It tests absence of any Collect, not merely the type of the latest event.

  6. 06 SELECT 1 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e

    SELECT 1 只是为 EXISTS 构造一行；1 的数值没有业务意义，也不是计数。EXISTS 只关心有没有符合条件的行，不关心输出的列值。

    SELECT 1 supplies an arbitrary value for EXISTS; it is not a count. EXISTS cares only whether a qualifying row exists, not its selected value.

  7. 07 WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id

    只看与外层这辆 v 相同 vehicle_id 的事件，形成相关子查询。如果漏掉相关条件，一辆车被取用就可能让所有候选都被排除。

    Correlate inner events to the current outer vehicle. Omitting this condition could make one Collect event incorrectly disqualify every candidate.

  8. 08 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'

    还必须是 Collect，其他事件不证明车辆真正被驾驶。只有同车且 Collect 的事件存在时，外层 NOT EXISTS 才为假。

    Require Collect as well: other events do not prove the car was driven. A same-vehicle Collect makes the outer NOT EXISTS false.

  9. 09)

    关闭 NOT EXISTS 子查询。这个逻辑相关性不强制物理计划逐车完整重扫；优化器可以改写成 anti-join。

    Close the NOT EXISTS subquery. Logical correlation does not force a full rescan per vehicle; the optimizer can transform it into an anti-join.

  10. 10ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

    按车辆 ID 排序，便于稳定比对 A 与 B 的全部 75 行，而不是只看前几行。没有 ORDER BY 时，SQL 不保证结果顺序相同。

    Sort identifiers for stable comparison of all 75 rows from A and B. Without ORDER BY, equal sets need not appear in equal order.

  11. 11-- Statement B

    注释标记 Statement B；它使用外连接表达同一个“没有 Collect”的问题。

    Label Statement B, which expresses the same absence test using an outer join.

  12. 12SELECT v.vehicle_id

    只输出题目要求的车辆编号。查询外层的 v 表示“正在判断是否从未被取用的那辆车”。

    Return only the required vehicle identifier. Outer alias v is the candidate vehicle whose collection history is being tested.

  13. 13FROM VEHICLES v

    从完整 VEHICLES 表出发，包含没有事件或只有预约/取消事件的车辆。若从 Collect 日志出发，反而找不到“从未出现”的车辆。

    Start from the entire fleet, including vehicles with no events or only reservations/cancellations. Starting from Collect events would omit the very absences being sought.

  14. 14LEFT JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e

    LEFT JOIN 保留左侧的所有车辆。匹配不到右侧事件时，右侧的 e 列会被补成 NULL，为后面的未匹配检查提供依据。

    LEFT JOIN preserves every vehicle, filling right-side event columns with NULL when no matching event exists.

  15. 15 ON e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id

    连接条件先限定事件属于同一辆车。此条件决定哪些事件能和车辆配对，不是在最终结果上检查 NULL。

    The first ON condition pairs an event with the same vehicle. It defines matches before the later unmatched-row filter.

  16. 16 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'

    Collect 条件继续放在 ON 中，只把取车事件视作匹配。若把它移到 WHERE，无匹配行的 e.event_type 为 NULL，会被过滤掉，破坏反连接语义。

    Keep the Collect predicate inside ON. Moving it to WHERE would discard unmatched rows with NULL event_type and break the anti-join.

  17. 17WHERE e.vehicle_id IS NULL

    e.vehicle_id 在真实事件中 NOT NULL，所以这一列为 NULL 能可靠识别 LEFT JOIN 没有匹配的车辆。匹配过 Collect 的车辆不会通过这个条件。

    Real event vehicle_id values are NOT NULL, so NULL here reliably identifies an unmatched vehicle. Vehicles having a Collect match fail this condition.

  18. 18ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

    按车辆 ID 排序，便于稳定比对 A 与 B 的全部 75 行，而不是只看前几行。没有 ORDER BY 时，SQL 不保证结果顺序相同。

    Sort identifiers for stable comparison of all 75 rows from A and B. Without ORDER BY, equal sets need not appear in equal order.

原始日志 / Raw transcript ↗（原资料留在本地版）输出截图 / Output screenshots ↗（原资料留在本地版）

Q3.1.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）比较两个查询计划，解释反连接改写。

(1.5 marks) Compare both execution plans and explain any anti-join transformation.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

两种写法得到相同的 Hash Right Anti Join 和最终排序，cost 都为 4579.18..4579.30；估计 48 行，实际 75 行。执行时间分别为 12.788 和 12.558 ms。

The two plans have the same operator structure and cost 4579.18..4579.30. Both return 75 rows, versus 48 estimated, using Hash Right Anti Join and a final vehicle_id sort. Statement A takes 12.788 ms and Statement B 12.558 ms in these runs.

优化器把 NOT EXISTS 去相关，并识别外连接的 NULL 检查为相同 anti-join。它对 900 辆车建 hash，经 bitmap 路径处理 59,539 条 Collect。Right 表示物理输入方向，不改变“保留从未取车的车辆”语义；不是为每辆车重扫整张日志。

PostgreSQL decorrelates NOT EXISTS and recognizes the outer-join NULL filter as the same anti-join operation. It hashes the small set of 900 vehicles and scans 59,539 Collect events through a bitmap index/heap path, keeping the vehicle rows without any match. The Right direction describes the physical join inputs chosen by the optimizer; it preserves the intended vehicle-side anti-join semantics. Neither syntax forces a separate full event scan for each vehicle. The minor time difference does not establish a consistent performance difference.

A2_3.1.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- Statement A
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT v.vehicle_id
FROM VEHICLES v
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
      AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
)
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;
-- Statement B
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT v.vehicle_id
FROM VEHICLES v
LEFT JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e
  ON e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
WHERE e.vehicle_id IS NULL
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;
下载本题 SQL / Download SQL ↗
每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 18 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

  2. 02-- Statement A

    注释把接下来的查询标为 Statement A，方便与 B 比较；-- 到行末都不执行。

    This comment labels Statement A for comparison with B; text following -- is not executed.

  3. 03EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT v.vehicle_id

    EXPLAIN 显示“Statement A: NOT EXISTS”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for Statement A: NOT EXISTS. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

  4. 04FROM VEHICLES v

    从完整 VEHICLES 表出发，包含没有事件或只有预约/取消事件的车辆。若从 Collect 日志出发，反而找不到“从未出现”的车辆。

    Start from the entire fleet, including vehicles with no events or only reservations/cancellations. Starting from Collect events would omit the very absences being sought.

  5. 05WHERE NOT EXISTS (

    NOT EXISTS 要求括号内找不到任何匹配行。它回答“是否不存在一次 Collect”，而不是检查车辆的最后一个事件。

    NOT EXISTS requires no matching inner row. It tests absence of any Collect, not merely the type of the latest event.

  6. 06 SELECT 1 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e

    SELECT 1 只是为 EXISTS 构造一行；1 的数值没有业务意义，也不是计数。EXISTS 只关心有没有符合条件的行，不关心输出的列值。

    SELECT 1 supplies an arbitrary value for EXISTS; it is not a count. EXISTS cares only whether a qualifying row exists, not its selected value.

  7. 07 WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id

    只看与外层这辆 v 相同 vehicle_id 的事件，形成相关子查询。如果漏掉相关条件，一辆车被取用就可能让所有候选都被排除。

    Correlate inner events to the current outer vehicle. Omitting this condition could make one Collect event incorrectly disqualify every candidate.

  8. 08 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'

    还必须是 Collect，其他事件不证明车辆真正被驾驶。只有同车且 Collect 的事件存在时，外层 NOT EXISTS 才为假。

    Require Collect as well: other events do not prove the car was driven. A same-vehicle Collect makes the outer NOT EXISTS false.

  9. 09)

    关闭 NOT EXISTS 子查询。这个逻辑相关性不强制物理计划逐车完整重扫；优化器可以改写成 anti-join。

    Close the NOT EXISTS subquery. Logical correlation does not force a full rescan per vehicle; the optimizer can transform it into an anti-join.

  10. 10ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

    按车辆 ID 排序，便于稳定比对 A 与 B 的全部 75 行，而不是只看前几行。没有 ORDER BY 时，SQL 不保证结果顺序相同。

    Sort identifiers for stable comparison of all 75 rows from A and B. Without ORDER BY, equal sets need not appear in equal order.

  11. 11-- Statement B

    注释标记 Statement B；它使用外连接表达同一个“没有 Collect”的问题。

    Label Statement B, which expresses the same absence test using an outer join.

  12. 12EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT v.vehicle_id

    EXPLAIN 显示“Statement B: LEFT JOIN with an unmatched-row test”的物理执行计划；ANALYZE 会真正运行该查询并记录实际耗时、行数与 loops，BUFFERS 增加缓冲页访问统计。这里不逐行打印普通查询结果；cost 是估计单位，不能直接当毫秒，也不能把父子节点时间简单相加。

    EXPLAIN shows the physical plan for Statement B: LEFT JOIN with an unmatched-row test. ANALYZE really executes it and records actual timing, rows and loops; BUFFERS adds page-access statistics. It does not return ordinary result tuples, cost is not milliseconds, and parent/child timings must not simply be summed.

  13. 13FROM VEHICLES v

    从完整 VEHICLES 表出发，包含没有事件或只有预约/取消事件的车辆。若从 Collect 日志出发，反而找不到“从未出现”的车辆。

    Start from the entire fleet, including vehicles with no events or only reservations/cancellations. Starting from Collect events would omit the very absences being sought.

  14. 14LEFT JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e

    LEFT JOIN 保留左侧的所有车辆。匹配不到右侧事件时，右侧的 e 列会被补成 NULL，为后面的未匹配检查提供依据。

    LEFT JOIN preserves every vehicle, filling right-side event columns with NULL when no matching event exists.

  15. 15 ON e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id

    连接条件先限定事件属于同一辆车。此条件决定哪些事件能和车辆配对，不是在最终结果上检查 NULL。

    The first ON condition pairs an event with the same vehicle. It defines matches before the later unmatched-row filter.

  16. 16 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'

    Collect 条件继续放在 ON 中，只把取车事件视作匹配。若把它移到 WHERE，无匹配行的 e.event_type 为 NULL，会被过滤掉，破坏反连接语义。

    Keep the Collect predicate inside ON. Moving it to WHERE would discard unmatched rows with NULL event_type and break the anti-join.

  17. 17WHERE e.vehicle_id IS NULL

    e.vehicle_id 在真实事件中 NOT NULL，所以这一列为 NULL 能可靠识别 LEFT JOIN 没有匹配的车辆。匹配过 Collect 的车辆不会通过这个条件。

    Real event vehicle_id values are NOT NULL, so NULL here reliably identifies an unmatched vehicle. Vehicles having a Collect match fail this condition.

  18. 18ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

    按车辆 ID 排序，便于稳定比对 A 与 B 的全部 75 行，而不是只看前几行。没有 ORDER BY 时，SQL 不保证结果顺序相同。

    Sort identifiers for stable comparison of all 75 rows from A and B. Without ORDER BY, equal sets need not appear in equal order.

完整执行计划 / Full query plan · 3.1.2_1
-- Statement A
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT v.vehicle_id
FROM VEHICLES v
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
      AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
)
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

LEFT JOIN BOOKING_EVENTS e
  ON e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
 AND e.event_type = 'Collect'
WHERE e.vehicle_id IS NULL
ORDER BY v.vehicle_id;

Q3.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（4分）对C:event_type<Collect和D:event_type>=Collect，在三组扫描设置下解释最多六计划，最后恢复全部扫描选项。

(4 marks) Explain C:event_type<Collect and D:event_type>=Collect under three scan settings; include index, bitmap, index-only flags and reset all.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

本阶段共有 196,441 条事件。测试库 C collation 下，C 只匹配 Cancel：8,158 行（4.15%）；D 匹配其余 188,283 行（95.85%）。二者只改比较方向，却改变了选择率。

There are 196,441 events at this stage. Under the test database C collation, C selects only Cancel: 8,158 rows (4.15%). D selects the other 188,283 rows (95.85%). Reversing the inequality changes selectivity.

Bitmap Scan 先从索引取位置，再按 heap 页批量访问。对 C，保留索引时 cost 1,910.62；禁止索引后只能顺序扫，cost 4,091.51。对 D，正常顺序扫 cost 4,091.51；抑制顺序扫后 bitmap 要处理几乎所有行和全部 1,636 个 heap blocks，cost 增至 8,384.15。本次时间见下表，缓存会影响不同运行；四个开关最后都恢复 on。

Bitmap access first finds index locations and then batches heap-page visits. For C the permitted bitmap path costs 1,910.62; suppressing all index paths yields a sequential scan costing 4,091.51. For D the normal sequential scan costs 4,091.51, whereas suppressing it forces a bitmap path costing 8,384.15 and visiting all 1,636 heap blocks. Times in the table are individual observations affected by cache state. All four switches are restored to on.

设置 / SettingQueryScanActual rowsTotal costTime ms
1 · All onCBitmap heap + index8,1581,910.624.667
1 · All onDSequential scan188,2834,091.5114.145
2 · Seq offCBitmap heap + index8,1581,910.622.242
2 · Seq offDBitmap heap + index188,2838,384.1517.270
3 · Index paths offCSequential scan8,1584,091.517.886
3 · Index paths offDSequential scan188,2834,091.5113.995
A2_3.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- Setting 1: index scans enabled; sequential scans enabled.
SET enable_indexscan = on;
SET enable_indexonlyscan = on;
SET enable_bitmapscan = on;
SET enable_seqscan = on;
-- Query C
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type < 'Collect';
-- Query D
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type >= 'Collect';
-- Setting 2: index scans enabled; sequential scans suppressed.
SET enable_indexscan = on;
SET enable_indexonlyscan = on;
SET enable_bitmapscan = on;
SET enable_seqscan = off;
-- Query C
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type < 'Collect';
-- Query D
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type >= 'Collect';
-- Setting 3: index scans suppressed; sequential scans enabled.
SET enable_indexscan = off;
SET enable_indexonlyscan = off;
SET enable_bitmapscan = off;
SET enable_seqscan = on;
-- Query C
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type < 'Collect';
-- Query D
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM BOOKING_EVENTS WHERE event_type >= 'Collect';
-- Restore all four scan settings.
SET enable_indexscan = on;
SET enable_indexonlyscan = on;
SET enable_bitmapscan = on;
SET enable_seqscan = on;

  4. 04SET enable_indexonlyscan = on;

    enable_indexonlyscan 控制Index Only Scan 路径；它还要求普通 index scan 被允许，并满足覆盖列等条件。本行设为 on，作用于当前会话后续语句，不修改表内容；三种索引相关开关要一起处理，才符合本题定义。

    Set enable_indexonlyscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: index-only scans, which additionally require ordinary index scans and suitable coverage. This changes planner options, not table data.

  5. 05SET enable_bitmapscan = on;

    Set enable_bitmapscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: bitmap access, which is not disabled merely by turning off ordinary index scans. This changes planner options, not table data.

  6. 06SET enable_seqscan = on;

    EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) 实际执行 Query D，SELECT * 需要整行事件。比较 event_type 大于等于字符串 Collect，是按数据库排序规则比较文字，不是比较事件发生先后；C collation 下匹配 188,283 行。本组实测用 Seq Scan，数字和完整计划在后面的证据中。

  29. 29-- Restore all four scan settings.

    实验结束，下面四行把全部扫描选项明确恢复为 on。必须清理会话设置，避免下一题继承被抑制的访问方法。

    Finish the experiment by explicitly restoring all four scan options to on so the next question does not inherit suppressed methods.

  30. 30SET enable_indexscan = on;

    enable_indexscan 控制普通 Index Scan 路径，也会影响 index-only 路径能否被考虑。本行设为 on，作用于当前会话后续语句，不修改表内容；这属于最后的恢复步骤。

    Set enable_indexscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: ordinary index scans, also required when considering index-only scans. This changes planner options, not table data.

  31. 31SET enable_indexonlyscan = on;

    enable_indexonlyscan 控制Index Only Scan 路径；它还要求普通 index scan 被允许，并满足覆盖列等条件。本行设为 on，作用于当前会话后续语句，不修改表内容；这属于最后的恢复步骤。

    Set enable_indexonlyscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: index-only scans, which additionally require ordinary index scans and suitable coverage. This changes planner options, not table data.

  32. 32SET enable_bitmapscan = on;

    enable_bitmapscan 控制Bitmap Index/Heap Scan 路径；只关普通 Index Scan 并不会连带关闭它。本行设为 on，作用于当前会话后续语句，不修改表内容；这属于最后的恢复步骤。

    Set enable_bitmapscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: bitmap access, which is not disabled merely by turning off ordinary index scans. This changes planner options, not table data.

  33. 33SET enable_seqscan = on;

    enable_seqscan 控制Sequential Scan 路径；off 是抑制，并非保证任何情况下都绝对不会出现。本行设为 on，作用于当前会话后续语句，不修改表内容；这属于最后的恢复步骤。

    Set enable_seqscan to on for subsequent statements in this session: sequential scans; off discourages this method rather than guaranteeing its absence in every possible query. This changes planner options, not table data.

Q4 快照与死锁Snapshots and deadlocks · 9 marks

回到课程专题：锁与死锁 / Related course topic →

EXPERIMENT REPLAY / 实测过程回放

同一辆车，两个语句快照One vehicle, two statement snapshots

逐步回放已记录的 Q4.1 调度，观察 T1 的答案何时过时。这里用于解释实测过程，不在浏览器中执行数据库。

Step through the recorded Q4.1 schedule and find when T1’s answer becomes stale. This is an explanatory replay, not database execution in the browser.

CONNECTION 1 / MEMBER 1

CONNECTION 2 / MEMBER 2

Q4.1.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（2分）会话1开始事务，找最新事件为Return的车，保持未提交。

(2 marks) Session 1: BEGIN and find a vehicle whose latest event is Return; leave transaction open.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

连接 1 在 READ COMMITTED 下 BEGIN，查得 VEH-0001 的最新事件是 Return 194486，时间为 2026-06-27 11:05:11。先对所有事件按 event_time DESC、event_id DESC 取最新，再检查 Return；不能先过滤 Return 再取最新。

Connection 1 begins a READ COMMITTED transaction and returns VEH-0001 with latest event 194486, a Return at 2026-06-27 11:05:11. The lateral subquery first selects the latest event across all event types, using event_id to break timestamp ties. Only after that does the outer query test for Return. An older Return cannot incorrectly qualify a vehicle whose latest state is Reserve, Collect or Incident.

下一步必须在另一连接重查同一辆车，连接 1 此时保持事务打开。这个普通读取没有预留车辆。

The selected vehicle is kept fixed for the same availability check in connection 2. Connection 1 stays open after this step.

A2_4.1.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- connection 1
BEGIN;
SHOW transaction_isolation;
SELECT v.vehicle_id, latest.event_id,
       latest.event_type, latest.event_time
FROM VEHICLES v
CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT e.event_id, e.event_type, e.event_time
    FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
    ORDER BY e.event_time DESC, e.event_id DESC
    LIMIT 1
) latest
WHERE v.vehicle_id = 'VEH-0001'
  AND latest.event_type = 'Return';
-- Keep this transaction open. Run 4.1.2 in connection 2.
下载本题 SQL / Download SQL ↗
每一行在做什么 / Read every line · 17 行

行号与上方代码一致。先读这一行改变了什么，再把它放回整条查询；括号、别名和注释也有说明。

  1. 01-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition

    文件说明注释：公开教学版本已移除提交者身份。-- 后直到行末不作为 SQL 执行，不会改变查询或会话；行号仍与下载文件一致。

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02-- connection 1

    下面的语句只发送给连接 1。-- connection 1 是给操作者看的标签，并不会让 psql 自动切换连接。

    Send the following statements only to connection 1. The comment labels a session for the operator; it does not switch psql connections.

  3. 03BEGIN;

    SQL BEGIN 开始显式事务，后续保持在同一连接。READ COMMITTED 下事务不等于固定一张快照；每条语句仍可能看到更新的已提交数据。

    SQL BEGIN starts an explicit transaction in this connection. At READ COMMITTED it does not freeze one snapshot for every later statement.

  4. 04SHOW transaction_isolation;

    SHOW 查看当前事务隔离级别，用输出确认是 read committed。它只检查设置，不会把隔离级别改成别的值。

    SHOW reports the current transaction isolation level, verifying read committed without changing it.

  5. 05SELECT v.vehicle_id, latest.event_id,

    外层输出车辆编号和最新事件 ID，便于两个连接确认谈论的是同一辆车、同一条历史状态。v 来自 VEHICLES，latest 来自后面的 lateral 子查询。

    Select the vehicle and latest-event identifier so both connections can verify the same car and historical state. v is the vehicle and latest is the lateral result.

  6. 06 latest.event_type, latest.event_time

    继续输出最新事件类型和时间。这些列用于解释为什么车辆此刻被题目规则判为可用，而不是只显示一个无法复查的车辆编号。

    Also show the latest event type and time so the availability decision can be checked against its actual historical state.

  7. 07FROM VEHICLES v

    从 VEHICLES 车辆表逐个考虑候选，给它别名 v。本次外层稍后固定测试车辆为 VEH-0001。

    Read candidate vehicles as v; the outer filter later fixes this experiment to VEH-0001.

  8. 08CROSS JOIN LATERAL (

    LATERAL 允许右侧子查询引用左侧 v 的 vehicle_id；CROSS JOIN 把该车找到的最新事件接上。若车辆完全没有事件，右侧为零行，这种写法不会把它当成“最新为 Return”。

    LATERAL lets the inner query reference v.vehicle_id. CROSS JOIN attaches its latest event; a vehicle with no events does not qualify as having a latest Return.

  9. 09 SELECT e.event_id, e.event_type, e.event_time

    从这辆车的事件中准备输出 ID、类型和时间。必须先考虑全部类型，不能在这里先筛掉非 Return。

    Select the identifier, type and time from the car’s events. All event types must remain eligible at this stage.

  10. 10 FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e

    读取完整 BOOKING_EVENTS，给事件表别名 e。可用性来自日志状态，而不是 VEHICLES 的 odometer 或 in_service。

    Read BOOKING_EVENTS as e. Availability comes from event history, not the vehicle’s odometer or in_service date.

  11. 11 WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id

    只让子查询查看当前外层 v 所对应的车辆事件。这就是 lateral 子查询与当前车之间的相关条件。

    Restrict the inner query to events for the current outer vehicle; this is the lateral correlation condition.

  12. 12 ORDER BY e.event_time DESC, e.event_id DESC

    按 event_time 倒序找最新事件，时间相同再用 event_id 倒序确定唯一顺序。它按事件记录的时间排序，因此未来的 Reserve 也可能排在最前。

    Order by event_time descending and then event_id descending for deterministic ties. Future-dated Reserve events can therefore sort first.

  13. 13 LIMIT 1

    排序后只取第一条事件，代表该车按题目规则的当前状态。LIMIT 1 在检查 Return 之前执行，这是防止误用旧 Return 的关键。

    Take the first event after ordering. Selecting this latest event before testing for Return prevents an older Return from falsely establishing availability.

  14. 14) latest

    关闭 lateral 子查询并把它的输出表命名为 latest，所以外层能写 latest.event_type 等列名。latest 不是原数据库中的实体表。

    Close the lateral subquery and alias its derived output latest. It is a query result, not a stored database table.

  15. 15WHERE v.vehicle_id = 'VEH-0001'

    外层固定为 VEH-0001，确保两个连接抢同一辆车；新载入数据时它最新事件是 Return。完成 Q4 后它变成 Reserve，再直接重跑不应假定仍可用。

    Fix the candidate to VEH-0001 so both sessions contend for the same car. It has a latest Return on fresh data; after Q4 its Reserve means a rerun cannot assume availability.

  16. 16 AND latest.event_type = 'Return';

    最后才要求 latest.event_type = Return。若最新是 Reserve、Collect 或 Incident，即使更早有 Return，也会返回零行，而不是错误宣称可用。

    Only now require the latest event to be Return. A later Reserve, Collect or Incident excludes the car even if it has an older Return.

  17. 17-- Keep this transaction open. Run 4.1.2 in connection 2.

    按注释停在这里，保留连接 1 的事务，再去连接 2 运行下一文件。若立刻 COMMIT 或在同一连接执行两个角色，就不再是题目给定的交错。

    Pause with connection 1’s transaction open and run the next file in connection 2. Committing now or using one connection for both roles changes the required schedule.

Q4.1.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）会话2重查同车，给另一成员插三天后Reserve并提交。

(0.5 marks) Session 2: BEGIN, recheck same vehicle, Reserve for another member in three days, COMMIT.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

连接 2 同样是 READ COMMITTED，也看到最新 Return。它为 member 2 插入三天后的 Reserve 并成功 COMMIT；连接 1 仍开着的普通 SELECT 并未阻止此次插入。

Connection 2 also reports READ COMMITTED and sees the same latest Return while connection 1 is open. It inserts a Reserve for member 2 at NOW() + INTERVAL '3 days' and commits successfully. The plain availability SELECT in connection 1 did not lock the vehicle against this insertion.

A2_4.1.2.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- connection 2
BEGIN;
SHOW transaction_isolation;
SELECT v.vehicle_id, latest.event_id,
       latest.event_type, latest.event_time
FROM VEHICLES v
CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT e.event_id, e.event_type, e.event_time
    FROM BOOKING_EVENTS e
    WHERE e.vehicle_id = v.vehicle_id
    ORDER BY e.event_time DESC, e.event_id DESC
    LIMIT 1
) latest
WHERE v.vehicle_id = 'VEH-0001'
  AND latest.event_type = 'Return';
INSERT INTO BOOKING_EVENTS
    (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)
VALUES (2, 'VEH-0001', 'Reserve', NOW() + INTERVAL '3 days', NULL);
COMMIT;
下载本题 SQL / Download SQL ↗

    Start insertion of a reservation event; it appends history rather than overwriting the earlier Return.

  18. 18 (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)

    声明五个目标列，并省略自动生成的 event_id。VALUES 的五个位置必须依此对应 member、vehicle、type、time、amount。

    List the five target columns, omitting generated event_id; VALUES must follow this exact member/vehicle/type/time/amount order.

  19. 19VALUES (2, 'VEH-0001', 'Reserve', NOW() + INTERVAL '3 days', NULL);

    为 member 2 预约 VEH-0001，类型 Reserve、amount 为 NULL；NOW()+INTERVAL 给出事务开始时刻后三天。NOW() 在 PostgreSQL 中是事务开始时间，不是每次调用重新跳动的墙上时钟；与无时区 event_time 的转换使用会话时区。

    Reserve VEH-0001 for member 2 with a NULL amount and a time three days after NOW(). PostgreSQL NOW() is the transaction-start time, not a ticking wall clock; conversion to event_time uses the session timezone.

  20. 20COMMIT;

    提交连接 2 的预约，使之后启动的 READ COMMITTED 语句能够看到它。连接 1 保持开启不阻止这个普通插入提交。

    Commit connection 2’s reservation so later READ COMMITTED statements can see it even though connection 1 remains open.

Q4.1.3

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）会话1尝试现在为另一成员Collect后提交，报告结果事件。

(1 marks) Session 1: attempt Collect for a different member at now (), then COMMIT; report resulting events.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

连接 2 提交后，连接 1 为 member 1 尝试现在 Collect，触发器报错：Vehicle VEH-0001 is reserved by member 2。事务进入失败状态，COMMIT 的结果是 ROLLBACK，没有新增 Collect。

Connection 1 attempts a present-time Collect for member 1 after connection 2 commits. BI_COLLECT rejects it with ERROR: Vehicle VEH-0001 is reserved by member 2. The transaction is then failed, so the attempted COMMIT produces ROLLBACK. No new Collect is stored.

最终最新事件是 Reserve 196442，其后是历史 Return 194486 和历史 Collect 194367。本次两个竞争新增事件中只有 Reserve 留下；Q1.3 的 Incident 是更早的独立事件。失败插入可能消耗 serial 值，序列间隙不等于存在已提交记录。

The verification query shows Reserve event 196442 as the latest event, ahead of historical Return 194486 and historical Collect 194367. Of the two competing new events in this experiment, only the Reserve survives. The Incident from Q1.3 remains a separate older historical event. Sequence allocation can leave a gap for the rejected Collect; a consumed serial value does not prove that a row committed.

A2_4.1.3.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- connection 1, after connection 2 has committed
INSERT INTO BOOKING_EVENTS
    (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)
VALUES (1, 'VEH-0001', 'Collect', NOW(), NULL);
-- The trigger raises an exception, so COMMIT ends this failed transaction as ROLLBACK.
COMMIT;
SELECT event_id, member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount
FROM BOOKING_EVENTS
WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0001'
ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC LIMIT 3;

    Public-edition header with author identifiers removed. Text after -- is a non-executable comment; line numbers still match the downloadable file.

  2. 02-- connection 1, after connection 2 has committed

    切回原来的连接 1，且必须等连接 2 已提交。重新开一个连接不能代表保留着旧查询结果的 T1。

    Return to the original connection 1 after connection 2 commits; a newly opened connection would not represent the same T1.

  3. 03INSERT INTO BOOKING_EVENTS

    尝试向事件表追加 Collect；这次插入会触发已有的 BEFORE INSERT 触发器。

    Attempt to append a Collect event, invoking the supplied BEFORE INSERT trigger.

  4. 04 (member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount)

    按列名列出五个输入，event_id 仍交给序列。触发器能通过 NEW 访问这行尚未成功插入的数据。

    List the five input columns while leaving event_id to the sequence. The trigger sees this pending row through NEW.

  5. 05VALUES (1, 'VEH-0001', 'Collect', NOW(), NULL);

    车辆相同但成员换成 1、类型改成 Collect，amount 仍 NULL。NOW() 是 T1 开始时间；即便时间比 T2 早，触发器读取使用的后续语句快照仍能看到 T2 的提交。

    Use the same vehicle but member 1 and Collect, with NULL amount. NOW() is T1’s start time, yet the later trigger read can still see T2’s committed reservation.

  6. 06-- The trigger raises an exception, so COMMIT ends this failed transaction as ROLLBACK.

    注释说明预期错误后的事务状态；它不捕获或忽略异常。本脚本在 psql 中必须允许预期错误之后继续发送 COMMIT，才能记录 ROLLBACK 输出。

    The comment explains the expected failed transaction; it does not catch the exception. psql must continue past the expected error to record COMMIT’s ROLLBACK result.

  7. 07COMMIT;

    因为触发器异常使该事务失败，这个 COMMIT 实际结束为 ROLLBACK；不会把拒绝的 Collect 提交。若想继续事务内工作，需要事先设计 SAVEPOINT 等恢复机制，本次没有使用。

    The trigger exception has failed the transaction, so COMMIT ends it as ROLLBACK and does not save the Collect. No savepoint recovery is used here.

  8. 08SELECT event_id, member_id, vehicle_id, event_type, event_time, amount

    事务清理后再查询事件 ID、成员、车辆、类型、时间与金额，核对究竟留下了哪些记录，而不是只根据成功/失败消息猜测。

    After transaction cleanup, query event details to verify which records survived rather than inferring them solely from command messages.

  9. 09FROM BOOKING_EVENTS

    验证仍从实际事件日志读取，普通 SELECT 在前一个失败事务结束后开始新的隐式语句事务。

    Read the actual event log in a new statement after the failed transaction has ended.

  10. 10WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0001'

    只检查争用车辆 VEH-0001，避免其他车辆的新旧事件干扰观察。

    Limit verification to contested vehicle VEH-0001.

  11. 11ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC LIMIT 3;

    按最新时间、再按最大事件 ID 取前三条，看到 Reserve 196442 和两条历史记录。LIMIT 3 只是展示范围；它不表示数据库只保留了三条历史事件。

    Show the three latest records, including Reserve 196442 and historical events. LIMIT 3 limits display, not the amount of history stored.

Q4.1.4

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（2.5分）从数据库与租车业务解释旧检查、快照及未预留资源。

(2.5 marks) Explain the outcome in database and car-share terms, including snapshots and absence of reservation.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

顺序是 T1 开始并读到可用 → T2 开始并读到可用 → T2 写 Reserve 并提交 → T1 尝试 Collect → 触发器异常 → T1 回滚。

This explanation describes the supplied trigger and the actual schedule; a snapshot is a consistent read, not a reservation of the vehicle.

READ COMMITTED 为每条语句取得新快照。T1 最初的查询看到 Return，之后 INSERT 内触发器的查询可以看到 T2 已提交的 Reserve；按最新事件排序，未来 Reserve 成为当前状态，因此最初答案已经过时。

This explanation describes the supplied trigger and the actual schedule; a snapshot is a consistent read, not a reservation of the vehicle.

普通 SELECT 的 ACCESS SHARE 表锁与插入兼容，没有取得阻止另一成员预订这辆车的行锁。本次是业务规则异常，不是两个连接因相互等待而发生的死锁。

This explanation describes the supplied trigger and the actual schedule; a snapshot is a consistent read, not a reservation of the vehicle.

业务上，三天后的预约立即挡住现在取车。简化模型没有表达预约时间区间与现在可用之间的区别。串行化车辆操作可解决检查后再写入的竞争，但真正支持未来预约还需要区间规则。

This explanation describes the supplied trigger and the actual schedule; a snapshot is a consistent read, not a reservation of the vehicle.

Q4.2.1

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1.5分）两会话反向更新VEH-0401与VEH-0501，产生死锁并保留完整错误。

(1.5 marks) Update VEH-0401 then 0501 in session 1, and reverse order in session 2 to produce and report a deadlock with full error.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

两个连接分别 BEGIN。T1 把 VEH-0401 加 10，T2 把 VEH-0501 加 20；T1 再更新 VEH-0501 时阻塞，在超时检测前让 T2 再请求 VEH-0401，形成环。下方保留本次完整错误和实际结果。

Both sessions begin. T1 adds 10 to VEH-0401 and T2 adds 20 to VEH-0501. T1 blocks while requesting VEH-0501; before deadlock detection, T2 requests VEH-0401 and completes the cycle. The full captured error and outcomes are below.

实测 T1 被选为死锁受害者，T1 的 COMMIT 返回 ROLLBACK；T2 获得释放的锁后完成并提交。这里的错误 PID 和 transaction ID 来自真实日志，重跑时不保证相同。

In this run T1 was the victim: its COMMIT reported ROLLBACK. T2 acquired the released lock, finished and committed. Process and transaction IDs come from the genuine log and may differ on a rerun.

A2_4.2.1.sql
-- INFS7903 Assignment 2 | Public teaching edition
-- Run the statements one at a time in the labelled connections.
-- When step 5 blocks, leave it waiting and run step 6 in connection 2.
-- step 1: connection 1
BEGIN;
-- step 2: connection 2
BEGIN;
-- step 3: connection 1
UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 10 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0401';
-- step 4: connection 2
UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 20 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0501';
-- step 5: connection 1
UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 10 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0501';
-- step 6: connection 2
UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 20 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0401';
-- step 7: connection 1
COMMIT;
-- step 8: connection 2
COMMIT;
-- step 9: connection 1
SELECT vehicle_id, odometer FROM VEHICLES WHERE vehicle_id IN ('VEH-0401', 'VEH-0501') ORDER BY vehicle_id;

  2. 02-- Run the statements one at a time in the labelled connections.

    这是人工交错执行清单：必须把每条语句发给其标记的连接。把整份文件在一个连接中一次运行，不会产生题目要求的双事务死锁。

    This is a manually interleaved two-connection schedule. Running the whole list in one connection cannot demonstrate the requested deadlock.

  3. 03-- When step 5 blocks, leave it waiting and run step 6 in connection 2.

    Leave step 5 waiting and issue step 6 in connection 2. Canceling or ending the waiting transaction changes the schedule and may prevent the cycle.

  4. 04-- step 1: connection 1

    执行顺序第 1 步，目标是连接 1。这只是标签，不会自动切换会话；后面的 SQL 才是真正送到数据库的操作。

  5. 05BEGIN;

  6. 06-- step 2: connection 2

    Begin connection 2’s independent transaction. Conflicting row locks acquired by its updates are retained until transaction end.

  8. 08-- step 3: connection 1

  9. 09UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 10 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0401';

    Increment only VEH-0401 by 10, rather than overwriting it with a guessed total. The primary-key WHERE avoids updating the whole fleet. It succeeds and holds the VEH-0401 row lock.

  13. 13UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 10 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0501';

    Increment only VEH-0501 by 10, rather than overwriting it with a guessed total. The primary-key WHERE avoids updating the whole fleet. It waits for T2’s VEH-0501 lock while retaining its first lock.

  14. 14-- step 6: connection 2

    执行顺序第 6 步，目标是连接 2。这只是标签，不会自动切换会话；后面的 SQL 才是真正送到数据库的操作。

  15. 15UPDATE VEHICLES SET odometer = odometer + 20 WHERE vehicle_id = 'VEH-0401';

    Increment only VEH-0401 by 20, rather than overwriting it with a guessed total. The primary-key WHERE avoids updating the whole fleet. It waits for T1’s VEH-0401 lock, completing the T1 → T2 → T1 cycle.

  16. 16-- step 7: connection 1

    执行顺序第 7 步，目标是连接 1。这只是标签，不会自动切换会话；后面的 SQL 才是真正送到数据库的操作。

  17. 17COMMIT;

    结束连接 1 的事务；在这次实测中 T1 被选为受害者，所以 COMMIT 输出 ROLLBACK，第一次成功的 +10 也不能留下。受害者选择可能随重跑调度变化。

    End connection 1’s transaction: it was the victim in this run, so COMMIT reports ROLLBACK and even its first +10 is undone. Victim choice may change on reruns.

  18. 18-- step 8: connection 2

    执行顺序第 8 步，目标是连接 2。这只是标签，不会自动切换会话；后面的 SQL 才是真正送到数据库的操作。

    Schedule step 8 belongs to connection 2. This label does not switch sessions; send the following SQL to that connection.

  19. 19COMMIT;

    结束连接 2 的事务；本次 T1 被中止后释放锁，T2 的第二条更新得以完成，COMMIT 提交两次 +20，并释放它的锁。

    End connection 2’s transaction: after T1 releases its locks, T2 completes and commits both +20 changes, releasing its locks.

  20. 20-- step 9: connection 1

    执行顺序第 9 步，目标是连接 1。这只是标签，不会自动切换会话；后面的 SQL 才是真正送到数据库的操作。

    Schedule step 9 belongs to connection 1. This label does not switch sessions; send the following SQL to that connection.

  21. 21SELECT vehicle_id, odometer FROM VEHICLES WHERE vehicle_id IN ('VEH-0401', 'VEH-0501') ORDER BY vehicle_id;

    在事务都结束后查询两辆车的里程，并按 ID 排序。IN 只选这两个主键；本次 T2 提交后结果为 VEH-0401 149120、VEH-0501 122170，用来核对回滚并非只撤销报错的第二句。

    After both transactions end, select the two odometers with IN and sort by identifier. Values 149120 and 122170 confirm that victim rollback undoes its first successful update too.

SELECT vehicle_id, odometer FROM VEHICLES WHERE vehicle_id IN ('VEH-0401', 'VEH-0501') ORDER BY vehicle_id;
 vehicle_id | odometer 
------------+----------
 VEH-0401   |   149120
 VEH-0501   |   122170
(2 rows)
原始日志 / Raw transcript ↗（原资料留在本地版）输出截图 / Output screenshots ↗（原资料留在本地版）

Q4.2.2

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（1分）画等待图的环，解释DBMS处理及两个事务结局。

(1 marks) Draw the wait-for cycle and explain DBMS intervention and both transaction outcomes.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

T1 持有 VEH-0401，等待 T2 的 VEH-0501，所以有 T1 → T2；T2 持有 VEH-0501，等待 T1 的 VEH-0401，所以有 T2 → T1。环已在图上标红。错误中 ShareLock on transaction 表示等待持有冲突行版本的事务结束。

Immediately before intervention, T1 holds the row lock for VEH-0401 and waits for the row held by T2 on VEH-0501; T2 holds VEH-0501 and waits for VEH-0401. The wait-for edges are T1 -> T2 and T2 -> T1, which form a cycle. The ShareLock-on-transaction wording in PostgreSQL's error reflects waiting for the transaction owning the conflicting row version to finish.

本次 PostgreSQL 中止 T1，连同它第一次成功的 +10 也一并回滚；释放锁后 T2 可继续，提交两次 +20。最终里程为 149,120 和 122,170。受害者选择可随调度变化，程序应准备重试被中止的事务。

In this run PostgreSQL selected T1 as the deadlock victim and aborted its transaction. T1's first +10 update was rolled back along with its second attempt, and its COMMIT reported ROLLBACK. Releasing T1's locks allowed T2's blocked update to finish; T2 then committed both +20 corrections. The final odometers are 149,120 for VEH-0401 and 122,170 for VEH-0501. The victim choice can differ in a rerun; applications must be prepared to retry the aborted transaction.

Q4.2.3

TASK SUMMARY + LINE GUIDE
中文题意 / Task in Chinese

（0.5分）提出一种预防策略并说明业务代价。

(0.5 marks) Name one prevention strategy and its operational cost.

解答、逐行代码与实测依据 / Solution, line guide and evidence

所有流程都按一致的 vehicle_id 升序拿锁或更新：两边都先请求 VEH-0401，再请求 VEH-0501，就不会形成这个反向等待环。代价是重叠车辆的修改要排队，且各代码路径都必须遵守顺序；不涉及相同车辆的操作仍可并行。

A consistent lock order prevents this cycle: every correction transaction must lock or update the vehicles in ascending vehicle_id order, so both request VEH-0401 before VEH-0501. The co-operative pays for waiting/queuing when staff edit overlapping vehicles and must enforce the ordering rule across all code paths; unrelated vehicles can still be updated concurrently.

文件与重放验证Archived files and reproducibility

20 个实际提交脚本已在新载入的数据库中按题目顺序重放；Q4 使用两个独立、持续打开的 psql 连接。向上递归、双向 EXCEPT ALL、物化视图刷新与失败 Collect 均另做了校验。

The twenty submitted scripts were replayed in question order on a freshly loaded database. Q4 used two independent persistent psql connections. Independent checks covered upward recursion, bidirectional EXCEPT ALL, materialized-view refresh and the rejected Collect.

输出截图来自实际 psql 日志的本地展示页，并非原生终端窗口截图。原始日志完整保留；截图只调整显示空白，并按报告要求截取超过 20 行结果的前后各 10 行。Q4.2 要按文件中的连接标签逐条发送，单个连接一次执行整个文件无法展示真实并发。

Output screenshots capture a local viewer of genuine psql transcripts, rather than native terminal windows. Original logs are retained in full; screenshot whitespace is reflowed and results longer than twenty rows show the first and last ten. Send Q4.2 statements individually to their labelled connections: one connection cannot demonstrate the concurrent schedule.

全课程复习工具Whole-course review toolkit

本工具应在读完相关章节后使用，用于检索、比较和闭卷提取。自编题不是往年试题或预测题；没有官方评分细则的地方只列参考要点。先用中文把因果关系讲通，再用同样完整的英文说明条件、步骤与结论。

Use this toolkit after the relevant chapters for retrieval, comparison and closed-book recall. Author-created exercises are neither past papers nor predictions. Where no official marking guide exists, only reference points are given. Explain the causal reasoning in Chinese first, then give equally complete conditions, steps and conclusions in English.

建议学习顺序与三轮复习Suggested learning order and three review passes

第一轮按01→02→03→04理解逻辑与规则，再05→06理解物理访问，接07→08→09把同一查询从语义连到成本，最后10→11→12理解多人执行和失败；13把这些知识迁移到综合任务。若SQL已很熟，可先做Tutorial1 Ex3验证，再决定第2章读得多细。

On the first pass, study 01→02→03→04 for logic and rules, 05→06 for physical access, 07→08→09 to connect query meaning with cost, and 10→11→12 for concurrent execution and failure. Chapter 13 transfers the ideas to integrated tasks. If SQL is already familiar, test yourself on Tutorial 1 Ex 3 before deciding how closely to read Chapter 2.

第二轮逐主题做原题：先盖住答案，列条件与方法，完成后比较理由而非只比最终值；错题按“概念不清、条件漏读、过程错误、表达不准”记录，并回到对应章节。Practical需能解释每条语句的目的与检查方式；没有可用服务器时先纸上追踪，但不把纸面结果当运行证据。

On the second pass, solve original questions by topic with answers hidden. Write conditions and methods, then compare reasoning as well as final values. Classify errors as conceptual, missed conditions, procedural or expressive and revisit the linked chapter. For practicals, explain each statement and check. Trace on paper if no server is available, but do not treat that trace as execution evidence.

第三轮做本页综合自测，再从练习总索引随机混合SQL、成本、图与恢复题。以2小时作一次个人限时练习可训练节奏，但它不是课程正式卷的结构。结束后把每个错误重做一遍，并用英文写出“条件—方法—证据—结论”四部分。不要根据本教材篇幅推断考试分值或必考主题。

On the third pass, attempt the integrated self-test below, then mix SQL, cost, graph and recovery questions from the original-question index. A personal two-hour timed session can train pacing but does not reproduce an official paper structure. Redo every error and write conditions, method, evidence and conclusion in English. Do not infer exam weighting or guaranteed topics from textbook length.

公式与方法速查Formula and method sheet

以下公式都带模型假设。符号B为块字节数，R为定长记录字节数，r为记录数，b为数据块数，P为指针大小，V为索引键大小，f为扇出，N/M为记录数。ceil为向上取整，floor为向下取整。不要把字节、块、记录或毫秒混加。

Every formula below has model assumptions. B is bytes per block, R bytes per fixed-length record, r records, b data blocks, P pointer size, V index-key size, f fan-out, and N/M input records. ceil rounds up and floor rounds down. Never add unlike units such as bytes, blocks, records and milliseconds.

对象 / Object公式或步骤 / Formula or procedure条件与解释 / Conditions and interpretation

闭包

Closure

X⁺ starts X; repeatedly add Y if A→Y and A⊆X⁺.

达到不动点；X⁺=全部属性仅证明超键，最小化后才是候选键。

Reach a fixed point; full closure proves a super key, not minimality.

3NF / BCNFEvery nontrivial X→A: X superkey OR A prime / X superkey.

检查蕴含依赖；主属性属于某个候选键。

Consider implied FDs; prime means in some candidate key.

块与浪费

Blocks and waste

bfr=floor(B/R); b=ceil(r/bfr); waste=B−bfr·R

非跨块、定长；最后一页需单独数实际记录。

Unspanned fixed records; count the final partial page separately.

磁盘时间

Disk time

Trot=60000/rpm ms; rd≈Trot/2; btt=B/tr; random=s+rd+btt

一次随机访问含寻道与等待；连续k块通常s+rd+k·btt。

A random access includes seeking/waiting; contiguous k blocks usually cost s+rd+k·btt.

有序二分

Ordered binary search

Approx ceil(log₂ b) probes in the course model.

边界与精确算法可差一次；记录结果不存在的处理。

Boundary cases and exact algorithms may differ by one probe; handle absence.

索引

Index

entry=V+P; f=floor(B/(V+P)); level_next=ceil(level/f)

稀疏主索引起始条目b，稠密二级索引起始条目r；逐层算。

Start with b sparse or r dense entries; count every level.

B+树

B+ tree

Find leaf → insert sorted → split → propagate separator → split root if necessary.

先写清order、最小占用与MIN-RIGHT/MAX-LEFT惯例。

State order, occupancy and separator convention first.

选择率

Selectivity

output≈s·N

0≤s≤1；相关谓词不能随意当独立相乘。

Selectivity lies in [0,1]; correlated predicates need not multiply independently.

课内连接成本

Course join cost

P(N)=N(1+log₂N); J(N,M)=P(N)+P(M); S(N)=N

本课简化比较单位，并非PostgreSQL计划cost或毫秒。

Course comparison units, not PostgreSQL cost or milliseconds.

基数

Cardinality

|R×S|=|R|·|S|; valid non-null FK join to unique key yields |R|.

后式需完整引用和唯一父键；输出行数不是连接工作量。

The FK formula needs valid references and uniqueness; output size is not work.

冲突图

Precedence graph

Different transactions + same item + at least one write → directed order edge.

无环⇔冲突可串行化；拓扑序是等价串行顺序。

A cyclic iff conflict-serialisable; topological orders give equivalent serial orders.

恢复性质

Recovery properties

Strict ⊆ Cascadeless ⊆ Recoverable

分别看未完成写能否被读/写、read-from与提交顺序；不是可串行化的包含链。

Examine unfinished writes, read-from and commit order separately from serialisability.

时间戳

Timestamp ordering

Read: TS≥WTS; write: TS≥RTS and TS≥WTS.

通过后RTS=max(RTS,TS)或WTS=TS；Thomas对过时写另作忽略。

Update RTS/WTS after acceptance; Thomas separately ignores obsolete writes.

恢复

Recovery

Steal⇒Undo; No-force⇒Redo; WAL before data-page flush.

活跃事务的检查点前记录仍可能需要；提交不等于已刷数据页。

Pre-checkpoint records of active transactions may still matter; commit is not a data-page flush.

Practical命令与操作速查Practical command and operation sheet

来源 / Source: Practicals/Prac1-v2.pdf · PDF p. 12-15（原资料留在本地版） Appendix A; repeated in all practicals · 本地逐页图文 / Local pages

先辨认当前环境：Shell用于目录和下载，psql是连接数据库的客户端，SQL语句发送给服务器。SQL用分号结束，psql反斜线命令通常不加分号。下面是示例模板而非让全部命令顺序执行的脚本；实际对象名、路径及权限须与练习实例匹配。

First identify the environment: the shell handles files/downloads, psql is the database client, and SQL goes to the server. SQL ends with semicolons; psql backslash commands normally do not. These are reference examples rather than one script to run sequentially. Match names, paths and permissions to the practice instance.

# Course Linux shell examples, not PowerShell / 课程Linux Shell示例
pwd
ls
cd directory_name
curl -O URL
psql
\c prac1
\dt
\d emp
\dv
\i file.sql
\timing on
\timing off
\pset pager off
\x auto
\?
\q

\i从客户端路径读取文件；“No such file”先查当前目录，不能把数据库表名当文件路径。\d显示结构，\dt列普通表，\dv列普通视图；\timing测客户端所见时间，不等同计划每个节点时间相加。\pset pager off关闭长输出分页，\x auto可改善宽行；q退出分页器，而\q退出psql。

\i reads a client-side file; for a missing-file error inspect the working directory rather than treating table names as paths. \d describes structure, \dt lists ordinary tables and \dv ordinary views. \timing measures client-visible time, not a sum of plan-node times. \pset pager off disables paging and \x auto can improve wide output. q exits a pager; \q exits psql.

-- Independent reference statements / 独立参考语句
SELECT current_database();
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM emp;
SELECT indexname,indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE tablename='emp';
EXPLAIN SELECT * FROM emp WHERE deptno=30;
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE,BUFFERS) SELECT * FROM emp WHERE deptno=30;
ANALYZE emp;
SET enable_seqscan TO off;
SET enable_seqscan TO on;
RESET enable_indexscan;
RESET enable_indexonlyscan;
RESET enable_bitmapscan;

EXPLAIN给估计，ANALYZE选项实际执行；对UPDATE/DELETE也会发生真实修改。计划中cost是规划器单位，actual time是测量时间，rows要结合loops解释。SET off通常抬高某类计划成本；实验结束重置所有改过的设置，并重新核对SHOW值。触发器/函数/视图的完整语法和目的见第3、4、6、9章。

EXPLAIN gives estimates; its ANALYZE option executes, including real changes for UPDATE/DELETE. Plan cost uses planner units; actual time is measured, and rows must be interpreted with loops. SET off commonly penal is es a plan class. Reset all changed settings after an experiment and verify SHOW values. Chapters 3,4,6 and 9 explain complete trigger/function/view/index syntax and purposes.

易混淆概念对照Commonly confused concepts

概念A / Concept A概念B / Concept B区分 / Distinction

候选键

Candidate key

超键

Super key

候选键必须最小；超键允许冗余属性。

A candidate key is minimal; a super key may contain redundant attributes.

无损分解

Lossless decomposition

依赖保持

Dependency preservation

一个保证连接还原事实，另一个保证约束可局部检查，互不替代。

One preserves reconstruction by joining; the other permits local FD enforcement. Neither substitutes for the other.

普通视图

Ordinary view

物化视图

Materialised view

定义与即时计算，对比存储结果与刷新/陈旧性。

Definition and current computation versus stored results with refresh and staleness.

CHECKNOT NULL

CHECK只拒绝FALSE；UNKNOWN不被它拒绝。

CHECK rejects FALSE; UNKNOWN is not rejected by CHECK alone.

延迟约束

Deferred constraint

停用约束

Disabled constraint

延迟仍须在检查点通过；停用可能根本不执行。

Deferral still requires validation later; disabling may omit enforcement.

稀疏

Sparse

稠密

Dense

稀疏省条目但要求有序可定位组；稠密覆盖每键值或记录。

Sparse entries need suitable ordering/group access; dense coverage represents every key value or record.

聚集索引

Clustering index

二级索引

Secondary index

前者联系数据物理顺序；后者提供另一访问路径。

The former follows data ordering; the latter provides an additional access path.

选择

Selection

投影

Projection

选择行；投影列。SQL SELECT关键字不是RA选择的同义词。

Selection filters rows; projection chooses columns. SQL SELECT is not synonymous with RA selection.

集合

Set

多重集

Bag

经典RA去重；SQL通常保留重复，DISTINCT才明确去重。

Classical RA de duplicates; SQL usually keeps duplicates unless DISTINCT is requested.

结果行数

Cardinality

成本

Cost

同样结果可有不同访问工作；估计行数也不等于actual rows。

Equal results can require different work; estimated rows also differ from actual rows.

严格调度

Strict schedule

Strict 2PL

前者是恢复性质；后者协议还保证冲突可串行化。

The former is a recovery property; the protocol additionally guarantees conflict serialisability.

优先图

Precedence graph

等待图

Wait-for graph

先发生冲突给串行顺序约束；等待者指向持锁者检测死锁。

Conflict order constrains serial execution; waiter-to-holder edges detect deadlock.

脏读

Dirty read

不可重复读

Unrepeatable read

读未提交写，和两次读取之间有已提交改变，是不同问题。

Reading an uncommitted write differs from seeing committed change between two reads.

死锁

Deadlock

饥饿

Starvation

互等形成循环，对比某事务一直得不到推进；无死锁未必无饥饿。

Cyclic waiting differs from indefinite postponement; deadlock freedom does not guarantee starvation freedom.

UndoRedo

移除失败事务的效果，对比重施已提交但未持久的数据修改。

Remove failed effects versus reapply committed effects not yet durable in data pages.

检查点

Checkpoint

提交

Commit

检查点协调恢复；事务提交决定业务完成。活跃事务不因检查点自动提交。

A checkpoint coordinates recovery; commit completes a transaction. Active transactions do not commit merely because of a checkpoint.

常见错误与纠正动作Common errors and corrective actions

闭包算全就宣布候选键。 纠正：逐个删除属性测试最小性。

Declaring a candidate key immediately after full closure. Correction: Remove each attribute and retest minimality.

按样例中没重复推断普遍FD。 纠正：区分约束假设、反例与样例支持。

Inferring a universal FD from a small duplicate-free sample. Correction: Separate an assumed constraint, a counterexample and sample support.

NOT IN忽略NULL。 纠正：先证明子查询无NULL，或用NOT EXISTS。

Ignoring NULL in NOT IN. Correction: Prove non-null ness or use NOT EXISTS.

聚合视图直接写回。 纠正：解释底层修改为何不唯一。

Updating an aggregate view automatically. Correction: Explain why the base modification is ambiguous.

跨行规则写入普通CHECK。 纠正：选择触发器并分析并发写路径。

Putting cross-row rules in an ordinary CHECK. Correction: Choose triggers and analyse concurrent write paths.

计算中间结果时提前取整。 纠正：保留精度，只在离散容量边界取整。

Rounding intermediate calculations prematurely. Correction: Retain precision and round at discrete capacity boundaries.

B+树混用分隔规则。 纠正：先写等值走向，每步核对叶链与边界。

Mixing B+ separator conventions. Correction: State equality routing and verify leaf order and separators.

把索引存在当必选。 纠正：检查选择率、回表、统计与全扫费用。

Assuming an existing index must be chosen. Correction: Inspect selectivity, heap access, statistics and scan expense.

关系除法忘记空目标。 纠正：明确候选全集与全称量词的空真。

Forgetting an empty divisor target. Correction: Define the candidate universe and vacuous truth.

图有环但不列证据。 纠正：给每条边写数据项与先后操作。

Claiming a cycle without operation witnesses. Correction: Give the item and ordered operations for each edge.

把abort当commit。 纠正：沿read-from检查读者是否仍可提交。

Treating abort as commit. Correction: Follow read-from dependencies before permitting reader commit.

检查点前日志一律跳过。 纠正：检查当时活跃事务和实际页面持久性。

Discarding all pre-checkpoint log records. Correction: Inspect active transactions and actual page durability.

抄示例耗时作为自己结果。 纠正：记录实际实验；未运行明确标推导。

Reporting example timings as personal observations. Correction: Retain actual evidence and label unrun reasoning.

从理解到英文作答Moving from understanding to English answers

资料主要以英文呈现，但未找到明确规定考试答题语言的文件。下面提供英文表达练习，不据此断言必须英语作答。有效答案必须把具体数据项、操作或数值代入模板；只背术语无法证明结论。

The supplied materials are mainly English, but no explicit rule specifying the examination answer language was found. These are English expression exercises, not a claim that English answers are compulsory. Substitute concrete items, operations and values into each pattern; terminology alone does not justify a conclusion.

规范化：“A不是超键且D不是主属性，因此A→D违反3NF。”先给闭包/键证据，再报范式。

“A is not a super key and D is non prime, so A→D violates 3 NF.” Establish the closure/key evidence before naming the normal form.

优化：“该谓词只返回约1%的行，因此索引可能避免大量堆页读取；是否采用须以计划为准。”

“The predicate returns approximately 1% of rows, so an index may avoid many heap-page reads. The chosen plan must be verified.”

调度：“w1(X)在r2(X)之前产生T1→T2；反向边来自r2(X)在w1(X)之前”不能同时指同一对操作；必须逐条选择真实证据。

Do not claim that the same pair both has w1(X) before r2(X) and the reverse. Choose actual witnesses, for example: “r1(X) precedes w2(X), giving T1→T2, while r2(X) precedes w1(X), giving T2→T1.”

恢复：“T4在崩溃时未提交，其A=20可能已因Steal落盘，所以需用BFIM恢复A=15。”条件和动作相连。

“T4 was uncommitted at the crash, and its A=20 may have reached disk under Steal. Undo therefore restores the BFIM A=15.” Connect the condition directly to the action.

掌握情况检查表Observable mastery checklist

勾选状态只保存在当前浏览器的本地存储；浏览器可能限制file页面存储，打印时可手工勾选。一次正确不算长期掌握：隔一天不看答案重做，并改变输入验证迁移。

Checks are stored only in this browser’s local storage, which may be restricted for file pages. Printed copies can be marked by hand. One correct attempt is not lasting mastery: repeat without answers after a day and change the inputs to test transfer.

综合自测：自编题与参考解析Integrated self-test: author-created questions and solutions

I01

R(A,B,C,D)，F={A→B,B→C,CD→A}。求键及最高范式。

R(A, B, C, D), F={A→B, B→C, CD→A}. Find keys and highest normal form.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

D不出现在右部，必须在键中。AD⁺先A→B→C得到全部；BD⁺由B→C再CD→A得到全部；CD⁺由CD→A→B得到全部，三者均最小。所有属性都在某个候选键内，所以为3NF；A→B的A非超键，非BCNF。

D never occurs on a right side and must be in every key. AD derives B then C; BD derives C then A; CD derives A then B. AD, BD and CD are minimal. Every attribute is prime, so 3 NF holds; A→B has a non-super key determinant, so BCNF fails.

I02

学生打开全部目标题但未完成任何一道，能否符合“全部解出”？目标题为空又如何？

A student has opened every target puzzle but completed none. Can they satisfy “solved all”? What if the target set is empty?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

非空目标时不能，候选记录必须有DateSolved非NULL；仅存在SOLVED行不足。双重NOT EXISTS找缺少完成记录的反例。目标为空时，对指定候选全集每人都无反例，全部符合。

Not for a nonempty target: candidate records must have non-null DateSolved; a SOLVED row alone is insufficient. Double NOT EXISTS seeks a missing completion counterexample. For an empty target, every member of the declared candidate universe has no counterexample and qualifies.

I03

视图筛weight>14并输出weight*15为wt。解释wt=18时更新颜色及指定wt插入。

A view selects weight>14 and exposes weight*15 as wt. Explain UPDATE colour WHERE wt=18 and INSERT assigning wt.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

wt=18要求weight=1.2，但又需>14，所以颜色UPDATE影响0行。wt是派生表达式，不可由普通自动更新机制赋值；不能把插入wt=18自行反解。直接基表插入1.2也不满足视图条件，CHECK OPTION会拒绝。

wt=18 implies weight=1.2, contradicting weight>14, so the colour update affects zero rows. The derived wt expression is not automatically assignable; do not invent inverse insert semantics. A base insertion of 1.2 also fails the view predicate and would be rejected under CHECK OPTION.

I04

块1024字节，定长记录120字节，共1000条且不跨块。求块数及浪费。

A block has 1024 bytes, records 120 bytes, and there are 1000 unspanned records. Find blocks and waste.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

bfr=floor(1024/120)=8；b=ceil(1000/8)=125，恰好全部满载。每页浪费1024−8×120=64字节；总浪费125×64=8000字节。若记录为1001条，则126页，最后一页1条，最后浪费904，不应仍按64算。

bfr=floor (1024/120)=8 and b=ceil (1000/8)=125, with every page full by record count. Waste is 64 bytes per page and 8000 total. With 1001 records there would be 126 pages; the final one holds one record and wastes 904 bytes rather than 64.

I05

10000数据块、50000记录，索引扇出100。比较稀疏与稠密多级块数。

There are 10000 data blocks and 50000 records. Index fan-out is 100. Compare sparse and dense multi level block counts.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

稀疏首层10000条目→100块，第二层1块，共101索引块，检索2索引页再1数据页。稠密首层50000条目→500块，再5块再1块，共506，检索3+1页。假设每条目等长且页无额外开销、根未缓存。

Sparse indexing starts with 10000 entries: 100 blocks then one root, 101 index blocks and two index reads plus data. Dense indexing starts with 50000 entries: 500, five and one blocks, 506 total and three index reads plus data. Assume fixed entries, no extra overhead and an un cached root.

I06

R有2行键3，S有3行键3，等值归并连接输出多少？

R has two rows with key 3 and S has three. What does an equality merge join emit?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

每一R行匹配每一S行，输出2×3=6行。收集重复键组后枚举笛卡尔匹配；只输出min(2,3)或推进双方一次会漏配。输出重复值是否再去重取决于查询语义。

Each R row matches each S row, yielding six rows. Gather equal-key groups and enumerate their Cartesian matches. Emitting min (2,3) or advancing both pointers once misses pairs. Any later deduplication depends on query semantics.

I07

为何工资筛选下推INNER JOIN常安全，跨LEFT JOIN却可能改变结果？

Explain why pushing σsalary>50000 below an INNER JOIN is safe but careless movement across LEFT JOIN can change results.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

只引用该输入的谓词可在INNER JOIN前筛，不改变匹配。外连接会为不匹配行补NULL；原WHERE中引用右表且拒绝NULL的谓词会删这些行，而放入ON/右侧输入后可能保留它们。必须写出谓词原位置和保留方向再判断。

A predicate on one input can filter before an inner join without changing matches. An outer join introduces null-extended rows. A null-rejecting right-side WHERE predicate removes them, whereas placing it in ON or the right input can retain them. State the original predicate location and preserved side before transforming.

I08

判断r1(X),r2(X),w1(X),c1,w2(X),c2。

Classify r1(X), r2(X), w1(X), c1, w2(X), c2.

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

没有读未提交写，w2也在c1后，因此严格、无级联且可恢复。但r1先w2给1→2，r2先w1给2→1，有环，不冲突可串行化。两个事务读同旧值可造成丢失更新；严格性不是串行化。

There are no dirty reads and w2 follows c1, so the schedule is strict, cascadeless and recoverable. Yet r1 before w2 gives 1→2 and r2 before w1 gives 2→1: a cycle, hence not conflict-serialisable. Both can read the old value and lose an update; strictness is not serialisability.

I09

TS(T)=10、RTS(X)=12、WTS(X)=15。Thomas能否忽略该写继续？

TS(T)=10, RTS(X)=12, WTS(X)=15. Can Thomas ignore T’s write?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

不能。先检查10<RTS12，必须回滚，因为较新事务已经读过；只有通过RTS检查且TS<WTS时，才按Thomas忽略过时写。规则顺序影响正确性。

No. The first test, 10<RTS12, requires abortion because a newer transaction has already read. Thomas ignores an obsolete write only after the RTS test passes and TS<WTS. Rule order matters.

I10

Steal/No-force完整刷新检查点包含未提交A:5→9；事务随后失败未提交。可跳过旧日志吗？

A Steal/No-force full-flush checkpoint contains an uncommitted A:5→9. That transaction later aborts or crashes uncommitted. Can its old log be skipped?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

不能。9可能已被检查点刷入数据页，需要检查点前BFIM5做Undo。另一个事务若在检查点前写、检查点后才提交，在延迟更新方案下仍可能需要其旧AFIM做Redo。检查点不等于事务完成边界。

No. The checkpoint may have flushed 9, requiring the pre-checkpoint BFIM5 for Undo. A transaction that wrote before the checkpoint but committed after it may also need its old AFIM for Redo under deferred update. A checkpoint is not a transaction completion boundary.

I11

已有完整email唯一索引，域索引帮助稀有查找却不加速全域汇总，矛盾吗？

A unique email index exists. A domain index speeds rare lookup but not all-domain grouping. Is that contradictory?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

不矛盾。完整地址排序不按域排序；域表达式索引按标准化域组织。稀有查找避免访问大多数行，而全量分组仍需覆盖全部成员；规划器比较I/O、聚合、缓存和统计后可能全扫。实际结论需计划和运行数据。

No. Whole-address order is not domain order; an expression index orders normal is ed domains. Rare lookup can avoid most rows, whereas full grouping still covers every member. The planner may prefer scanning after considering I/O, aggregation, caching and statistics. Confirm with plans and observed data.

I12

S1读可用车，S2预订并提交，S1在READ COMMITTED下尝试Collect。需检查什么？

Session 1 reads an available car; Session 2 reserves and commits; Session 1 attempts Collect under READ COMMITTED. What must be checked?

先独立尝试：写出条件、方法和理由，再展开。 / Try first: write the conditions, method and reasons before revealing the solution.

答案与推理 / Answer and reasoning

自编练习；参考解析 / Author-created exercise; reference solution

读没有预留车。S1后来语句用新快照，触发器按最新事件规则可能看到S2的Reserve并拒绝；须检查成员身份、event_time排序和触发器实现。拒绝异常不同于锁等待。若要预防竞态，需要针对同一资源的一致锁定/原子状态转换协议，并在锁后重查。

The read reserved nothing. S1’s later statement takes a new snapshot and its latest-event trigger may see S2’s Reserve and reject Collect. Check member identity, event_time ordering and trigger code. Exception rejection differs from lock waiting. Prevention needs a consistent resource-locking or atomic state-transition protocol with rechecking.

INFS7903 · GLOSSARY

统一双语术语表Unified bilingual glossary

按英文术语检索，也可使用浏览器查找中文。解释是速查定义；条件、推导与例子仍以所链接章节为准。

Search by English term or use browser search for Chinese. These are lookup definitions; use the linked chapters for conditions, derivations and examples.

关系Relation

具有命名属性的一组元组；经典模型按集合解释。

A set of tuples over named attributes in the classical model.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Table / 表

元组Tuple

关系中的一条记录，由属性值按模式组成。

A record whose attribute values follow a relation schema.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Attribute / 属性

值域Domain

属性允许的值及其语义范围。

The permitted values and semantic range of an attribute.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Data type / 数据类型

函数依赖Functional dependency

同一X值的合法元组必须具有同一Y值，记X→Y。

Legal tuples agreeing on X must agree on Y, written X→Y.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Causation / 因果

决定项Determinant

函数依赖箭头左边的属性集合。

The attribute set on the left of an FD.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Candidate key / 候选键

属性闭包Attribute closure

由给定依赖从X能推出的全部属性X⁺。

All attributes derivable from X using the given FDs.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: FD closure / 依赖闭包

超键Superkey

能唯一标识元组的属性集合，可能有冗余。

An attribute set identifying tuples, possibly with redundant attributes.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Candidate key / 候选键

候选键Candidate key

没有真子集仍为超键的最小超键。

A super key with no proper subset that is also a super key.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Primary key / 主键

主属性Prime attribute

属于至少一个候选键的属性。

An attribute belonging to at least one candidate key.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Primary-key attribute / 主键属性

部分依赖Partial dependency

非主属性依赖于某候选键的真子集。

A non prime attribute depends on a proper subset of a candidate key.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Transitive dependency / 传递依赖

第三范式Third normal form

每个非平凡X→A满足X为超键或A为主属性。

Every nontrivial X→A has X a super key or A prime.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: BCNF

BC范式Boyce–Codd normal form

每个非平凡依赖的决定项均为超键。

Every nontrivial FD has a super key determinant.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: 3NF

无损分解Lossless decomposition

分解后自然连接恰好还原合法原关系。

Joining the projections reconstructs exactly the legal original relation.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Dependency preservation / 依赖保持

依赖保持Dependency preservation

局部依赖的合并能够推出原有依赖。

The combined local dependencies imply the original dependencies.

第1章 / Chapter 1 · 易混 / Compare: Losslessness / 无损性

相关子查询Correlated subquery

子查询引用外层当前行；逻辑上按候选关联求值。

A sub query refers to the current outer row in its logical evaluation.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: Uncorrelated subquery / 非相关子查询

存在检验EXISTS

子查询有任意一行就为真，不关心SELECT列的具体值。

True when the sub query returns any row, regardless of its selected value.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: IN

空标记NULL

表示缺失或未知；普通比较可能产生UNKNOWN。

A missing/unknown marker; ordinary comparisons may yield UNKNOWN.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: Empty string / 空串

三值逻辑Three-valued logic

SQL条件可取TRUE、FALSE、UNKNOWN。

SQL conditions can evaluate to TRUE, FALSE or UNKNOWN.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: Boolean logic / 二值逻辑

分组GROUP BY

按相同分组键把输入行组成聚合组。

Forms aggregation groups with equal grouping keys.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: ORDER BY / 排序

分组过滤HAVING

在分组和聚合后筛选组。

Filters groups after grouping and aggregation.

第2章 / Chapter 2 · 易混 / Compare: WHERE

视图View

以查询定义的逻辑关系；普通视图通常不存结果。

A query-defined logical relation; ordinary views normally do not store results.

第3章 / Chapter 3 · 易混 / Compare: Materialised view / 物化视图

物化视图Materialised view

保存查询结果的对象，需要刷新以反映变化。

An object storing query results and requiring refresh to reflect changes.

第3章 / Chapter 3 · 易混 / Compare: Ordinary view / 普通视图

视图检查选项WITH CHECK OPTION

限制经视图写入后的行仍满足视图条件。

Requires rows written through a view to satisfy its predicate.

第3章 / Chapter 3 · 易混 / Compare: CHECK constraint

授权Authorisation

规定某身份可以执行哪些操作。

Determines which operations an identified principal may perform.

第3章 / Chapter 3 · 易混 / Compare: Authentication / 身份认证

完整性约束Integrity constraint

限制允许的数据库状态。

A restriction on legal database states.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Concurrency control / 并发控制

外键Foreign key

要求非空引用值在被引用唯一键中存在。

Requires non-null referencing values to exist in the referenced unique key.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Primary key / 主键

参照动作Referential action

父键修改或删除时对子行采取的规则。

The rule applied to referencing rows when a parent key changes or is deleted.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: CASCADE / NO ACTION

可延迟约束Deferrable constraint

可按设置延后到事务结束等时机检查。

A constraint whose checking may be postponed, for example to transaction end.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Disabled constraint / 停用约束

触发器Trigger

在指定数据库事件发生时执行函数的机制。

A mechanism invoking a function on specified database events.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Procedure / 存储过程

旧行 / 新行OLD / NEW

行级触发器中修改前后记录；事件决定哪一个存在。

Before/after records in row triggers; availability depends on the event.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Row / statement trigger

序列Sequence

独立生成数值的对象；回滚不保证回收已取号码。

An independent numeric generator whose consumed values need not return on rollback.

第4章 / Chapter 4 · 易混 / Compare: Counter table / 计数表

块 / 页Block / page

DBMS和存储传输、缓冲的重要单位。

A principal unit of storage transfer and buffering.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Record / 记录

块因子Blocking factor

给定布局下一块能容纳的记录数量。

The number of records fitting a block under the specified layout.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Fan-out / 扇出

跨块记录Spanned record

一条记录可以分布在多个块。

A record may extend across more than one block.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Variable-length record / 变长记录

寻道时间Seek time

磁头移动到目标磁道所需时间。

Time to move a disk head to the target track.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Rotational delay / 旋转等待

旋转等待Rotational delay

目标扇区转到磁头下面前的等待。

Waiting for the target sector to rotate under the head.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Transfer time / 传输时间

堆文件Heap file

记录无搜索键全局顺序的文件组织。

A file organisation without global search-key order.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Heap memory / 内存堆

有序文件Ordered file

按指定排序键组织记录。

A file organised by a designated ordering key.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Secondary index / 二级索引

散列Hashing

由散列函数把键映射到桶，主要服务等值定位。

Maps keys to buckets using a hash function, chiefly for equality access.

第5章 / Chapter 5 · 易混 / Compare: Range search / 范围搜索

搜索键Search key

索引用于定位的属性，可不唯一。

The indexed attribute set used for access, not necessarily unique.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Candidate key / 候选键

稠密索引Dense index

为每搜索键值或每记录提供条目，需说明实现。

Provides entries for every search-key value or record, depending on design.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Sparse index / 稀疏索引

稀疏索引Sparse index

只为部分键值提供定位条目，依赖合适数据顺序。

Indexes selected key values and depends on suitable data ordering.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Dense index / 稠密索引

聚集索引Clustering index

An index locating value groups on a non unique ordering field in the course model.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Secondary index / 二级索引

扇出Fan-out

内部节点能容纳的孩子指针数量。

The number of child pointers an internal node can contain.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Blocking factor / 块因子

B+树B+ tree

平衡多路搜索树，数据条目集中于有序叶层。

A balanced multi way search tree with data entries in ordered leaves.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: B-tree / B树

表达式索引Expression index

以计算表达式结果作为索引键。

An index whose keys are computed expression results.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Ordinary column index / 列索引

位图扫描Bitmap scan

先生成匹配位置位图再访问相应堆页的执行方式。

An execution method building a location bitmap before visiting heap pages.

第6章 / Chapter 6 · 易混 / Compare: Persistent bitmap index / 持久位图索引

选择Selection

按条件保留关系中的行，记σ。

Filters relation rows by a predicate, denoted σ.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Projection / 投影

投影Projection

保留指定属性；经典RA消除重复，记π。

Keeps designated attributes, de duplicating in classical RA, denoted π.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: SQL SELECT

并相容Union compatibility

集合运算输入列数相同且对应值域相容。

Set-operation inputs have the same arity and compatible corresponding domains.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Natural join / 自然连接

笛卡尔积Cartesian product

输出每一左元组与每一右元组的组合。

Produces every left/right tuple combination.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Join / 连接

θ连接Theta join

对乘积施加一般比较条件的连接。

A join applying a general comparison predicate to tuple pairs.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Equijoin / 等值连接

自然连接Natural join

对全部同名属性等值匹配并合并重复列。

Matches all same-named attributes for equality and merges those columns.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Explicit equijoin / 显式等值连接

外连接Outer join

保留指定方向的不匹配行并用NULL补另一侧。

Retains unmatched rows on designated sides with null extension.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Inner join / 内连接

关系除法Division

找到与目标集合所有元素都有配对的候选。

Finds candidates paired with every element of a target relation.

第7章 / Chapter 7 · 易混 / Compare: Existential query / 存在查询

嵌套循环连接Nested-loop join

对外层每个输入重复查找或扫描内层。

Repeats inner access for each outer input row or block.

第8章 / Chapter 8 · 易混 / Compare: Merge join / 归并连接

归并连接Merge join

利用有序连接键推进游标，重复键组需完整配对。

Advances ordered join keys and fully combines equal-key groups.

第8章 / Chapter 8 · 易混 / Compare: Merge sort / 归并排序

散列连接Hash join

先按键构建散列表，再用另一输入探测。

Builds a hash table by key and probes it with the other input.

第8章 / Chapter 8 · 易混 / Compare: Hash index / 散列索引

基数Cardinality

关系或中间结果的行数。

The row count of a relation or intermediate result.

第8章 / Chapter 8 · 易混 / Compare: Cost / 成本

选择率Selectivity

满足某谓词的输入比例。

The fraction of input rows satisfying a predicate.

第8章 / Chapter 8 · 易混 / Compare: Distinct values / 不同值数量

谓词下推Predicate pushdown

把安全的筛选移到更接近数据源以缩小中间结果。

Moves safe filters nearer data sources to reduce intermediate results.

第9章 / Chapter 9 · 易混 / Compare: Projection pushdown / 投影下推

查询计划Query plan

实现逻辑查询的物理操作及组合。

Physical operations and their composition implementing a logical query.

第9章 / Chapter 9 · 易混 / Compare: Query result / 查询结果

实际执行并解释EXPLAIN ANALYZE

执行语句并报告测量统计；修改语句也会执行。

Executes the statement and reports measurements, including modifying statements.

第9章 / Chapter 9 · 易混 / Compare: EXPLAIN

仅索引扫描Index-only scan

尝试仅靠索引满足列需求，仍可能因可见性回表。

Attempts to satisfy columns from the index but may still visit heap pages for visibility.

第9章 / Chapter 9 · 易混 / Compare: Index scan / 索引扫描

记忆化节点Memoize

缓存参数化内层查找结果以复用重复参数。

Caches parameter is ed inner results for repeated parameter values.

第9章 / Chapter 9 · 易混 / Compare: Materialize / 物化执行节点

事务Transaction

一项逻辑业务工作的读写单元。

A read/write unit implementing one logical business task.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Statement / 语句

原子性Atomicity

事务效果全部完成或全部撤销。

Transaction effects complete together or are removed together.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Durability / 持久性

隔离性Isolation

并发执行避免所选语义禁止的干扰。

Concurrency avoids interference prohibited by the chosen semantics.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Consistency / 一致性

丢失更新Lost update

旧副本写回覆盖另一个事务的修改。

A stale write overwrites another transaction’s change.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Dirty write / 脏写

脏读Dirty read

读取另一个尚未提交事务写入的值。

Reads a value written by another uncommitted transaction.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Unrepeatable read / 不可重复读

冲突等价Conflict equivalence

每一对冲突操作的相对顺序相同。

Every pair of conflicting operations has the same relative order.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Equal graph edges / 边集合相同

优先图Precedence graph

由冲突先后关系形成的事务有向图。

A directed transaction graph formed from conflict order.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Wait-for graph / 等待图

可恢复调度Recoverable schedule

读者提交不得先于其所依赖写者提交。

A reader commits only after the writer it depends on commits.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Cascadeless schedule / 无级联调度

严格调度Strict schedule

其他事务不读写未完成事务写过的数据项。

Others neither read nor write an item written by an unfinished transaction.

第10章 / Chapter 10 · 易混 / Compare: Strict 2PL

共享锁Shared lock

允许相容的多个读者持有的访问锁。

An access lock compatible with other shared readers.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Exclusive lock / 排他锁

两阶段锁Two-phase locking

先只获取/升级，首次释放后只释放/降级。

Acquire/upgrade in a growing phase, then only release/downgrade after shrinking begins.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Two-phase commit / 两阶段提交

严谨两阶段锁Rigorous 2PL

全部共享和排他锁持有到事务结束。

Holds all shared and exclusive locks until transaction end.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Strict 2PL

保守两阶段锁Conservative 2PL

在执行前原子取得所需全部锁。

Atomic ally acquires all required locks before execution.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Basic 2PL

死锁Deadlock

事务循环等待对方持有的资源。

Transactions cyclically wait for resources held by one another.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Starvation / 饥饿

等待或死亡Wait-die

老事务可等年轻者；年轻者请求老者资源则回滚。

Older requesters may wait for younger holders; younger requesters abort against older holders.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Wound-wait / 受伤或等待

受伤或等待Wound-wait

老事务终止年轻持有者；年轻请求者等待。

Older requesters abort younger holders; younger requesters wait.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Wait-die / 等待或死亡

时间戳排序Timestamp ordering

按预定时间戳顺序检查读写是否仍可接受。

Tests reads/writes against a predetermined timestamp order.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Wall-clock event time / 业务时间

Thomas写规则Thomas write rule

通过读时间检查后忽略已被更新写覆盖的过时写。

After the read-timestamp test passes, ignores obsolete writes superseded by newer writes.

第11章 / Chapter 11 · 易混 / Compare: Basic TO / 基本时间戳排序

预写日志Write-ahead logging

相关日志在数据页持久化前先持久化。

Relevant log records become durable before corresponding data pages.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: Commit / 提交

前映像Before image

修改前值，为Undo提供恢复依据。

The old value used as evidence for Undo.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: After image / 后映像

后映像After image

修改后值，为Redo提供重施依据。

The new value used for Redo.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: Before image / 前映像

允许窃取Steal

未提交修改所在页也可写回磁盘。

Pages with uncommitted changes may be written to disk.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: No-steal

提交强制写回Force

提交前要求该事务修改的数据页持久化。

Requires modified data pages to be durable at commit.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: No-force

检查点Checkpoint

记录恢复边界与系统状态；具体刷新行为依算法。

Records recovery state/boundaries; flushing behaviour depends on the algorithm.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: Commit / 提交

撤销Undo

反向移除未完成事务的效果。

Reverses the effects of incomplete transactions.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: Redo / 重做

重做Redo

重新施加需要持久保留的已提交效果。

Reapplies committed effects that must persist.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: Undo / 撤销

影子分页Shadow paging

用稳定旧页表和复制修改页，通过持久切换根提交。

Uses a stable old page table and copied modified pages, committing via a durable root switch.

第12章 / Chapter 12 · 易混 / Compare: WAL / 预写日志

递归公共表表达式Recursive CTE

种子与递归项迭代扩展可达集合，需终止与环处理。

An anchor and recursive term iterative ly extend reach ability, with termination/cycle handling.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: One-level parent join / 单层父连接

窗口函数Window function

按分区及顺序计算行相关值而不把组压成一行。

Computes row-related values over partitions/order without collapsing each group to one row.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: GROUP BY

行编号ROW_NUMBER

在窗口内为每行赋唯一序号；并列需明确排序。

Assigns a unique position per partition; ties need explicit ordering.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: RANK / 排名

反连接Anti-join

保留没有匹配右行的左行。

Retains left rows with no matching right row.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: Outer join / 外连接

多版本并发控制MVCC

利用版本和快照决定查询可见的已提交状态。

Uses versions and snapshots to determine visible committed state.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: 2PL / 两阶段锁

读已提交READ COMMITTED

PostgreSQL中每条语句获得新快照，事务内可见不同提交状态。

In PostgreSQL each statement gets a new snapshot, so a transaction may see changing committed states.

第13章 / Chapter 13 · 易混 / Compare: Snapshot lock / 快照并非锁
